# 工具与器械

**tool**
英/美 /tuːl/
n. 工具；手段
[义] An object held in the hand and used to carry out a particular job, such as hammering or cutting; something used to achieve a purpose. 手持、用于完成特定工作的物件（如敲击、切割）；亦可喻指达成目的的手段（hand tool 手工具, power tool 电动工具, gardening tools 园艺工具）。可数。
[例] ① He kept his tools in a box in the garage. 他把工具放在车库的一个箱子里。 ② Language is a powerful tool for learning about other cultures. 语言是了解其他文化的有力工具。
[搭] hand tool 手工具, power tool 电动工具, gardening tools 园艺工具, tool kit 工具箱, tool box 工具箱
[联] equipment 设备, device 装置, instrument 器械, implement 工具, machinery 机械, hammer 锤子, screwdriver 螺丝刀, repair 修理, workshop 作坊
[记] 可数：a tool。tool 作比喻义时不可数（a useful tool）。与 cool 不同音

**equipment**
英/美 /ɪˈkwɪpmənt/
n. 设备；装备
[义] The set of tools, machines, or other items needed for a particular activity or purpose. 某项活动或用途所需的一整套工具、机器或其他物品（office equipment 办公设备, sports equipment 运动装备, safety equipment 安全装备）。不可数。
[例] ① The lab has state-of-the-art research equipment. 实验室配有最先进的研究设备。 ② Always wear the right safety equipment on a building site. 在建筑工地务必佩戴合适的安全装备。
[源] 源自法语 *équiper*（装备），经拉丁语 *equipare*（使就绪），*equi-*（平等、就绪）+ *parare*（准备）。
[族] equip 装备, equipped 配备……的
[搭] office equipment 办公设备, sports equipment 运动装备, safety equipment 安全装备, medical equipment 医疗设备, heavy equipment 重型设备
[联] tool 工具, device 装置, machinery 机械, gear 装备, apparatus 成套装置, supplies 补给品, kit 成套工具, install 安装, maintain 维护
[记] 不可数；不说 × an equipment，可说 a piece of equipment。equip 动词：equip sb. with sth.

**device**
英/美 /dɪˈvaɪs/
n. 装置；设备；手段
[义] An object or machine invented for a particular purpose; a plan or method. 为特定用途而发明的物件或机器；计划、手段（electronic device 电子设备, mobile device 移动设备, safety device 安全装置）。可数。
[例] ① She checked that every device was charged before the trip. 出发前她检查了每件设备是否已充电。 ② The story uses humour as a narrative device. 故事以幽默作为叙事手法。
[源] 源自 Old French *devis*（分割、设计），与 divide（分开）同族。
[族] devise 设计, divide 分开
[搭] electronic device 电子设备, mobile device 移动设备, safety device 安全装置, handheld device 手持设备, connect a device 连接设备
[联] equipment 设备, gadget 小装置, machine 机器, instrument 器械, tool 工具, smartphone 智能手机, sensor 传感器, install 安装
[记] 可数：a device。device vs. devise：device 名词「装置」，devise 动词「设计」。leave sb. to their own devices 听任某人自行其是

**instrument**
英/美 /ˈɪnstrəmənt/
n. 器械；仪器；乐器
[义] A tool or implement, especially for delicate or scientific work; an object for producing musical sounds. 工具或器具，尤指精密或科学用途；亦指乐器（surgical instruments 手术器械, measuring instruments 测量仪器, musical instrument 乐器）。可数。
[例] ① The surgeon laid out the instruments on a sterile tray. 外科医生把器械摆在无菌托盘上。 ② She plays three musical instruments. 她会三种乐器。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *instrumentum*（工具、装备），*in-*（在内）+ *struere*（建造、安排）→ 用来完成工作的装置。
[族] instruct 指导, instruction 说明, instructor 教员, infrastructure 基础设施
[搭] surgical instruments 手术器械, measuring instruments 测量仪器, musical instrument 乐器, precision instrument 精密仪器, scientific instrument 科学仪器
[联] tool 工具, device 装置, equipment 设备, apparatus 成套装置, implement 工具, surgery 手术, measure 测量, orchestra 管弦乐
[记] 可数：an instrument。instrumental 形容词：instrumental in 对……起重要作用；instrumental music 器乐

**appliance**
英/美 /əˈplaɪəns/
n. 家用电器；器具
[义] A machine or device designed to perform a specific task in the home, especially in the kitchen or for cleaning. 为家庭特定用途设计的机器，尤指厨房或清洁用途（household appliances 家用电器, kitchen appliance 厨房电器, electrical appliance 电器）。可数。
[例] ① The kitchen is fitted with modern appliances. 厨房配备了现代电器。 ② Unplug small appliances when you leave on holiday. 外出度假时拔掉小家电插头。
[源] 源自 apply（应用）+ -ance → 被应用于特定用途的装置。
[族] apply 应用, application 应用, applicable 适用的
[搭] household appliances 家用电器, kitchen appliance 厨房电器, electrical appliance 电器, major appliances 大家电, small appliance 小家电
[联] device 装置, equipment 设备, machine 机器, refrigerator 冰箱, oven 烤箱, washer 洗衣机, gadget 小装置, plug 插头
[记] 可数：an appliance。appliance 多指插电的家用机器；tool 多指手工具

**implement**
英/美 /ˈɪmplɪmənt/
n. 工具；器具
v. 实施；执行
[义] A tool or utensil used for a particular purpose; to put a plan or system into effect. 为特定用途而用的工具或器具；实施、执行（计划）（farm implements 农具, implement a policy 实施政策）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Ancient farmers used simple stone implements. 古代农民使用简单的石器农具。 ② The company will implement the new rules next month. 公司将于下月实施新规定。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *implere*（填满、完成），*im-*（进入）+ *plere*（满）→ 使计划「落地」。
[族] implementation 实施, complete 完成
[搭] farm implements 农具, kitchen implements 厨房用具, implement a plan 实施计划, implement changes 落实变革, implement a policy 实施政策
[联] tool 工具, instrument 器械, utensil 器皿, equipment 设备, execute 执行, carry out 执行, policy 政策
[记] 名词可数；动词 implement 较正式。implementation 实施（不可数/可数）

**gadget**
英/美 /ˈɡædʒɪt/
n. 小装置；小玩意
[义] A small mechanical or electronic device or tool, especially one that is novel or cleverly designed. 小型机械或电子装置，尤指新颖或设计巧妙者（kitchen gadget 厨房小工具, latest gadget 最新小装置, tech gadget 科技小玩意）。可数。
[例] ① He loves buying the latest kitchen gadgets. 他喜欢买最新厨房小玩意。 ② The fair was full of clever gadgets for travellers. 展会上满是给旅行者用的巧妙小装置。
[搭] kitchen gadget 厨房小工具, latest gadget 最新小装置, tech gadget 科技小玩意, handy gadget 便利小装置
[联] device 装置, tool 工具, appliance 电器, machine 机器, gizmo 小装置（口语）, invention 发明, electronic 电子的
[记] 可数：a gadget。口语色彩；略偏「新奇、非必需但有趣」

**machine**
英/美 /məˈʃiːn/
n. 机器；机械
[义] An apparatus using mechanical power and having several parts, each with a definite function. 利用机械动力、由多个部件组成、各司其职的装置（washing machine 洗衣机, machine tool 机床, vending machine 自动售货机）。可数。
[例] ① The washing machine broke down mid-cycle. 洗衣机在洗涤中途坏了。 ② Factories replaced hand tools with machines in the nineteenth century. 十九世纪工厂以机器取代手工具。
[源] 源自希腊语 *mēkhanē*（装置、计谋），与 mechanic（技工）同族。
[族] machinery 机械, mechanical 机械的, mechanic 技工, mechanism 机制
[搭] washing machine 洗衣机, machine tool 机床, vending machine 自动售货机, sewing machine 缝纫机, operate a machine 操作机器
[联] equipment 设备, device 装置, engine 发动机, motor 马达, factory 工厂, automate 自动化, repair 修理
[记] 可数：a machine。machine 强调机械运作；device 可更小、更电子

**machinery**
英/美 /məˈʃiːnəri/
n. 机器；机械；机构
[义] Machines collectively, or the working parts of a machine; the organization or structure of a system. 机器的统称，或机器的工作部件；机构的运作结构（heavy machinery 重型机械, farm machinery 农机, the machinery of government 政府运作机制）。不可数。
[例] ① The factory invested in new machinery last year. 工厂去年投资了新机器。 ② Bureaucratic machinery can slow down simple decisions. 官僚机构运作可能拖慢简单决策。
[搭] heavy machinery 重型机械, farm machinery 农机, industrial machinery 工业机械, the machinery of government 政府运作机制
[联] machine 机器, equipment 设备, engine 发动机, factory 工厂, manufacture 制造, maintenance 维护, operator 操作员
[记] 不可数；不说 × a machinery，可说 a piece of machinery

**utensil**
英/美 /juːˈtensl/
n. 器皿；用具
[义] A tool or container used in a kitchen or household, especially for cooking or eating. 厨房或家庭中用于烹饪或进食的工具或容器（cooking utensils 炊具, kitchen utensils 厨房用具）。可数。
[例] ① Pack lightweight cooking utensils for the camping trip. 露营带上轻便炊具。 ② The drawer was full of utensils she rarely used. 抽屉里满是她很少用到的厨具。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *utensilis*（有用的），*uti*（使用）→ 供日常使用的器具。
[族] utility 实用, utilize 利用
[搭] cooking utensils 炊具, kitchen utensils 厨房用具, eating utensils 餐具, pack utensils 打包用具
[联] tool 工具, implement 器具, appliance 电器, pot 锅, pan 平底锅, spoon 勺, knife 刀, cookware 炊具
[记] 可数：a utensil。多指厨房/餐饮用具；tool 范围更广

**gear**
英/美 /ɡɪə(r)/
n. 装备；齿轮；档
[义] Equipment needed for a particular activity; a toothed wheel that transmits motion in a machine; a particular speed setting in a vehicle. 某项活动所需的装备；传递运动的齿轮；车辆档位（camping gear 露营装备, safety gear 防护装备, change gear 换挡）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They loaded their climbing gear into the van. 他们把攀岩装备装进面包车。 ② He shifted into a lower gear on the steep hill. 上陡坡时他换到低档。
[搭] camping gear 露营装备, safety gear 防护装备, change gear 换挡, fishing gear 渔具, top gear 最高档/《最高档》节目名
[联] equipment 设备, tool 工具, machinery 机械, helmet 头盔, rope 绳索, shift 换挡, mechanism 机制
[记] 装备义不可数/集合；齿轮、档位可数。get into gear 进入状态；be in gear 已挂档

**hammer**
英/美 /ˈhæmə(r)/
n. 锤子
v. 锤击；猛击
[义] A tool with a heavy metal head and a handle, used for hitting nails or breaking things; to hit repeatedly with force. 有金属锤头与柄的工具，用于钉钉子或砸物；猛击、连续敲打（hammer and nails 锤子和钉子, hammer sth. flat 把某物锤平）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He drove the nail in with three blows of the hammer. 他用锤子三下把钉子钉进去。 ② Rain hammered on the roof all night. 雨整夜敲打着屋顶。
[形] hammered / hammered / hammering
[搭] hammer and nails 锤子和钉子, hammer sth. flat 把某物锤平, hammer sth. into shape 锤打成形, hammer away at 反复敲打/钻研, under the hammer 拍卖中
[联] nail 钉子, screw 螺丝, screwdriver 螺丝刀, tool 工具, strike 敲击, toolbox 工具箱, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a hammer。习语 hammer home 把观点讲透；hammer and tongs 激烈争吵

**nail**
英/美 /neɪl/
n. 钉子；指甲
v. 钉牢； nailed it（口语）搞定
[义] A thin pointed piece of metal used to fasten things together by being hammered into wood; the hard covering at the end of a finger or toe. 敲入木头以固定物体的金属尖钉；手指或脚趾的指甲（hit the nail on the head 一针见血, nail sth. to the wall 把某物钉在墙上）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He bent the first nail and had to pull it out. 第一根钉子弯了，他只好拔出来。 ② She nailed the boards together to make a shelf. 她把木板钉在一起做成架子。
[搭] hit the nail on the head 一针见血, nail sth. to the wall 把某物钉在墙上, nail polish 指甲油, nail file 指甲锉, nail down 确定/钉牢
[联] hammer 锤子, screw 螺丝, wood 木头, fasten 固定, finger 手指, toolbox 工具箱, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a nail。nail 作动词口语 nailed it 表「完美搞定」。finger nail / toenail 指甲/趾甲

**screw**
英/美 /skruː/
n. 螺丝；螺旋
v. 拧紧； screw up 搞砸
[义] A thin pointed piece of metal with a raised spiral line, turned to fasten things; to fasten with a screw; to twist or turn. 带螺旋纹、需旋转固定的金属紧固件；用螺丝固定；拧转（screw in a screw 拧入螺丝, screw tight 拧紧, screw up 搞砸〔口语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① One screw was missing from the chair leg. 椅子腿上少了一颗螺丝。 ② Screw the lid on tightly so nothing spills. 把盖子拧紧，别洒出来。
[搭] screw in a screw 拧入螺丝, screw tight 拧紧, screw up 搞砸, screw driver 螺丝刀（亦作 screwdriver）, loose screw 松动的螺丝/怪人（喻）
[联] screwdriver 螺丝刀, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, nail 钉子, tighten 拧紧, loosen 松开, thread 螺纹
[记] 可数：a screw。screw up 口语「搞砸」；have a screw loose 脑子有点不正常（俚）

**screwdriver**
英/美 /ˈskruːdraɪvə(r)/
n. 螺丝刀
[义] A tool with a handle and a shaped tip that fits into the head of a screw. 有柄、刀头与螺丝槽口匹配的拧螺丝工具（flat-head screwdriver 一字螺丝刀, Phillips screwdriver 十字螺丝刀, electric screwdriver 电动螺丝刀）。可数。
[例] ① Pass me the Phillips screwdriver—these are cross-head screws. 递我十字螺丝刀，这些是十字头螺丝。 ② He repaired the cabinet with little more than a screwdriver. 他几乎只用一把螺丝刀就修好了柜子。
[搭] flat-head screwdriver 一字螺丝刀, Phillips screwdriver 十字螺丝刀, electric screwdriver 电动螺丝刀, use a screwdriver 用螺丝刀
[联] screw 螺丝, tool 工具, wrench 扳手, bolt 螺栓, repair 修理, toolbox 工具箱, tighten 拧紧
[记] 可数：a screwdriver。Phillips 源自发明人 Henry Phillips；英式亦说 cross-head screwdriver

**wrench**
英/美 /rentʃ/
n. 扳手；扭伤
v. 猛拧；扭伤
[义] A tool used for gripping and turning nuts, bolts, or pipes; to twist suddenly and violently, often causing injury. 夹持并拧转螺母、螺栓或管道的工具；猛扭、扭伤（adjustable wrench 活动扳手, pipe wrench 管钳, throw a wrench in the works 打乱计划〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He tightened the bolt with an adjustable wrench. 他用活动扳手把螺栓拧紧。 ② She wrenched her shoulder lifting the heavy box. 抬重箱子时她扭伤了肩膀。
[搭] adjustable wrench 活动扳手, pipe wrench 管钳, throw a wrench in the works 打乱计划, torque wrench 扭力扳手
[联] spanner 扳手（英式）, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, pliers 钳子, tighten 拧紧, mechanic 技工, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 可数：a wrench。英式 spanner 同义；wrench 美式更常见。动词 wrench 表扭伤

**spanner**
英 /ˈspænə(r)/ 美 /ˈspænər/
n. 扳手
[义] A tool with shaped jaws for gripping and turning nuts and bolts. 钳口成型的扳手，用于夹持并拧转螺母螺栓（adjustable spanner 活动扳手, ring spanner 梅花扳手）。可数。chiefly British.
[例] ① He rummaged in the toolbox for the right spanner. 他在工具箱里翻找合适的扳手。 ② You'll need a 10 mm spanner for that nut. 拧那颗螺母需要 10 毫米扳手。
[搭] adjustable spanner 活动扳手, ring spanner 梅花扳手, throw a spanner in the works 打乱计划（英式）
[联] wrench 扳手（美式）, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, pliers 钳子, mechanic 技工, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 可数：a spanner。英式；美式多用 wrench。习语 throw a spanner/wrench in the works

**pliers**
英/美 /ˈplaɪəz/
n. 钳子；老虎钳
[义] A tool with two crossed pivoted arms used for gripping, bending, or cutting wire or small objects. 两臂交叉枢接、用于夹持、弯折或剪断金属丝或小物件的工具（a pair of pliers 一把钳子, needle-nose pliers 尖嘴钳, wire cutters 钢丝钳）。复数形式。
[例] ① Use pliers to pull the staple out of the wood. 用钳子把订书钉从木头里拔出来。 ② Electricians carry several types of pliers in their belts. 电工腰带上别着几种钳子。
[搭] a pair of pliers 一把钳子, needle-nose pliers 尖嘴钳, wire cutters 钢丝钳, grip with pliers 用钳夹持
[联] wrench 扳手, wire 金属丝, grip 夹持, cut 剪, tool 工具, electrician 电工, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 复数形式 a pair of pliers。plier 单数极少单独使用

**saw**
英/美 /sɔː/
n. 锯
v. 锯；锯开
[义] A tool with a toothed blade used for cutting wood or metal; to cut with a saw. 带齿刃、用于锯切木材或金属的工具；用锯切割（hand saw 手锯, circular saw 圆锯, saw sth. in half 把某物锯成两半）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He marked the board and cut it with a hand saw. 他在木板上做记号，用手锯锯开。 ② They sawed through the fallen branch blocking the path. 他们锯断挡路的倒木树枝。
[形] saw / sawed / sawed 或 sawn / sawing
[搭] hand saw 手锯, circular saw 圆锯, chain saw 链锯, saw sth. in half 把某物锯成两半, saw off 锯下
[联] cut 切, blade 刀刃, wood 木头, carpenter 木匠, drill 钻, axe 斧, timber 木材
[记] 可数：a saw。过去分词英式 sawn、美式 sawed 均可。see（看见）同形异义

**drill**
英/美 /drɪl/
n. 钻；钻机；训练
v. 钻孔；训练
[义] A tool or machine for making holes in hard materials; an exercise or practice. 在坚硬材料上打孔的工具或机器；操练、训练（electric drill 电钻, drill a hole 钻孔, fire drill 消防演习）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He drilled pilot holes before driving in the screws. 拧螺丝前他先钻了引导孔。 ② The school holds a fire drill once a term. 学校每学期举行一次消防演习。
[搭] electric drill 电钻, drill a hole 钻孔, fire drill 消防演习, drill bit 钻头, power drill 电钻
[联] screw 螺丝, hole 孔, tool 工具, bore 钻孔, practice 练习, workshop 车间, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a drill。drill 作「训练」义时可数/不可数均可。drill bit 钻头

**axe**
英 /æks/ 美 /æks/
n. 斧
v. 大幅削减；解雇
[义] A tool with a heavy bladed head on a handle, used for chopping wood; to cancel or cut drastically. 长柄重刃、用于劈柴的工具；大幅削减、砍掉（chop wood with an axe 用斧劈柴, axe a project 砍掉项目）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He split the log with a single swing of the axe. 他一斧子把原木劈开。 ② The company axed dozens of jobs last quarter. 公司上季度裁减了数十个岗位。
[搭] chop wood with an axe 用斧劈柴, axe a project 砍掉项目, battle axe 战斧, axe handle 斧柄
[联] chop 砍, wood 木头, blade 刃, hatchet 短柄小斧, lumberjack 伐木工, cut 切, tool 工具
[记] 可数：an axe。美式拼写 ax 亦可。动词 axe 英式拼写；美式常作 ax

**chisel**
英/美 /ˈtʃɪzl/
n. 凿子
v. 凿；雕
[义] A tool with a sharp flat edge used for cutting and shaping wood or stone. 刃口锋利扁平、用于切削塑形木材或石头的工具（wood chisel 木凿, chisel away at 慢慢凿掉）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The sculptor chose the right chisel for the marble block. 雕塑家为大理石块选合适的凿子。 ② Someone had chiselled graffiti into the bench. 有人在长椅上凿刻了涂鸦。
[搭] wood chisel 木凿, chisel away at 慢慢凿掉, sharp chisel 锋利的凿子, stone chisel 石凿
[联] carve 雕刻, sculpt 雕塑, hammer 锤, blade 刃, stone 石头, wood 木头, sculptor 雕塑家
[记] 可数：a chisel。动词 chisel 亦喻「欺诈、占便宜」（chisel sb. out of sth.）

**file**
英/美 /faɪl/
n. 锉刀；文件
v. 锉；归档
[义] A tool with a rough surface for smoothing or shaping metal or wood; a collection of documents; to rub with a file or submit documents. 表面粗糙、用于打磨金属或木头的工具；文件集合；用锉刀磨或提交文件（nail file 指甲锉, metal file 金属锉, file sth. down 把某物锉薄）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She filed the rough edge until it was smooth. 她把毛边锉到光滑。 ② Keep a metal file in the toolbox for small repairs. 工具箱里放一把金属锉，做小修时用。
[搭] nail file 指甲锉, metal file 金属锉, file sth. down 把某物锉薄, file cabinet 文件柜, on file 存档
[联] sandpaper 砂纸, smooth 磨平, sharpen 磨尖, tool 工具, document 文件, polish 抛光, edge 边缘
[记] 同形异义：工具 vs. 文件。语境区分。file 作「列队行进」亦常见（file past 列队走过）

**sandpaper**
英/美 /ˈsændpeɪpə(r)/
n. 砂纸
v. 用砂纸打磨
[义] Strong paper with a rough layer of sand glued to it, used for making surfaces smooth. 一面涂沙、用于打磨表面的强韧纸（sandpaper the surface 用砂纸打磨表面, coarse sandpaper 粗砂纸）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Sandpaper the edges before applying varnish. 上清漆前先用砂纸磨边。 ② He kept several grades of sandpaper in the workshop. 他在车间备有几种粒度的砂纸。
[搭] sandpaper the surface 用砂纸打磨表面, coarse sandpaper 粗砂纸, fine sandpaper 细砂纸, sheet of sandpaper 一张砂纸
[联] smooth 磨平, polish 抛光, wood 木头, surface 表面, file 锉, varnish 清漆, carpenter 木匠
[记] 不可数；可说 a sheet of sandpaper。动词 sandpaper

**bolt**
英/美 /bəʊlt/
n. 螺栓；门闩；闪电
v. 闩门；猛冲
[义] A metal pin with a head, screwed into a nut to fasten things; a bar that slides to lock a door; a flash of lightning. 带帽金属杆，与螺母配合紧固；门闩；闪电（nuts and bolts 具体细节, bolt the door 闩门）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Tighten every bolt before starting the engine. 启动发动机前拧紧每颗螺栓。 ② She bolted the door and checked the windows. 她闩上门，又检查了窗户。
[搭] nuts and bolts 具体细节, bolt the door 闩门, lightning bolt 闪电, bolt upright 笔直地, bolt from the blue 晴天霹雳
[联] nut 螺母, screw 螺丝, wrench 扳手, fasten 固定, lock 锁, metal 金属, tighten 拧紧
[记] 可数：a bolt。nuts and bolts 喻「基本细节」。bolt 动词表「猛冲、狼吞虎咽」

**nut**
英/美 /nʌt/
n. 螺母；坚果
[义] A small metal block with a hole threaded on the inside, screwed onto a bolt; the dry fruit of some trees. 内孔有螺纹、拧在螺栓上的金属块；坚果（tighten a nut 拧紧螺母, nuts and bolts 基本细节）。可数。
[例] ① The nut was loose, so the wheel wobbled. 螺母松了，轮子晃荡。 ② He ate a handful of nuts while waiting. 等待时他抓了一把坚果吃。
[搭] tighten a nut 拧紧螺母, nuts and bolts 基本细节, wing nut 翼形螺母, nutcracker 胡桃钳
[联] bolt 螺栓, screw 螺丝, wrench 扳手, fasten 固定, peanut 花生, almond 杏仁, metal 金属
[记] 可数：a nut。nut 俚语「疯子、怪人」；hard nut to crack 难对付的人/事

**tape**
英/美 /teɪp/
n. 胶带；磁带；卷尺
v. 用胶带粘贴；录音
[义] A long narrow strip of sticky material used for fastening; magnetic recording material; to record or fasten with tape. 用于粘贴的狭长黏性材料；磁带；用胶带粘或录音（adhesive tape 胶带, duct tape 强力胶带, tape measure 卷尺）。不可数/可数/动词。
[例] ① Tape the box shut before you carry it downstairs. 搬下楼前用胶带封好箱子。 ② She used masking tape to mark the wall for painting. 她用美纹纸胶带在墙上标出要刷漆的位置。
[搭] adhesive tape 胶带, duct tape 强力胶带, masking tape 美纹纸胶带, tape measure 卷尺, red tape 繁文缛节
[联] glue 胶, stick 粘, fasten 固定, measure 测量, record 录音, seal 封, package 包裹
[记] 不可数/可数。red tape 喻「官僚繁文」。duct tape 万能修修补补的梗

**glue**
英/美 /ɡluː/
n. 胶水
v. 粘贴
[义] A sticky substance used for joining things together; to join with glue. 用于粘合物体的黏性物质；用胶粘贴（super glue 强力胶, glue sth. together 把某物粘在一起, glued to the screen 盯着屏幕〔喻〕）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Apply a thin layer of glue along the edge. 沿边缘涂薄层胶水。 ② The kids glued sequins onto cardboard stars. 孩子们把亮片粘在纸板星星上。
[搭] super glue 强力胶, glue sth. together 把某物粘在一起, glue stick 固体胶棒, wood glue 木工胶
[联] tape 胶带, adhesive 黏合剂, stick 粘, paste 浆糊, bond 粘合, repair 修补, craft 手工
[记] 不可数；a tube of glue。 glued to 喻「目不转睛盯着」。glue 与 blue 押韵可联想

**rope**
英/美 /rəʊp/
n. 绳子；绳索
v. 用绳捆；套索
[义] A length of thick strong cord made by twisting strands together; to tie or catch with a rope. 多股拧成的粗韧绳；用绳捆绑或套住（a piece of rope 一段绳, rope ladder 绳梯, know the ropes 熟悉门道〔喻〕）。可数/不可数/动词。
[例] ① They tied the boat to the dock with a thick rope. 他们用粗绳把船系在码头上。 ② Climbers checked every knot in the rope before ascending. 攀岩者上升前检查了绳上每个结。
[搭] a piece of rope 一段绳, rope ladder 绳梯, know the ropes 熟悉门道, rope in 拉拢参与, skip rope 跳绳
[联] string 细绳, cord 绳, chain 链, knot 结, tie 系, climb 攀爬, ladder 梯子
[记] 可数/不可数。give sb. enough rope 放任其自取灭亡。rope vs. string：rope 更粗更结实

**chain**
英/美 /tʃeɪn/
n. 链条；连锁
v. 用链条拴住
[义] A series of connected metal links used for pulling or fastening; a connected series of stores, events, etc. 串联金属环，用于拉拽或固定；连锁（chain saw 链锯, supply chain 供应链, chain reaction 连锁反应）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He locked the gate with a heavy chain and padlock. 他用粗链和挂锁锁上大门。 ② The bicycle was chained to the railings. 自行车被链在栏杆上。
[搭] chain saw 链锯, supply chain 供应链, chain reaction 连锁反应, chain store 连锁店, chain link 链环
[联] rope 绳, link 环, lock 锁, fasten 固定, metal 金属, connect 连接, bind 捆绑
[记] 可数：a chain。chain 喻「一连串」（chain of events）。chain smoker 一根接一根抽烟的人

**hook**
英/美 /hʊk/
n. 钩；挂钩
v. 钩住；吸引
[义] A curved piece of metal or plastic for catching, holding, or hanging things; to catch or fasten with a hook. 用于挂、抓、固定的弯形金属或塑料件；钩住、吸引（coat hook 衣帽钩, hook sth. on 把某物挂上, off the hook 脱身〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Hang your jacket on the hook by the door. 把夹克挂在门边挂钩上。 ② The campaign hooked viewers with a dramatic opening scene. 宣传以戏剧性开场抓住了观众。
[搭] coat hook 衣帽钩, hook sth. on 把某物挂上, off the hook 脱身, hook and eye 钩眼扣, fishing hook 鱼钩
[联] hang 挂, catch 抓住, loop 环, chain 链, bait 鱼饵, attach 附着, peg 钉
[记] 可数：a hook。by hook or by crook 千方百计。hook 动词喻「吸引、上瘾」

**clamp**
英/美 /klæmp/
n. 夹钳；夹板
v. 夹紧； clamp down 严厉打击
[义] A device for holding things tightly together; to fasten or hold with a clamp; to suppress firmly. 将物体紧紧夹在一起的装置；用夹钳固定；严厉打击（C-clamp C 形夹, clamp sth. in place 把某物夹牢, clamp down on 严厉打击）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Use a clamp to hold the two boards while the glue dries. 胶水干之前用夹钳夹住两块板。 ② Authorities clamped down on illegal street racing. 当局严厉打击非法街头飙车。
[搭] C-clamp C 形夹, clamp sth. in place 把某物夹牢, clamp down on 严厉打击, hose clamp 喉箍
[联] grip 夹持, pliers 钳, screw 螺丝, tighten 拧紧, hold 固定, vice 台虎钳, fasten 紧固
[记] 可数：a clamp。clamp down on 新闻/政策高频。clamp 与 cramp（抽筋）勿混

**scissors**
英/美 /ˈsɪzəz/
n. 剪刀
[义] A tool with two blades joined in the middle, used for cutting paper, cloth, etc. 两刃在中间枢接、用于剪纸布等的工具（a pair of scissors 一把剪刀, cut with scissors 用剪刀剪）。复数形式。
[例] ① These scissors are too blunt to cut thick cardboard. 这把剪刀太钝，裁不动厚纸板。 ② Keep scissors out of reach of small children. 剪刀应放在幼儿够不到的地方。
[搭] a pair of scissors 一把剪刀, cut with scissors 用剪刀剪, kitchen scissors 厨房剪刀, nail scissors 指甲剪
[联] cut 剪, blade 刃, knife 刀, shear 大剪刀, sharp 锋利的, craft 手工, tailor 裁缝
[记] 复数 a pair of scissors；不说 × a scissor。scissor 作动词少见，多用 cut

**knife**
英/美 /naɪf/
n. 刀
v. 用刀刺
（复数 knives /naɪvz/）
[义] A tool or weapon with a sharp blade and a handle, used for cutting. 带锋利刃与柄、用于切割的工具（kitchen knife 厨刀, pocket knife 折叠刀, knife and fork 刀叉）。可数。
[例] ① Sharpen the knife before you chop the vegetables. 切菜前把刀磨快。 ② Plastic knives are provided for the picnic. 野餐提供塑料刀。
[搭] kitchen knife 厨刀, pocket knife 折叠刀, knife and fork 刀叉, sharp knife 锋利的刀, butter knife 黄油刀
[联] blade 刀刃, cut 切, scissors 剪刀, fork 叉, sharp 锋利, chop 剁, utensil 用具
[记] 复数 knives。knife 在冲突语境可指武器，本章侧重工具义；under the knife 接受手术（喻）

**blade**
英/美 /bleɪd/
n. 刀刃；叶片
[义] The flat cutting edge of a knife, sword, or tool; a flat wide leaf or the flat part of a propeller. 刀、剑或工具的扁平刃部；叶片；螺旋桨叶（ razor blade 刀片, blade of grass 草叶, replace the blade 更换刀片）。可数。
[例] ① The blade needs sharpening—it tears instead of slicing. 刀刃该磨了，现在切下去是撕不是片。 ② Wipe the blade clean after each use. 每次用完把刀刃擦干净。
[搭] razor blade 刀片, blade of grass 草叶, replace the blade 更换刀片, sharp blade 锋利的刀刃, razor-sharp blade 极锋利的刃
[联] knife 刀, sharp 锋利, cut 切, scissors 剪刀, grass 草, shave 刮, edge 边缘
[记] 可数：a blade。blade 亦指草叶、桨叶、冰刀刃等

**ruler**
英/美 /ˈruːlə(r)/
n. 尺子；统治者
[义] A straight strip marked with centimetres or inches, used for measuring or drawing straight lines; a person who rules. 标有厘米或英寸刻度、用于测量或画直线的直条；统治者（plastic ruler 塑料尺, under the ruler 在尺子下〔测量〕）。可数。
[例] ① Draw a straight line using a ruler and pencil. 用尺子和铅笔画一条直线。 ② The ruler snapped when he leaned on it. 他倚在上面时尺子断了。
[搭] plastic ruler 塑料尺, ruler measurement 尺量, yardstick 码尺, straight edge 直边
[联] measure 测量, length 长度, inch 英寸, centimetre 厘米, draw 画, tape measure 卷尺, scale 比例尺
[记] 可数：a ruler。ruler vs. rule：ruler 实物尺；rule 规则/统治

**tape measure**
英/美 /teɪp ˈmeʒə(r)/
n. 卷尺
[义] A long flexible strip marked with measurements, that rolls up into a case, used for measuring length. 标有刻度、可卷入盒中的柔性长尺，用于量长度（retractable tape measure 伸缩卷尺, take measurements with a tape measure 用卷尺量尺寸）。可数。
[例] ① She pulled the tape measure across the room to check the width. 她拉卷尺量房间宽度。 ② The tape measure retracted with a snap. 卷尺啪的一声缩回。
[搭] retractable tape measure 伸缩卷尺, take measurements with a tape measure 用卷尺量尺寸, steel tape measure 钢卷尺
[联] ruler 直尺, measure 测量, length 长度, inch 英寸, carpenter 木匠, dimension 尺寸, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数：a tape measure。亦说 measuring tape，同义

**scale**
英/美 /skeɪl/
n. 秤；规模；刻度；鳞
v. 攀登； scaling 缩放
[义] A device for weighing; the size or extent of something; a mark on a measuring instrument; a small plate on a fish or reptile. 称重装置；规模；刻度；鱼鳞（kitchen scale 厨房秤, on a large scale 大规模, scale of 1:100 1:100 比例）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Place the bowl on the scale and reset it to zero. 把碗放在秤上，归零。 ② The map is drawn to a scale of one centimetre per kilometre. 地图按一厘米代表一公里绘制。
[搭] kitchen scale 厨房秤, on a large scale 大规模, scale of 1:100 1:100 比例, weigh on a scale 上秤称重, bathroom scale 体重秤
[联] weigh 称重, weight 重量, measure 测量, balance 天平, size 规模, ruler 尺, kilogram 千克
[记] 多义同形；语境区分。scales 复数可指「天平/秤」。scale 动词表「攀登、按比例调整」

**level**
英/美 /ˈlevl/
n. 水平仪；水平；等级
adj. 平的；同高度的
v. 使平坦；夷平
[义] A tool for checking whether a surface is horizontal; a position on a scale; flat and even. 检查表面是否水平的工具；等级；平坦的（spirit level 水平仪, on the level 诚实可靠〔口语〕, level surface 水平面）。可数/形容词/动词。
[例] ① Use a level to make sure the shelf is straight. 用水平仪确认架子是否水平。 ② Water finds its own level. 水往低处流（水平面自成）。
[搭] spirit level 水平仪, on the level 诚实可靠, level surface 水平面, level off 趋平, eye level 与眼齐平
[联] flat 平的, horizontal 水平的, measure 测量, straight 直的, balance 平衡, surface 表面, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a level。level with sb. 对某人坦白。level 作形容词、动词、名词均常见

**ladder**
英/美 /ˈlædə(r)/
n. 梯子；阶梯
[义] A piece of equipment with steps for climbing up or down; a series of stages by which you can advance. 带级、供上下的设备；晋升阶梯（climb a ladder 爬梯子, stepladder 折梯, career ladder 职业阶梯〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① He propped the ladder against the wall and climbed carefully. 他把梯子靠墙支好，小心往上爬。 ② She moved up the career ladder within five years. 她五年内沿职业阶梯上升。
[搭] climb a ladder 爬梯子, stepladder 折梯, career ladder 职业阶梯, rope ladder 绳梯, top of the ladder 顶端
[联] step 台阶, climb 爬, height 高度, rope 绳, tool 工具, scaffold 脚手架, rise 上升
[记] 可数：a ladder。英式 ladder 亦指「丝袜抽丝」（get a ladder in your tights）

**shovel**
英/美 /ˈʃʌvl/
n. 铲子；铁锹
v. 铲
[义] A tool with a broad blade and a handle, used for lifting and moving snow, soil, or coal. 宽刃长柄、用于铲雪土煤的工具（snow shovel 雪铲, shovel snow 铲雪, shovel sth. into 把某物铲进）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He shovelled snow from the driveway before breakfast. 早饭前他把车道上的雪铲干净。 ② Grab a shovel—we need to dig out the car. 拿把铁锹，得把车挖出来。
[搭] snow shovel 雪铲, shovel snow 铲雪, shovel sth. into 把某物铲进, coal shovel 煤铲
[联] dig 挖, spade 锹, rake 耙, soil 土, snow 雪, garden 花园, bucket 桶
[记] 可数：a shovel。动词 shovel / shovelled / shovelling（英式双 l）

**rake**
英/美 /reɪk/
n. 耙子
v. 耙； rake in 大赚
[义] A garden tool with a long handle and a row of teeth, used for gathering leaves or smoothing soil; to gather or smooth with a rake; to earn a lot. 长柄带齿、用于耙集落叶或平整土壤的园艺工具；耙；大赚（leaf rake 叶耙, rake the lawn 耙草坪, rake in profits 大赚）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Autumn leaves covered the yard until we raked them into piles. 秋叶铺满院子，我们把它们耙成堆。 ② The film raked in millions on opening weekend. 影片首周末票房大收。
[搭] leaf rake 叶耙, rake the lawn 耙草坪, rake in profits 大赚, rake over old coals 重提旧事
[联] shovel 铲, lawn 草坪, leaf 叶子, garden 花园, hoe 锄, soil 土, gather 收集
[记] 可数：a rake。rake in 喻「轻松赚大钱」。rake 亦指「浪荡子、花花公子」（旧义）

**hoe**
英/美 /həʊ/
n. 锄头
v. 锄
[义] A garden tool with a thin flat blade, used for breaking up soil and removing weeds. 薄平刃、用于松土除草的园艺工具（garden hoe 园艺锄, hoe the rows 锄行间）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She hoed between the vegetable rows every morning. 她每天早晨在菜畦间锄草。 ② A sharp hoe makes weeding faster. 锋利的锄头除草更快。
[搭] garden hoe 园艺锄, hoe the rows 锄行间, draw hoe 钉齿锄, weed 除草
[联] shovel 铲, rake 耙, weed 杂草, soil 土, garden 花园, cultivate 耕作, spade 锹
[记] 可数：a hoe。hoe 与 how 不同音。动词 hoe the garden

**broom**
英/美 /bruːm/
n. 扫帚
v. 扫除
[义] A brush with a long handle, used for sweeping floors. 长柄刷具，用于清扫地面（ sweep with a broom 用扫帚扫, new broom 新官上任〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She swept the porch with a straw broom. 她用草编扫帚扫门廊。 ② A new broom sweeps clean, but old corners remain. 新扫帚扫得干净，老角落还在（谚，喻新领导爱折腾）。
[搭] sweep with a broom 用扫帚扫, new broom 新官上任, broom closet 清洁用具间, whisk broom 小扫帚
[联] sweep 扫, mop 拖把, brush 刷子, clean 清洁, dust 灰尘, floor 地板, sponge 海绵
[记] 可数：a broom。new broom sweeps clean 谚语。broom 亦指「金雀花属植物」

**mop**
英/美 /mɒp/
n. 拖把
v. 用拖把拖； mop up 收尾
[义] A tool with a bundle of thick strings or a sponge on a handle, used for washing floors; to clean with a mop. 长柄一端束状纤维或海绵、用于拖地的工具；用拖把清洁（mop the floor 拖地, mop up spills 擦掉溢出物, mop and bucket 拖把和水桶）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He mopped the kitchen floor after the party. 聚会后他拖了厨房地。 ② Mop up the coffee before it stains the carpet. 趁咖啡还没渍进地毯，快擦掉。
[搭] mop the floor 拖地, mop up spills 擦掉溢出物, mop and bucket 拖把和水桶, mop handle 拖把杆
[联] broom 扫帚, sweep 扫, floor 地板, clean 清洁, bucket 桶, sponge 海绵, wipe 擦
[记] 可数：a mop。mop 动词 mop up 亦指「收尾、扫清残敌」。mop of hair 浓密的头发

**brush**
英/美 /brʌʃ/
n. 刷子；画笔；灌木丛
v. 刷；轻触
[义] An object with bristles or wires set into a handle, used for cleaning, painting, or grooming; to clean or touch lightly with a brush. 柄上装毛或金属丝、用于清洁、绘画或梳理的物件；轻刷、轻触（toothbrush 牙刷, paintbrush 漆刷, brush sth. off 刷掉/不予理会）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Use a stiff brush to scrub the grout between the tiles. 用硬毛刷刷洗瓷砖缝。 ② She brushed the dust off her jacket. 她把夹克上的灰尘掸掉。
[搭] toothbrush 牙刷, paintbrush 漆刷, brush sth. off 刷掉/不予理会, wire brush 钢丝刷, brush aside 漠视
[联] broom 扫帚, scrub 刷洗, paint 漆, clean 清洁, bristle 刷毛, sweep 扫, polish 抛光
[记] 可数：a brush。brush up on 复习温习。brush with death 与死神擦肩而过

**sponge**
英/美 /spʌndʒ/
n. 海绵；海绵块
v. 用海绵擦；蹭
[义] A light porous substance that absorbs water; a piece of this or synthetic material used for washing; to absorb or obtain without paying. 多孔吸水物质；用于擦洗的天然或合成块；白吃白占（kitchen sponge 厨房海绵, sponge bath 擦浴, sponge off sb. 蹭某人的〔口语〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Wipe the counter with a damp sponge. 用湿海绵擦台面。 ② Natural sea sponges are still used in some artisan workshops. 一些手工坊仍用天然海棉。
[搭] kitchen sponge 厨房海绵, sponge bath 擦浴, sponge off sb. 蹭某人的, sponge cake 海绵蛋糕
[联] absorb 吸收, wipe 擦, clean 清洁, mop 拖把, porous 多孔的, wash 洗, soak 浸泡
[记] 可数：a sponge。sponge off 口语「依赖他人」。sponge cake 海绵蛋糕与清洁海绵同词不同义

**bucket**
英/美 /ˈbʌkɪt/
n. 桶；水桶
[义] A roughly cylindrical open container with a handle, used for carrying liquids or solids. 带柄的近似圆柱敞口容器，用于装运液体或固体（bucket of water 一桶水, mop and bucket 拖把和水桶, kick the bucket 翘辫子〔俚〕）。可数。
[例] ① Fill the bucket with soapy water before mopping. 拖地前给桶装满肥皂水。 ② The kids built sandcastles with a plastic bucket and spade. 孩子们用塑料桶和小铲堆沙堡。
[搭] bucket of water 一桶水, mop and bucket 拖把和水桶, kick the bucket 翘辫子, bucket list 遗愿清单
[联] pail 桶（同义）, mop 拖把, water 水, carry 提, container 容器, spade 铲, fill 装满
[记] 可数：a bucket。bucket list 来自 kick the bucket 前的遗愿。pail 与 bucket 常可互换

**vacuum**
英/美 /ˈvækjuːm/
n. 真空；真空吸尘器
v. 用吸尘器清洁
[义] A space entirely without matter; a vacuum cleaner; to clean with a vacuum cleaner. 完全无物质的空；真空吸尘器；用吸尘器清洁（vacuum the carpet 吸地毯, vacuum cleaner 吸尘器, power vacuum 强力真空〔喻〕）。可数/不可数/动词。
[例] ① She vacuumed the living room before the guests arrived. 客人来之前她用吸尘器吸了客厅。 ② Dust the shelves first, then vacuum the floor. 先掸架子，再吸地板。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *vacuus*（空的）。
[族] vacant 空的, evacuate 撤离, vacuum cleaner 吸尘器
[搭] vacuum the carpet 吸地毯, vacuum cleaner 吸尘器, vacuum seal 真空密封, vacuum pack 真空包装
[联] clean 清洁, carpet 地毯, dust 灰尘, mop 拖把, suction 吸力, hose 软管, appliance 电器
[记] 可数/不可数/动词。口语 vacuum = vacuum cleaner。in a vacuum 在真空中/脱离现实

**power tool**
英/美 /ˈpaʊə tuːl/
n. 电动工具
[义] A tool driven by electricity or compressed air rather than by hand. 由电或压缩空气驱动、非纯手力的工具（cordless power tool 无绳电动工具, power drill 电钻, power saw 电锯）。可数。
[例] ① Always wear goggles when using power tools. 使用电动工具时务必戴护目镜。 ② Cordless power tools are easier to use on ladders. 无绳电动工具在梯子上更好用。
[搭] cordless power tool 无绳电动工具, power drill 电钻, power saw 电锯, power tool safety 电动工具安全
[联] tool 工具, drill 钻, saw 锯, electric 电动的, machine 机器, workshop 车间, battery 电池
[记] 可数：a power tool。与 hand tool 相对。power tool 强调动力来源

**toolbox**
英/美 /ˈtuːlbɒks/
n. 工具箱
[义] A box or case for keeping tools in. 存放工具的箱或盒（metal toolbox 金属工具箱, toolbox talk 工地安全简报〔行业用语〕）。可数。
[例] ① He rummaged through the toolbox for the right wrench. 他在工具箱里翻找合适的扳手。 ② Keep a basic toolbox in the car for emergencies. 车里备一个基本工具箱应急。
[搭] metal toolbox 金属工具箱, toolbox talk 工地安全简报, open the toolbox 打开工具箱
[联] tool 工具, hammer 锤, wrench 扳手, screw 螺丝, repair 修理, garage 车库, kit 成套工具
[记] 可数：a toolbox。便携单箱；成套工具说 toolkit，大抽屉柜说 tool chest

**workshop**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkʃɒp/
n. 车间；作坊；研讨会
[义] A room or building where tools and machines are used for making or repairing things; a meeting for discussion or training. 使用工具与机器制作或修理物品的房间或建筑；研讨培训会（home workshop 家庭作坊, workshop tools 车间工具, writing workshop 写作班）。可数。
[例] ① He spends weekends in the garage workshop building furniture. 他周末在车库作坊里做家具。 ② The university runs creative writing workshops each term. 大学每学期开创意写作研讨班。
[搭] home workshop 家庭作坊, workshop tools 车间工具, writing workshop 写作班, workshop floor 车间地面
[联] tool 工具, carpenter 木匠, machine 机器, craft 工艺, repair 修理, factory 工厂, bench 工作台
[记] 可数：a workshop。work + shop；研讨义与工具义同形，靠语境区分

**workbench**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkbenʧ/
n. 工作台
[义] A sturdy table at which manual work is done, especially in a workshop. 进行手工操作的结实桌子，尤指车间里的（cluttered workbench 杂乱的工作台, vise on a workbench 台虎钳）。可数。
[例] ① Sawdust covered the workbench after the afternoon's project. 下午做完项目后，工作台上满是锯末。 ② Clamp the wood to the workbench before you cut. 锯之前先把木头夹在工作台上。
[搭] cluttered workbench 杂乱的工作台, vise on a workbench 台虎钳, workbench surface 工作台面
[联] workshop 车间, bench 长凳, tool 工具, carpenter 木匠, clamp 夹钳, saw 锯, vise 台虎钳
[记] 可数：a workbench。美式亦拼 work bench（两词）

**vise**
英 /vaɪs/ 美 /vaɪs/
n. 台虎钳
[义] A tool with two jaws that can be tightened to hold an object firmly on a workbench. 两钳口可拧紧、将物体牢牢固定在工作台上的工具（bench vise 台虎钳, grip in a vise 用虎钳夹住）。可数。美式拼写 vice.
[例] ① Secure the metal rod in the vise before filing. 锉之前先把金属杆夹进虎钳。 ② The vise jaw left marks on the soft wood. 虎钳口在软木上留下了印痕。
[搭] bench vise 台虎钳, grip in a vise 用虎钳夹住, vise grip 大力钳（品牌泛指）
[联] clamp 夹钳, workbench 工作台, grip 夹持, tool 工具, hold 固定, tighten 拧紧, pliers 钳
[记] 可数：a vise。美式拼 vice，与 vice（恶习、副职）同形需语境区分

**needle**
英/美 /ˈniːdl/
n. 针；指针
[义] A small thin piece of steel with a point at one end and a hole for thread, used in sewing; the pointed end of a syringe; a thin pointer on a dial. 一端尖、一端有穿线孔的细钢针；注射针头；仪表指针（sewing needle 缝针, needle and thread 针线, knitting needle 织针）。可数。
[例] ① Thread the needle before you start hemming the skirt. 给裙边卷边前先穿针引线。 ② The compass needle pointed north. 指南针指针指向北方。
[搭] sewing needle 缝针, needle and thread 针线, knitting needle 织针, needle point 针尖, on a needle 如坐针毡
[联] thread 线, sew 缝, stitch 针脚, pin 别针, yarn 纱线, knit 编织, compass 指南针
[记] 可数：a needle。needle 亦指唱针、松针、注射针；本章侧重缝纫/手工针

**thread**
英/美 /θred/
n. 线；螺纹
v. 穿线； threading 发帖
[义] A long thin strand of cotton, nylon, etc., used in sewing; the raised spiral line on a screw; to pass thread through a needle. 缝纫用的棉线、尼龙线等；螺丝上的螺旋纹；穿线（spool of thread 一卷线, thread a needle 穿针, screw thread 螺纹）。不可数/可数/动词。
[例] ① The thread snapped when she pulled too hard. 她拉太猛，线断了。 ② Match the screw thread to the correct nut size. 把螺纹与合适螺母尺寸对应。
[搭] spool of thread 一卷线, thread a needle 穿针, screw thread 螺纹, hang by a thread 千钧一发
[联] needle 针, sew 缝, screw 螺丝, stitch 针脚, yarn 纱线, fabric 织物, knit 编织
[记] 不可数/可数。thread 动词亦指「穿行、穿针、网络发帖」。lose the thread 失去头绪

**spade**
英/美 /speɪd/
n. 锹；黑桃
[义] A tool with a broad flat blade and a long handle, used for digging; a playing-card suit. 宽平刃长柄、用于挖掘的工具；扑克黑桃（garden spade 园艺锹, dig with a spade 用锹挖, call a spade a spade 直言不讳〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① He dug a hole for the rose bush with a spade. 他用锹给玫瑰灌木挖坑。 ② Keep a spade and bucket in the shed for gardening. 棚里备一把锹和一个桶，园艺用。
[搭] garden spade 园艺锹, dig with a spade 用锹挖, call a spade a spade 直言不讳, spade handle 锹柄
[联] shovel 铲, dig 挖, hoe 锄, garden 花园, soil 土, bucket 桶, rake 耙
[记] 可数：a spade。shovel 更宽更偏「铲运」，spade 更偏「挖」。call a spade a spade 谚语

**crowbar**
英/美 /ˈkrəʊbɑː(r)/
n. 撬棍；铁撬
[义] A metal bar with a flattened end, used as a lever to prise things open or move heavy objects. 一端扁平、用作杠杆撬开或移动重物的金属杆（pry with a crowbar 用撬棍撬, crowbar open 撬开）。可数。
[例] ① They used a crowbar to lift the fallen beam. 他们用撬棍抬起倒下的横梁。 ② Thieves crowbarred the back door during the night. 窃贼夜里用撬棍撬了后门。
[搭] pry with a crowbar 用撬棍撬, crowbar open 撬开, iron crowbar 铁撬
[联] lever 杠杆, pry 撬, wrench 扳手, force 用力, break 破开, tool 工具, metal 金属
[记] 可数：a crowbar。动词 crowbar 表「用撬棍撬」。与 crow（乌鸦）同形不同源

**flashlight**
英/美 /ˈflæʃlaɪt/
n. 手电筒
[义] A small portable electric lamp powered by batteries. 电池供电的小型便携电灯（British torch 同义）（shine a flashlight 照射手电筒, flashlight beam 手电光束）。可数。
[例] ① Keep a flashlight in the glove compartment. 手套箱里放手电筒。 ② The power cut left us searching for flashlights in the dark. 停电后我们在黑暗里摸手电筒。
[搭] shine a flashlight 照射手电筒, flashlight beam 手电光束, rechargeable flashlight 充电手电
[联] torch 手电筒（英式）, light 光, battery 电池, lamp 灯, dark 黑暗, emergency 应急, power cut 停电
[记] 可数：a flashlight。英式 torch；美式 torch 多指火炬。flashlight 亦指舞台追光灯（不同义）

**torch**
英 /tɔːtʃ/ 美 /tɔːrtʃ/
n. 手电筒；火炬
[义] In British English, a small portable electric light; a stick with material that burns at the top, carried in processions or for light. 英式指手电筒；亦指火把、火炬（torch light 手电光, carry a torch for sb. 单恋某人〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① She grabbed a torch from the kitchen drawer when the lights went out. 灯灭时她从厨房抽屉抓了把手电。 ② Olympic torch relay routes are planned years ahead. 奥运火炬传递路线提前数年规划。
[搭] torch light 手电光, carry a torch for sb. 单恋某人, Olympic torch 奥运火炬, torch battery 手电电池
[联] flashlight 手电筒（美式）, light 光, flame 火焰, battery 电池, relay 传递, illuminate 照明
[记] 可数：a torch。carry a torch for sb. 旧式喻「仍暗恋某人」。美式 flashlight vs. 英式 torch

**whisk**
英/美 /wɪsk/
n. 打蛋器
v. 搅拌；迅速带走
[义] A kitchen tool made of wire loops or a balloon shape, used for beating eggs or cream; to beat or move quickly. 由金属丝环或球状头组成、用于搅打蛋液或奶油的厨房工具；快速搅拌或带走（wire whisk 钢丝打蛋器, whisk eggs 打鸡蛋, whisk away 迅速带走）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Whisk the egg whites until stiff peaks form. 把蛋白打到硬性发泡。 ② She whisked the sauce smoothly with a balloon whisk. 她用球型打蛋器把酱汁搅顺滑。
[搭] wire whisk 钢丝打蛋器, whisk eggs 打鸡蛋, balloon whisk 球型打蛋器, electric whisk 电动打蛋器
[联] stir 搅拌, beat 搅打, spoon 勺, mix 混合, cream 奶油, egg 鸡蛋, utensil 用具
[记] 可数：a whisk。whisk away/off 喻「迅速带走」。whisk 与 risk 不同音

**spatula**
英/美 /ˈspætʃələ/
n. 锅铲；刮刀
[义] A broad flat blade on a handle, used for lifting, spreading, or mixing food, especially in cooking. 宽平刃长柄，用于翻动、涂抹或混合食物，尤指烹饪（rubber spatula 橡胶刮刀, metal spatula 金属锅铲, flip with a spatula 用铲翻面）。可数。
[例] ① Use a spatula to flip the pancakes without breaking them. 用锅铲翻面，别把煎饼弄破。 ② Scrape the bowl clean with a rubber spatula. 用橡胶刮刀把碗刮干净。
[搭] rubber spatula 橡胶刮刀, metal spatula 金属锅铲, flip with a spatula 用铲翻面, offset spatula 偏移刮刀
[联] spoon 勺, ladle 汤勺, flip 翻面, fry 煎, bake 烘焙, utensil 用具, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数：a spatula。美式 flipper 口语亦指锅铲。spatula 亦指实验室抹刀

**ladle**
英/美 /ˈleɪdl/
n. 长柄勺；汤勺
v. 用勺舀
[义] A large long-handled spoon with a deep bowl, used for serving soup or stew. 深碗长柄大勺，用于盛汤或炖菜（soup ladle 汤勺, ladle out 舀出, ladle sth. into bowls 把某物舀进碗里）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She ladled hot soup into bowls for the guests. 她把热汤舀进客人的碗里。 ② The ladle hung on a hook beside the stove. 汤勺挂在炉子旁的钩子上。
[搭] soup ladle 汤勺, ladle out 舀出, ladle sth. into bowls 把某物舀进碗里, serving ladle 分餐勺
[联] spoon 勺, soup 汤, serve 盛, pot 锅, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, stew 炖菜
[记] 可数：a ladle。动词 ladle 较书面。与 candle（蜡烛）不同源

**grater**
英/美 /ˈɡreɪtə(r)/
n. 擦丝器；刨丝器
[义] A kitchen tool with a rough surface, used for shredding cheese, vegetables, etc. 表面粗糙、用于刨碎奶酪蔬菜等的厨房工具（cheese grater 奶酪擦丝器, box grater 盒式刨丝器, grate cheese 刨奶酪）。可数。
[例] ① Grate the carrot on the fine side of the box grater. 用盒式刨丝器细面把胡萝卜刨丝。 ② The grater left shallow cuts on his knuckle. 刨丝器在他指节上划了几道浅口子。
[搭] cheese grater 奶酪擦丝器, box grater 盒式刨丝器, grate cheese 刨奶酪, microplane grater 细刨丝器
[联] shred 刨丝, cheese 奶酪, peel 削皮, knife 刀, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, slice 切片
[记] 可数：a grater。动词 grate 刨丝；grate 亦指「令人烦躁、吱嘎响」

**peeler**
英/美 /ˈpiːlə(r)/
n. 削皮器；削皮刀
[义] A kitchen tool with a blade, used for removing the skin from fruit or vegetables. 带刃、用于削去果蔬皮的厨房工具（potato peeler 土豆削皮器, vegetable peeler 蔬菜削皮刀, peel apples 削苹果皮）。可数。
[例] ① A sharp peeler makes quick work of potato skins. 锋利的削皮器很快能削完土豆皮。 ② Peel the carrots with a Y-shaped peeler. 用 Y 形削皮刀削胡萝卜皮。
[搭] potato peeler 土豆削皮器, vegetable peeler 蔬菜削皮刀, peel apples 削苹果皮, swivel peeler 旋转削皮器
[联] peel 削皮, knife 刀, potato 土豆, vegetable 蔬菜, kitchen 厨房, grater 刨丝器, slice 切片
[记] 可数：a peeler。动词 peel；peeler 专指工具

**can opener**
英/美 /kæn ˈəʊpənə(r)/
n. 开罐器
[义] A tool for opening metal cans of food. 打开金属食品罐的工具（manual can opener 手动开罐器, electric can opener 电动开罐器）。可数。
[例] ① The can opener's blade dulled after years of use. 开罐器的刀片用了多年后变钝了。 ② She rummaged for the can opener before making chili. 做 chili 前她翻找开罐器。
[搭] manual can opener 手动开罐器, electric can opener 电动开罐器, twist can opener 旋转式开罐器
[联] tin 罐头（英式）, can 罐头, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, blade 刀刃, open 打开, jar 罐
[记] 可数：a can opener。亦说 tin opener（英式）。英式 tin = 美式 can（罐头）

**stapler**
英/美 /ˈsteɪplə(r)/
n. 订书机
[义] A device for fastening sheets of paper together with staples. 用订书钉把纸张钉在一起的装置（office stapler 办公订书机, staple papers 钉文件, reload the stapler 给订书机装钉）。可数。
[例] ① Staple the pages in the top left corner. 把几页纸钉在左上角。 ② The stapler jammed when she tried to staple too many sheets. 她一次钉太多页，订书机卡住了。
[搭] office stapler 办公订书机, staple papers 钉文件, reload the stapler 装钉, heavy-duty stapler 重型订书机
[联] staple 订书钉, paper 纸, clip 夹子, office 办公室, bind 装订, punch 打孔器, stationery 文具
[记] 可数：a stapler。staple 名词/动词；stapler 指机器

**staple**
英/美 /ˈsteɪpl/
n. 订书钉；主要商品
v. 用订书钉钉
[义] A short piece of wire driven through sheets of paper and bent to hold them; a main product or item; to fasten with staples. 穿过纸张并弯折固定的短金属丝；主要商品；用订书钉钉（staple remover 起钉器, staple gun 钉枪, staple food 主食）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Pull the staple out carefully so you don't tear the paper. 小心拔出订书钉，别把纸撕了。 ② Rice is a staple food across much of Asia. 大米是亚洲许多地区的主食。
[搭] staple remover 起钉器, staple gun 钉枪, staple food 主食, office staples 办公订书钉
[联] stapler 订书机, paper 纸, nail 钉, fasten 固定, food 食物, bind 装订, clip 夹子
[记] 可数：a staple。staple 作形容词表「主要的」（staple diet 主要饮食）

**paper clip**
英/美 /ˈpeɪpə klɪp/
n. 回形针；纸夹
[义] A small piece of bent wire or plastic used to hold sheets of paper together. 弯曲金属丝或塑料小件，用于夹住纸张（bend a paper clip 弯回形针, clip papers together 把纸夹在一起）。可数。
[例] ① She sorted the receipts and held them with a paper clip. 她把收据整理好，用回形针夹住。 ② A bent paper clip can work as a temporary hook. 弯一下的回形针可以临时当挂钩用。
[搭] bend a paper clip 弯回形针, clip papers together 把纸夹在一起, metal paper clip 金属回形针, jumbo paper clip 大号回形针
[联] stapler 订书机, staple 订书钉, binder clip 长尾夹, clip 夹子, paper 纸, office 办公室, stationery 文具
[记] 可数：a paper clip。亦说 gem clip（英式旧称）。勿与 paperclip 软件混谈

**binder clip**
英/美 /ˈbaɪndə klɪp/
n. 长尾夹；文件夹夹
[义] A clip with two metal loops and a hinged lever, used to hold thick stacks of paper. 双金属环加铰链压柄，用于夹住较厚一叠纸（large binder clip 大号长尾夹, clip a stack of papers 夹一叠纸）。可数。
[例] ① A binder clip kept the draft chapters from sliding apart. 长尾夹把草稿章节夹在一起，没散开。 ② He clipped the cable to the desk edge with a binder clip. 他用长尾夹把线缆夹在桌边。
[搭] large binder clip 大号长尾夹, clip a stack of papers 夹一叠纸, metal binder clip 金属长尾夹
[联] paper clip 回形针, stapler 订书机, clip 夹子, bind 装订, paper 纸, folder 文件夹, office 办公室
[记] 可数：a binder clip。比 paper clip 夹力更大，适合厚文件

**hole punch**
英/美 /həʊl pʌntʃ/
n. 打孔器
[义] A tool that punches round holes in paper or other thin material, often for filing. 在纸张等薄材料上打出圆孔的工具，常便于归档（three-hole punch 三孔打孔器, punch holes in paper 在纸上打孔）。可数。
[例] ① Punch the pages before you put them in the binder. 放进活页夹前先给页面打孔。 ② The hole punch jammed when she tried to punch too many sheets. 她一次打太多页，打孔器卡住了。
[搭] three-hole punch 三孔打孔器, punch holes in paper 在纸上打孔, single-hole punch 单孔打孔器, heavy-duty hole punch 重型打孔器
[联] punch 打孔, paper 纸, binder 活页夹, stapler 订书机, office 办公室, file 归档, clip 夹子
[记] 可数：a hole punch。动词 punch holes；hole punch 指工具。亦简称 punch

**tongs**
英/美 /tɒŋz/
n. 夹子；火钳
[义] A tool with two arms joined at one end, used for picking up or holding hot or awkward objects. 两臂在一端连接、用于夹取烫手或不便用手拿的物品的工具（kitchen tongs 厨房夹子, barbecue tongs 烧烤夹, a pair of tongs 一把夹子）。复数形式。
[例] ① Use tongs to flip the sausages on the grill. 用夹子翻动烤架上的香肠。 ② She lifted the ice cubes with tongs into the glass. 她用夹子把冰块夹进玻璃杯。
[搭] kitchen tongs 厨房夹子, barbecue tongs 烧烤夹, a pair of tongs 一把夹子, salad tongs 沙拉夹, sugar tongs 糖夹
[联] grip 夹持, flip 翻面, grill 烧烤, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, spatula 锅铲, hot 烫的
[记] 复数 a pair of tongs；不说 × a tong。tongs 亦指实验室或美容用夹具

**colander**
英/美 /ˈkɒləndə(r)/
n. 漏盆；滤盆
[义] A bowl-shaped kitchen utensil with holes in it, used for draining liquids from food. 碗形、带孔，用于沥干食物中液体的厨房器皿（strain pasta in a colander 在漏盆里沥干意面, metal colander 金属漏盆）。可数。
[例] ① Drain the boiled potatoes in a colander before mashing them. 土豆煮熟后先在漏盆里沥干再捣。 ② Rinse the berries in a colander under cold water. 在漏盆里用冷水冲洗浆果。
[搭] strain pasta in a colander 在漏盆里沥干意面, metal colander 金属漏盆, plastic colander 塑料漏盆, rinse in a colander 在漏盆里冲洗
[联] sieve 筛子, strainer 滤网, drain 沥干, pasta 意面, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, rinse 冲洗
[记] 可数：a colander。colander 孔较大、沥干用；sieve/strainer 更细、过滤用

**sieve**
英/美 /sɪv/
n. 筛子；滤网
v. 筛；过滤
[义] A utensil with a mesh bottom, used for separating finer particles from coarser ones or for straining liquids. 底部为网孔、用于分离粗细颗粒或过滤液体的器皿（fine sieve 细筛, sieve flour 筛面粉, pass through a sieve 通过筛子）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Sieve the flour to remove any lumps before baking. 烘焙前把面粉过筛，去掉结块。 ② The tea leaves were caught in the fine mesh sieve. 茶叶被细网筛留住了。
[搭] fine sieve 细筛, sieve flour 筛面粉, pass through a sieve 通过筛子, mesh sieve 网筛
[联] strainer 滤网, colander 漏盆, filter 过滤, flour 面粉, sift 筛, mesh 网孔, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数：a sieve。动词 sieve / sift 近义。sieve 强调「筛分颗粒」

**strainer**
英/美 /ˈstreɪnə(r)/
n. 滤网；过滤器
[义] A device with a mesh or perforated surface used to retain solids while liquids pass through. 带网孔或穿孔表面、留住固体而让液体通过的设备（tea strainer 茶滤, mesh strainer 滤网, strain soup through a strainer 用滤网滤汤）。可数。
[例] ① Pour the broth through a fine strainer into a clean pot. 把肉汤通过细滤网倒进干净的锅。 ② She balanced the tea strainer over the cup before pouring. 倒茶前她把茶滤架在杯口上。
[搭] tea strainer 茶滤, mesh strainer 滤网, strain soup through a strainer 用滤网滤汤, fine strainer 细滤网
[联] sieve 筛子, colander 漏盆, filter 过滤, drain 沥干, mesh 网孔, tea 茶, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数：a strainer。strainer 多指液体过滤；colander 多指沥干大块食物

**rolling pin**
英/美 /ˈrəʊlɪŋ pɪn/
n. 擀面杖
[义] A cylinder rolled over dough to flatten it. 在面团上滚动以擀平的圆柱形工具（wooden rolling pin 木制擀面杖, roll out dough with a rolling pin 用擀面杖擀面团）。可数。
[例] ① Roll the pastry flat with a rolling pin before cutting shapes. 用擀面杖把面皮擀平再切形。 ② Dust the rolling pin with flour so the dough won't stick. 在擀面杖上撒面粉，免得面团粘住。
[搭] wooden rolling pin 木制擀面杖, roll out dough with a rolling pin 用擀面杖擀面团, marble rolling pin 大理石擀面杖
[联] dough 面团, bake 烘焙, flour 面粉, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, flatten 压平, pastry 面皮
[记] 可数：a rolling pin。roll out 擀开；rolling pin 无电动义

**mortar and pestle**
英/美 /ˈmɔːtə(r) ænd ˈpesl/
n. 研钵和研杵
[义] A hard bowl (mortar) and a heavy club-shaped tool (pestle) used together to crush or grind spices and other substances. 硬质碗（研钵）与重杵（研杵）配合，用于捣碎或研磨香料等物质（grind spices in a mortar and pestle 用研钵研杵研磨香料）。可数。
[例] ① She crushed garlic and herbs in a stone mortar and pestle. 她在石制研钵里用研杵捣碎大蒜和香草。 ② A mortar and pestle releases more aroma than an electric grinder. 研钵研杵比电动研磨器更能释香。
[搭] grind spices in a mortar and pestle 用研钵研杵研磨香料, stone mortar and pestle 石制研钵研杵, marble mortar 大理石研钵
[联] grind 研磨, crush 捣碎, spice 香料, garlic 大蒜, herb 香草, kitchen 厨房, pestle 研杵
[记] 可数：a mortar and pestle。mortar 单独亦可指灰浆、迫击炮，语境区分

**corkscrew**
英/美 /ˈkɔːkskruː/
n. 开瓶器（红酒）
v. 螺旋式移动
[义] A tool for pulling corks from bottles, especially wine bottles. 从瓶中拔出软木塞的工具，尤指红酒瓶（twist the corkscrew 转动开瓶器, lever corkscrew 杠杆式开瓶器）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He twisted the corkscrew slowly until the cork rose. 他慢慢转动开瓶器，直到软木塞升起。 ② Keep a corkscrew in the picnic basket for the wine. 野餐篮里放把开瓶器，方便开酒。
[搭] twist the corkscrew 转动开瓶器, lever corkscrew 杠杆式开瓶器, waiter's corkscrew 侍酒师开瓶器, pull a cork 拔塞
[联] bottle opener 开瓶器, wine 葡萄酒, cork 软木塞, twist 扭转, screw 螺丝, kitchen 厨房, bar 酒吧
[记] 可数：a corkscrew。cork + screw。corkscrew 动词表「螺旋式前进」（corkscrew through traffic）

**bottle opener**
英/美 /ˈbɒtl əʊpənə(r)/
n. 开瓶器
[义] A device for removing metal caps from bottles, especially beer or soda bottles. 打开瓶金属盖的设备，尤指啤酒或汽水瓶（cap opener 瓶盖起子, flip-top bottle opener 翻盖式开瓶器）。可数。
[例] ① The bottle opener on the fridge magnet never gets lost. 冰箱贴上的开瓶器总找得到。 ② He dug a bottle opener out of the picnic kit. 他从野餐套装里翻出开瓶器。
[搭] cap opener 瓶盖起子, flip-top bottle opener 翻盖式开瓶器, wall-mounted bottle opener 壁挂开瓶器
[联] corkscrew 红酒开瓶器, can opener 开罐器, cap 瓶盖, beer 啤酒, kitchen 厨房, bar 酒吧, twist off 拧开
[记] 可数：a bottle opener。corkscrew 专指软木塞；bottle opener 多指金属旋盖瓶

**cutting board**

[例] ① Chop the vegetables on a separate cutting board from the meat. 蔬菜和肉用不同的砧板切。 ② Oil the wooden cutting board to keep it from cracking. 给木制砧板上油，防止开裂。

[记] 可数：a cutting board。英式亦说 chopping board。生熟分开砧板是厨房安全常识

**measuring cup**
英/美 /ˈmeʒərɪŋ kʌp/
n. 量杯
[义] A cup marked with scales for measuring ingredients in cooking. 标有刻度、用于烹饪计量食材的杯子（set of measuring cups 一套量杯, measure flour with a measuring cup 用量杯量面粉）。可数。
[例] ① Measure two cups of flour using the measuring cup. 用量杯量两杯面粉。 ② Liquid measuring cups usually have a spout for pouring. 液体量杯通常带倾倒嘴。

[记] 可数：a measuring cup。干量杯与液体量杯刻度读法不同（干料堆平，液体看弯月面）

**garlic press**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːlɪk pres/
n. 压蒜器
[义] A kitchen tool that crushes garlic cloves by forcing them through small holes. 将蒜瓣从小孔挤出压碎的工具（squeeze garlic through a press 用压蒜器压蒜, metal garlic press 金属压蒜器）。可数。
[例] ① Press two cloves of garlic into the sauce at the end. 最后把两瓣蒜用压蒜器压进酱汁。 ② Rinse the garlic press immediately so it doesn't dry stuck. 压蒜器用完立刻冲洗，免得干了粘住。

**sander**
英/美 /ˈsændə(r)/
n. 砂光机；打磨机
[义] A power tool that smooths surfaces by abrasion with sandpaper. 用砂纸摩擦打磨表面的电动工具（electric sander 电动砂光机, belt sander 带式砂光机, sand the wood smooth 把木头打磨光滑）。可数。
[例] ① He used an electric sander to smooth the tabletop. 他用电动砂光机把桌面磨平。 ② Wear a mask when operating a belt sander indoors. 室内用带式砂光机时要戴口罩。

[记] 可数：a sander。动词 sand；sander 指电动工具。手工打磨用 sandpaper

**chainsaw**
英/美 /ˈtʃeɪnsɔː/
n. 链锯
[义] A motor-driven saw with a chain carrying cutting teeth, used for cutting trees and thick wood. 链条带切削齿、由马达驱动的锯，用于砍树或锯厚木（operate a chainsaw 操作链锯, chainsaw safety gear 链锯防护装备）。可数。
[例] ① The crew used a chainsaw to clear fallen trees after the storm. 风暴后工人用链锯清理倒树。 ② Chainsaw training is required before you can use one on site. 现场使用链锯前须受训。

**soldering iron**
英/美 /ˈsɒldərɪŋ aɪən/
n. 电烙铁
[义] A tool with a heated metal tip, used to melt solder and join metal parts, especially in electronics. 加热金属头、用于熔化焊料连接金属部件的工具，尤用于电子维修（plug in the soldering iron 插上电烙铁, solder a wire joint 焊接线接头）。可数。
[例] ① He heated the soldering iron before joining the broken wire. 焊接断线前先加热电烙铁。 ② A fine-tip soldering iron works better for circuit boards. 细头电烙铁更适合电路板。

[记] 可数：a soldering iron。solder 动词/名词；与 welding（熔焊大件）不同，多用于小件电子

**plane**
英/美 /pleɪn/
n. 刨子；飞机；平面
v. 刨平；滑翔
[义] A woodworking tool with a blade for smoothing or shaping wood; an aircraft; a flat surface. 带刃、用于刨平或塑形木材的木工工具；飞机；平面（wood plane 木工刨, plane the edge smooth 把边缘刨平）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He planed the door edge until it fit the frame. 他把门框边刨到能嵌进框里。 ② Keep the plane blade sharp for clean shavings. 刨刃保持锋利，削出的木花才干净。
[搭] wood plane 木工刨, plane the edge smooth 把边缘刨平, hand plane 手刨, plane blade 刨刃
[联] shave 刨花, wood 木头, carpenter 木匠, smooth 磨平, blade 刃, workshop 车间, sander 砂光机
[记] 可数：a plane。本章取木工刨子义；aircraft 飞机、geometry 平面为另义。plane 动词亦指「滑翔、刨平」

**mallet**
英/美 /ˈmælɪt/
n. 木槌；槌棒
[义] A hammer with a large wooden or rubber head, used to hit things without damaging them. 大木或橡胶头、敲击时不易损坏物体的槌（rubber mallet 橡胶槌, tap with a mallet 用槌轻敲, croquet mallet 槌球棒）。可数。
[例] ① Tap the chisel gently with a rubber mallet. 用橡胶槌轻轻敲凿子。 ② She used a wooden mallet to crack the crab shells. 她用木槌敲开蟹壳。
[搭] rubber mallet 橡胶槌, tap with a mallet 用槌轻敲, wooden mallet 木槌, croquet mallet 槌球棒
[联] hammer 锤子, chisel 凿子, tap 轻敲, strike 敲击, wood 木头, tool 工具, carpentry 木工
[记] 可数：a mallet。比 hammer 冲击力柔和，适合敲凿子、组装家具

**rivet**
英/美 /ˈrɪvɪt/
n. 铆钉
v. 铆接；吸引
[义] A short metal pin or bolt for holding two metal plates together; to fasten with rivets; to fix someone's attention. 将两块金属板固定的短金属销；铆接；牢牢吸引（rivet gun 铆枪, rivet attention 吸引注意力〔喻〕）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The bridge joints are held together with steel rivets. 桥接处用钢铆钉固定。 ② Her speech riveted the audience for an hour. 她的演讲牢牢吸引了听众一小时。
[搭] rivet gun 铆枪, rivet attention 吸引注意力, steel rivet 钢铆钉, pop rivet 抽芯铆钉
[联] bolt 螺栓, nail 钉, fasten 固定, metal 金属, weld 焊接, joint 接缝, construction 建筑
[记] 可数：a rivet。rivet 动词 rivet attention 高频喻义「牢牢吸引」。pop rivet 家庭维修常见

**adhesive**
英/美 /ədˈhiːsɪv/
n. 黏合剂；胶带
adj. 黏性的
[义] A substance used for sticking objects together; able to stick. 用于粘合物体的物质；有黏性的（strong adhesive 强力胶, adhesive tape 胶带, apply adhesive evenly 均匀涂胶）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Let the adhesive dry for twenty minutes before moving the parts. 部件涂胶后等二十分钟再移动。 ② Use a removable adhesive for temporary labels. 临时标签用可移除黏合剂。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *adhaerere*（粘附），*ad-*（向）+ *haerere*（粘）。
[族] adhere 粘附, adhesion 黏附, adhesive tape 胶带
[搭] strong adhesive 强力胶, adhesive tape 胶带, apply adhesive evenly 均匀涂胶, industrial adhesive 工业胶
[联] glue 胶水, tape 胶带, stick 粘, bond 粘合, seal 密封, repair 修补, sticky 黏的
[记] 可数/形容词。adhesive tape 常指胶带；glue 更口语。adhere 动词较正式

**allen key**
英/美 /ˈælən kiː/
n. 内六角扳手
[义] An L-shaped or T-handled tool with a hexagonal end that fits socket screw heads. L 形或 T 柄、六角端插入内六角螺钉头的扳手（hex key 同义, tighten with an allen key 用内六角扳手拧紧, ball-end allen key 球头内六角）。可数。亦作 Allen key.
[例] ① Flat-pack furniture often comes with a tiny allen key for every cam lock. 平板家具常配小内六角，专门拧偏心轮。 ② A ball-end allen key reaches screws at a slight angle in tight spots. 球头款在窄位可略斜着够螺钉。
[搭] hex key 内六角扳手, tighten with an allen key 用内六角扳手拧紧, allen key set 内六角扳手套装, ball-end allen key 球头内六角, T-handle allen key T 柄内六角
[联] wrench 扳手, screw 螺丝, bolt 螺栓, furniture 家具, bike 自行车, assemble 组装, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 可数：an allen key。亦说 hex key / hex wrench；Allen 为品牌名泛指。公制与英制尺寸勿混用

**stepladder**
英/美 /ˈsteplædə(r)/
n. 折梯；梯凳
[义] A short ladder with flat steps and a hinged support, used for reaching heights indoors. 带平级踏板和铰链支撑的短梯，用于室内登高（folding stepladder 折叠梯凳, climb a stepladder 爬折梯）。可数。
[例] ① She climbed the stepladder to change the ceiling bulb. 她爬折梯换天花板灯泡。 ② Fold the stepladder flat before storing it in the closet. 折梯收进柜子前先折平。
[搭] folding stepladder 折叠梯凳, climb a stepladder 爬折梯, aluminum stepladder 铝制折梯, two-step stepladder 两级梯凳
[联] ladder 梯子, step 台阶, climb 爬, height 高度, fold 折叠, tool 工具, ceiling 天花板
[记] 可数：a stepladder。比 full ladder 短，适合室内换灯泡、挂画

**goggles**
英/美 /ˈɡɒɡlz/
n. 护目镜；泳镜
[义] Close-fitting glasses that protect the eyes from dust, chemicals, sparks, or water. 紧贴眼部、防尘化火花或防水的眼镜（safety goggles 护目镜, swimming goggles 泳镜, wear goggles 戴护目镜）。复数形式。
[例] ① Wear safety goggles when using the power sander. 用电动砂光机时要戴护目镜。 ② Chlorine in the pool stings if you forget your goggles. 忘带泳镜，泳池里的氯会刺激眼睛。
[搭] safety goggles 护目镜, swimming goggles 泳镜, wear goggles 戴护目镜, welding goggles 焊接护目镜, a pair of goggles 一副护目镜
[联] helmet 头盔, mask 面罩, protect 保护, eye 眼睛, safety 安全, workshop 车间, dust 灰尘
[记] 复数 a pair of goggles。化学飞溅、打磨粉尘宜戴护目镜；一般防屑可用 safety glasses

**hard hat**
英/美 /hɑːd hæt/
n. 安全帽；硬质帽
[义] A rigid protective helmet worn on building sites and in industrial workplaces. 建筑工地和工业场所佩戴的硬质防护头盔（wear a hard hat 戴安全帽, hard hat area 必须戴安全帽区域）。可数。
[例] ① Everyone on the construction site must wear a hard hat. 工地上每个人都必须戴安全帽。 ② His hard hat was dented when a tool fell from above. 上方掉下工具，把他的安全帽砸凹了。
[搭] wear a hard hat 戴安全帽, hard hat area 戴帽区域, construction hard hat 建筑安全帽, white hard hat 白色安全帽（监理等）
[联] helmet 头盔, safety 安全, construction 建筑, site 工地, protect 保护, goggles 护目镜, gear 装备
[记] 可数：a hard hat。hard hat 亦喻「现场工人、蓝领」（wear a hard hat 喻实干派）

**pruning shears**
英/美 /ˈpruːnɪŋ ʃɪəz/
n. 修枝剪；园艺剪
[义] A tool like strong scissors, used for cutting plant stems and small branches. 类似强力剪刀、用于剪植物茎秆和小树枝的工具（a pair of pruning shears 一把修枝剪, prune roses with shears 用修枝剪修剪玫瑰）。复数形式。
[例] ① Sharpen the pruning shears before the spring trimming. 春季修剪前把修枝剪磨快。 ② She clipped dead stems with pruning shears along the hedge. 她沿树篱用修枝剪剪掉枯枝。
[搭] a pair of pruning shears 一把修枝剪, prune roses with shears 用修枝剪修剪玫瑰, bypass pruning shears 旁路式修枝剪, sharpen shears 磨剪刀
[联] scissors 剪刀, garden 花园, prune 修剪, branch 树枝, hedge 树篱, plant 植物, rake 耙
[记] 复数 a pair of pruning shears。亦简称 shears / pruners。secateurs 英式同义

**wheelbarrow**
英/美 /ˈwiːlbærəʊ/
n. 独轮手推车
[义] A small cart with one wheel at the front and two handles, used for carrying loads in gardening or building work. 前轮单轮、双手柄的小车，用于园艺或建筑搬运（push a wheelbarrow 推独轮车, load the wheelbarrow 装车手推车）。可数。
[例] ① He wheeled a load of mulch across the yard in a wheelbarrow. 他用独轮车把一车覆盖物推过院子。 ② Balance the wheelbarrow so it doesn't tip when you turn. 转弯时稳住独轮车，别让它翻。
[搭] push a wheelbarrow 推独轮车, load the wheelbarrow 装车, garden wheelbarrow 园艺独轮车, heavy-duty wheelbarrow 重型独轮车
[联] cart 手推车, shovel 铲, garden 花园, haul 搬运, wheel 轮子, construction 建筑, load 装载
[记] 可数：a wheelbarrow。wheel + barrow（手推车）。与 barrow 单轮车同族

**hose**
英/美 /həʊz/
n. 软管；水管
v. 用软管冲洗
[义] A long flexible tube conveying water or other liquids; to spray or wash with a hose. 输送水或其他液体的长柔性管；用软管冲洗（garden hose 花园水管, hose down the patio 用水管冲洗露台, fire hose 消防水带）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She dragged the hose to the far corner of the garden. 她把水管拖到花园最远那头。 ② Hose the muddy wheels before you put the bike away. 收车前用水管冲掉轮子上的泥。
[搭] garden hose 花园水管, hose down the patio 用水管冲洗露台, fire hose 消防水带, hose nozzle 水管喷头, retractable hose 伸缩水管
[联] water 水, spray 喷洒, garden 花园, pipe 管道, nozzle 喷头, wash 冲洗, watering can 洒水壶
[记] 可数：a hose。hose down 冲洗。hose 亦指「紧身裤/长袜」（不同义）

**watering can**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtərɪŋ kæn/
n. 洒水壶；浇水壶
[义] A container with a handle and a spout with holes, used for pouring water on plants. 带柄、喷口带孔、用于给植物浇水的容器（fill the watering can 装满洒水壶, sprinkle water gently 轻轻洒水）。可数。
[例] ① The child filled a small watering can and tended the seedlings. 孩子装满小洒水壶照料幼苗。 ② A long-spout watering can reaches plants at the back of the bed. 长嘴洒水壶能浇到花床深处的植物。
[搭] fill the watering can 装满洒水壶, long-spout watering can 长嘴洒水壶, plastic watering can 塑料洒水壶, sprinkle water gently 轻轻洒水
[联] hose 水管, garden 花园, plant 植物, water 水, sprinkle 洒, seedling 幼苗, trowel 小铲
[记] 可数：a watering can。sprinkler 喷头/喷淋器为另一类浇水工具

**trowel**
英/美 /ˈtraʊəl/
n. 小铲；抹子
[义] A small hand tool with a flat blade, used for spreading mortar, digging small holes, or gardening. 小平刃手工具，用于抹灰、挖小坑或园艺（garden trowel 园艺小铲, planting trowel 种植铲, spread mortar with a trowel 用抹子抹灰）。可数。
[例] ① She used a trowel to dig holes for the bulbs. 她用园艺小铲给球茎挖坑。 ② The mason smoothed the cement with a flat trowel. 泥瓦工用平抹子把水泥抹平。
[搭] garden trowel 园艺小铲, planting trowel 种植铲, spread mortar with a trowel 用抹子抹灰, brick trowel 砌砖抹子
[联] shovel 铲, spade 锹, dig 挖, garden 花园, mortar 灰浆, plant 植物, hoe 锄
[记] 可数：a trowel。garden trowel 家庭园艺必备；brick trowel 建筑用

**plunger**
英/美 /ˈplʌndʒə(r)/
n. 马桶搋子；柱塞
[义] A tool with a rubber cup on a handle, used to unblock pipes by suction and pressure. 柄端带橡胶吸盘、靠吸力与压力疏通管道的工具（toilet plunger 马桶搋子, use a plunger on the drain 用搋子通下水道）。可数。
[例] ① The sink was blocked, so he reached for the plunger under the cabinet. 水槽堵了，他从柜子下拿出搋子。 ② Pump the plunger several times before flushing again. 再冲水前先抽压搋子几次。
[搭] toilet plunger 马桶搋子, use a plunger on the drain 用搋子通下水道, sink plunger 水槽搋子, flange plunger 凸缘式搋子
[联] unblock 疏通, drain 下水道, pipe 管道, suction 吸力, toilet 马桶, sink 水槽, repair 修理
[记] 可数：a plunger。plunge 动词「猛冲、投入」。厨房水槽与马桶搋子形状略有不同

**tweezers**
英/美 /ˈtwiːzəz/
n. 镊子；眉钳
[义] A small tool with two arms for picking up tiny objects or removing hairs. 两臂夹取微小物体或拔除毛发的小工具（a pair of tweezers 一把镊子, pluck eyebrows with tweezers 用眉钳修眉）。复数形式。
[例] ① She used tweezers to remove the splinter from his finger. 她用镊子把他手指里的刺挑出来。 ② Keep tweezers in the first-aid kit for small debris. 急救箱里放镊子，方便取小异物。
[搭] a pair of tweezers 一把镊子, pluck eyebrows with tweezers 用眉钳修眉, surgical tweezers 手术镊, precision tweezers 精密镊子
[联] pinch 捏, grip 夹持, splinter 刺, eyebrow 眉毛, precision 精密, tool 工具, first-aid 急救
[记] 复数 a pair of tweezers。动词 tweeze 少见，多用 pluck/remove with tweezers

**scraper**
英/美 /ˈskreɪpə(r)/
n. 刮刀；刮板
[义] A tool with a flat or curved blade for removing paint, ice, or residue from a surface. 平或弯刃、用于刮除漆面、冰或残留物的工具（paint scraper 刮漆刀, ice scraper 除冰铲, scrape off old paint 刮掉旧漆）。可数。
[例] ① He scraped ice from the windshield with a plastic scraper. 他用塑料除冰铲刮挡风玻璃上的冰。 ② Use a putty knife or scraper to lift the old wallpaper. 用腻子刀或刮刀揭旧墙纸。
[搭] paint scraper 刮漆刀, ice scraper 除冰铲, scrape off old paint 刮掉旧漆, floor scraper 地板刮刀
[联] scrape 刮, blade 刃, remove 去除, paint 漆, ice 冰, windshield 挡风玻璃, spatula 刮刀
[记] 可数：a scraper。ice scraper 冬季驾车常备。scrape 动词高频

**squeegee**
英/美 /ˈskwiːdʒiː/
n. 刮水器；橡胶刮板
[义] A tool with a rubber blade on a handle, used for wiping water from windows or smooth surfaces. 柄端橡胶刃、用于刮干窗户或光滑表面水分的工具（window squeegee 窗户刮水器, squeegee the shower door 刮淋浴门上的水）。可数。
[例] ① She ran a squeegee across the window after washing it. 洗完窗户后她用刮水器刮了一遍。 ② A shower squeegee keeps glass doors clear of soap scum. 淋浴间刮水器能让玻璃门少积皂垢。
[搭] window squeegee 窗户刮水器, squeegee the shower door 刮淋浴门上的水, rubber squeegee 橡胶刮板, windshield squeegee 挡风玻璃刮水器
[联] wipe 擦, window 窗户, rubber 橡胶, clean 清洁, blade 刃, sponge 海绵, mop 拖把
[记] 可数：a squeegee。亦用于洗车、清洁大面积玻璃。动词 squeegee

**razor**
英/美 /ˈreɪzə(r)/
n. 剃刀；剃须刀
[义] A tool with a sharp blade for shaving hair from the skin. 刃口锋利、用于剃除皮肤毛发工具（safety razor 安全剃刀, electric razor 电动剃须刀, razor blade 刀片）。可数。
[例] ① He replaced the razor blade before shaving. 剃须前他换了新刀片。 ② Electric razors are convenient for travel. 电动剃须刀旅行方便。
[搭] safety razor 安全剃刀, electric razor 电动剃须刀, razor blade 刀片, disposable razor 一次性剃刀, shave with a razor 用剃刀剃须
[联] blade 刀刃, shave 剃, sharp 锋利, cut 切, grooming 梳理, scissors 剪刀, trim 修剪
[记] 可数：a razor。razor-sharp 喻「极锋利」。razor 亦可指「剃刀般薄」的险崖（razor edge）

**jack**
英/美 /dʒæk/
n. 千斤顶；插座
v. 用千斤顶顶起
[义] A device for lifting heavy objects, especially vehicles; a socket or connector. 顶起重物的装置，尤指车辆；插座（car jack 汽车千斤顶, jack up the car 用千斤顶顶起汽车, hydraulic jack 液压千斤顶）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He jacked up the car to change the flat tire. 他用千斤顶把车顶起换瘪胎。 ② Always place the jack on solid ground before lifting. 顶车前务必把千斤顶放在坚实地面上。
[搭] car jack 汽车千斤顶, jack up the car 用千斤顶顶起汽车, hydraulic jack 液压千斤顶, bottle jack 瓶式千斤顶
[联] lift 抬起, tire 轮胎, repair 修理, vehicle 车辆, wrench 扳手, toolbox 工具箱, hydraulic 液压的
[记] 可数：a jack。jack up 亦喻「抬高价格」。本章取千斤顶义；plug jack 为电子插座义

**staple gun**
英/美 /ˈsteɪpl ɡʌn/
n. 钉枪；码钉枪
[义] A tool that drives heavy staples into wood, fabric, or other materials for fastening. 将粗订书钉打入木材、布料等以固定的工具（load staples into the gun 给钉枪装钉, staple fabric to the frame 用钉枪把布钉在框上）。可数。
[例] ① He used a staple gun to reattach the loose upholstery. 他用钉枪把松动的椅面布重新钉牢。 ② Wear goggles when firing a staple gun on a project. 用钉枪做项目时要戴护目镜。
[搭] load staples into the gun 给钉枪装钉, staple fabric to the frame 用钉枪把布钉在框上, electric staple gun 电动钉枪, heavy-duty staple gun 重型钉枪
[联] staple 订书钉, nail gun 钉枪, fasten 固定, wood 木头, upholstery 椅面, goggles 护目镜, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a staple gun。比 hand stapler 钉力更大，可钉入木头。nail gun 射钉枪类似

**nail gun**
英/美 /neɪl ɡʌn/
n. 钉枪；射钉枪
[义] A power tool that drives nails into wood or other materials quickly and forcefully. 快速有力地将钉子打入木材或其他材料的电动工具（pneumatic nail gun 气动钉枪, fire nails into the frame 向框架射钉）。可数。
[例] ① The crew used a nail gun to frame the walls in hours. 工人用钉枪几小时就立好了墙框。 ② Never point a nail gun at anyone, even when unloaded. 切勿将钉枪对准任何人，即使未装钉。
[搭] pneumatic nail gun 气动钉枪, fire nails into the frame 向框架射钉, electric nail gun 电动钉枪, nail gun safety 钉枪安全
[联] nail 钉子, hammer 锤子, staple gun 钉枪, power tool 电动工具, construction 建筑, wood 木头, fasten 固定
[记] 可数：a nail gun。高危工具，须培训与护具。比手锤效率高得多

**oven mitt**
英/美 /ˈʌvən mɪt/
n. 烤箱手套；防烫手套
[义] A thick padded glove worn to protect the hand when taking hot dishes from an oven. 从烤箱取热菜时佩戴的厚衬垫手套，防烫（pair of oven mitts 一副烤箱手套, slip on an oven mitt 戴上烤箱手套）。可数。
[例] ① Always use an oven mitt—the rack is hotter than you think. 务必戴烤箱手套，烤架比你想的更烫。 ② She pulled the tray out with both mitts and set it on the trivet. 她双手戴着手套把烤盘端出来，放在隔热垫上。
[搭] pair of oven mitts 一副烤箱手套, slip on an oven mitt 戴上烤箱手套, silicone oven mitt 硅胶防烫手套, heat-resistant mitt 耐热手套
[联] glove 手套, kitchen 厨房, hot 烫的, bake 烘焙, oven 烤箱, protect 保护, spatula 锅铲
[记] 可数：an oven mitt。亦说 oven glove。mitt 比 mitten 更短，常指单只厚手套

**pastry brush**
英/美 /ˈpeɪstri brʌʃ/
n. 油刷；糕点刷
[义] A small brush used to spread butter, egg wash, or sauce on food during cooking. 烹饪时涂抹黄油、蛋液或酱汁的小刷子（brush egg wash on the dough 在面团上刷蛋液, silicone pastry brush 硅胶油刷）。可数。
[例] ① Brush melted butter over the tops with a pastry brush. 用油刷在表面刷一层融化黄油。 ② A silicone pastry brush cleans up more easily than a bristle one. 硅胶油刷比毛刷刷更容易清洗。
[搭] brush egg wash on the dough 在面团上刷蛋液, silicone pastry brush 硅胶油刷, bristle pastry brush 毛刷油刷, basting brush 涂抹刷
[联] brush 刷子, bake 烘焙, butter 黄油, dough 面团, kitchen 厨房, spread 涂抹, glaze 糖釉
[记] 可数：a pastry brush。亦说 basting brush。勿与 paintbrush 混用

**potato masher**
英/美 /pəˈteɪtəʊ ˈmæʃə(r)/
n. 土豆捣碎器；压土豆器
[义] A kitchen tool with a flat or wavy metal plate on a handle, used to crush boiled potatoes. 柄端带扁平或波浪金属板、用于压碎煮熟土豆的厨房工具（mash potatoes smooth 把土豆压成泥, wire potato masher 钢丝土豆压器）。可数。
[例] ① Mash the potatoes until smooth with a potato masher. 用压土豆器把土豆压到顺滑。 ② A ricer makes fluffier mash than a traditional masher. 土豆压泥器压出的薯泥比传统压器更蓬松。
[搭] mash potatoes smooth 把土豆压成泥, wire potato masher 钢丝土豆压器, ricer 土豆压泥器, crush boiled potatoes 压熟土豆
[联] potato 土豆, mash 捣碎, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具, boil 煮, spoon 勺, colander 漏盆
[记] 可数：a potato masher。ricer 为另一类压泥工具，孔洞挤出

**ice cream scoop**
英/美 /ˈaɪs kriːm skuːp/
n. 冰淇淋勺；挖球器
[义] A spoon-like tool with a deep round bowl, used for serving ice cream in rounded portions. 深圆碗状、用于挖出圆球冰淇淋的勺形工具（scoop ice cream 挖冰淇淋, disher scoop 挖球勺）。可数。
[例] ① Dip the ice cream scoop in warm water before each scoop. 每挖一球前把冰淇淋勺浸一下温水。 ② Kids fought over who got to use the scoop at the party. 孩子们抢着聚会时用挖球勺。
[搭] scoop ice cream 挖冰淇淋, disher scoop 挖球勺, trigger scoop 触发式挖球勺, portion scoop 分量勺
[联] scoop 勺, spoon 勺, ice cream 冰淇淋, serve 盛, kitchen 厨房, ladle 汤勺, dessert 甜点
[记] 可数：an ice cream scoop。scoop 动词/名词「一勺」；亦用于挖甜瓜球等

**pizza cutter**
英/美 /ˈpiːtsə ˈkʌtə(r)/
n. 披萨滚刀；披萨刀
[义] A tool with a sharp rotating wheel on a handle, used to slice pizza. 柄端带锋利转轮、用于切披萨的工具（roll the pizza cutter 滚动披萨刀, wheel pizza cutter 轮式披萨刀）。可数。
[例] ① Roll the pizza cutter firmly across the pie in one motion. 把披萨滚刀一口气稳稳滚过整张饼。 ② A dull wheel makes the cutter skid instead of slice. 轮子钝了，刀会滑而不切。
[搭] roll the pizza cutter 滚动披萨刀, wheel pizza cutter 轮式披萨刀, sharp pizza cutter 锋利的披萨刀
[联] knife 刀, cut 切, blade 刃, pizza 披萨, wheel 轮子, kitchen 厨房, slice 切片
[记] 可数：a pizza cutter。亦说 pizza wheel。比普通刀更省力、切口更整齐

**pin**
英/美 /pɪn/
n. 别针；大头针；销钉
v. 别住；钉住
[义] A short thin piece of metal with a sharp point, used for fastening cloth, paper, or as a fastener; to hold with a pin. 尖端锋利、用于固定布料纸张或作紧固件的短金属件；用针别住（safety pin 安全别针, sewing pin 缝针, pin sth. to the wall 把某物钉在墙上）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She pinned the pattern to the fabric with sewing pins. 她用缝针把纸样别在布料上。 ② Keep pins in a cushion so they don't scatter. 把针插在针垫上，免得散落。
[搭] safety pin 安全别针, sewing pin 缝针, pin cushion 针垫, pin sth. to the wall 把某物钉在墙上, hairpin 发夹
[联] needle 针, thimble 顶针, thread 线, fasten 固定, sew 缝, clip 夹子, tack 图钉
[记] 可数：a pin。pin 多义：地图钉、销钉、胸针、发夹。语境区分

**safety pin**
英/美 /ˈseɪfti pɪn/
n. 安全别针；扣针
[义] A pin bent back on itself to form a spring and guard, used for fastening clothing or cloth. 弯回形成弹簧护罩的别针，用于固定衣物或布料（fasten with a safety pin 用安全别针固定, open a safety pin 打开安全别针）。可数。
[例] ① She safety-pinned the torn hem before the ceremony. 仪式前她用安全别针别住撕破的褶边。 ② Keep safety pins in the kit for quick clothing fixes. 急救包里放安全别针，方便临时修衣服。
[搭] fasten with a safety pin 用安全别针固定, open a safety pin 打开安全别针, large safety pin 大号安全别针, diaper pin 尿布别针
[联] pin 别针, fasten 固定, clip 夹子, sew 缝, clothing 衣物, emergency 应急, needle 针
[记] 可数：a safety pin。比 straight pin 多护罩，不易扎手。diaper pin 尿布别针较大

**thimble**
英/美 /ˈθɪmbl/
n. 顶针；指套
[义] A small hard cap worn on the finger to push the needle when sewing. 戴在手指上、缝纫时推针的小硬帽（wear a thimble 戴顶针, push the needle with a thimble 用顶针推针）。可数。
[例] ① A metal thimble protects your finger when you sew thick cloth. 缝厚布时金属顶针能保护手指。 ② She kept her grandmother's thimble in the sewing box. 她把祖母的顶针放在缝纫盒里。
[搭] wear a thimble 戴顶针, push the needle with a thimble 用顶针推针, metal thimble 金属顶针, sewing thimble 缝纫顶针
[联] needle 针, pin 别针, sew 缝, finger 手指, thread 线, sewing machine 缝纫机, stitch 针脚
[记] 可数：a thimble。thumb + -ble 原义与拇指相关。现代手工缝纫仍常用

**seam ripper**
英/美 /siːm ˈrɪpə(r)/
n. 拆线器；挑线器
[义] A small tool with a pointed hook used to cut and remove stitches. 带尖钩、用于割断并拆除缝线的小工具（rip out stitches 挑开缝线, use a seam ripper carefully 小心使用拆线器）。可数。
[例] ① She used a seam ripper to remove the old label inside the collar. 她用拆线器拆掉领内旧标签。 ② A sharp seam ripper saves time when altering hems. 改裤边时锋利的拆线器很省时。
[搭] rip out stitches 挑开缝线, use a seam ripper carefully 小心使用拆线器, sharp seam ripper 锋利的拆线器
[联] sew 缝, stitch 针脚, needle 针, thread 线, scissors 剪刀, alter 修改, hem 褶边
[记] 可数：a seam ripper。缝纫必备；钩刃锋利，勿对着皮肤

**sewing machine**
英/美 /ˈsəʊɪŋ məʃiːn/
n. 缝纫机
[义] A machine that stitches fabric together with thread, operated by hand or electricity. 用线缝合布料的手动或电动机器（electric sewing machine 电动缝纫机, thread the sewing machine 给缝纫机穿线, sew on a machine 用机器缝）。可数。
[例] ① She threaded the sewing machine and tested the tension. 她给缝纫机穿线并试了张力。 ② The old sewing machine still runs if you oil the parts. 老缝纫机上油后还能用。
[搭] electric sewing machine 电动缝纫机, thread the sewing machine 给缝纫机穿线, sew on a machine 用机器缝, portable sewing machine 便携缝纫机
[联] sew 缝, needle 针, thread 线, stitch 针脚, fabric 织物, iron 熨斗, thimble 顶针
[记] 可数：a sewing machine。thread the machine 穿线是关键步骤。家用多为 electric

**iron**
英/美 /ˈaɪən/
n. 熨斗；铁
v. 熨烫
[义] A device that heats up and is used to press clothes flat; the metal element iron. 加热后用于熨平衣物的装置；金属铁（steam iron 蒸汽熨斗, iron a shirt 熨衬衫, cast iron 铸铁）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She filled the steam iron and pressed the creases out of the shirt. 她给蒸汽熨斗加水，把衬衫褶痕熨平。 ② Iron the dress while it is still slightly damp. 裙子略湿时熨效果最好。
[搭] steam iron 蒸汽熨斗, iron a shirt 熨衬衫, cast iron 铸铁, ironing board 烫衣板, clothes iron 熨衣机
[联] press 压平, clothes 衣服, wrinkle 褶皱, steam 蒸汽, sew 缝, laundry 洗衣, garment 衣物
[记] 可数：an iron。本章取熨斗义；化学元素铁、铸铁另义。ironing board 烫衣板常配套

**protractor**
英/美 /prəˈtektə(r)/
n. 量角器
[义] A flat semicircular or rectangular tool marked with degrees, used for measuring and drawing angles. 标有度数、用于测量和画角的半圆或矩形平板工具（measure an angle with a protractor 用量角器量角, set square and protractor 三角板与量角器）。可数。
[例] ① Place the protractor on the vertex and read the angle. 把量角器中心对准顶点再读角度。 ② Students kept a protractor in their geometry kit. 学生几何套装里都放量角器。
[搭] measure an angle with a protractor 用量角器量角, semicircular protractor 半圆量角器, geometry kit 几何套装, degree mark 刻度
[联] angle 角, ruler 尺子, compass 圆规, measure 测量, geometry 几何, degree 度, draw 画
[记] 可数：a protractor。protract 动词「延长」；protractor 专指量角工具

**compass**
英/美 /ˈkʌmpəs/
n. 圆规；指南针
[义] A V-shaped drawing tool for circles and arcs; a device showing direction by magnetic north. 画圆与弧的 V 形绘图工具；靠磁北指示方向的装置（drawing compass 圆规, pair of compasses 一副圆规, magnetic compass 指南针）。可数。
[例] ① Draw a circle with a compass and mark the radius. 用圆规画圆并标出半径。 ② The hikers checked the compass when the trail forked. 小路分叉时徒步者查看了指南针。
[搭] drawing compass 圆规, pair of compasses 一副圆规, magnetic compass 指南针, compass needle 指南针指针, compass point 方位点

[记] 可数：a compass / a pair of compasses。绘图圆规与指南针同形，语境区分。needle 词条已列指南针指针义

**caliper**
英/美 /ˈkælɪpə(r)/
n. 卡尺；测径器
[义] A tool with two hinged legs or jaws for measuring thickness, diameter, or distance between surfaces. 两腿或两钳口铰接、用于测厚度、直径或两面间距的工具（vernier caliper 游标卡尺, measure with calipers 用卡尺测量）。可数。英式亦拼 calliper.
[例] ① The mechanic measured the brake disc with a vernier caliper. 技工用游标卡尺测量刹车盘。 ② Digital calipers display the reading on a small screen. 数显卡尺在小屏上显示读数。
[搭] vernier caliper 游标卡尺, measure with calipers 用卡尺测量, digital caliper 数显卡尺, outside caliper 外卡尺
[联] measure 测量, ruler 尺子, diameter 直径, thickness 厚度, precision 精密, tool 工具, mechanic 技工
[记] 可数：a caliper。亦拼 calliper。比 ruler 更精密，机械加工常用

**putty knife**
英/美 /ˈpʌti naɪf/
n. 腻子刀；油灰刀
[义] A flat flexible blade on a handle, used for applying or smoothing putty, filler, or paste. 柄端扁平柔韧刃、用于涂抹或抹平腻子、填料或膏体的工具（spread putty with a knife 用腻子刀抹腻子, flexible putty knife 软腻子刀）。可数。
[例] ① He smoothed the filler with a wide putty knife before sanding. 他用宽腻子刀抹平填料再打磨。 ② A flexible putty knife works better in corners. 软腻子刀在角落里更好用。

[记] 可数：a putty knife。亦统称 spackle knife。与 scraper 用途相近

**caulking gun**
英/美 /ˈkɒkɪŋ ɡʌn/
n. 胶枪；填缝枪
[义] A handheld tool that holds a tube of sealant and squeezes caulk through a nozzle when the trigger is pulled. 手持工具，夹持胶管、扣动扳机经喷嘴挤出填缝剂或密封胶（load the caulking gun 给胶枪装胶, cut the nozzle at 45 degrees 喷嘴斜切四十五度, smooth the bead 抹平胶条）。可数。亦作 caulk gun.
[例] ① Cut the tube nozzle at a 45-degree angle before loading the caulking gun. 装胶枪前把胶管嘴斜切四十五度。 ② Pull the trigger steadily so the bead width stays even along the seam. 扣扳机要匀，接缝处胶条宽度才一致。
[搭] load the caulking gun 给胶枪装胶, seal the gap with a caulking gun 用胶枪封缝, silicone caulk 硅胶密封胶, dripless caulking gun 防滴胶枪
[联] seal 密封, caulk 填缝剂, adhesive 黏合剂, gap 缝隙, window 窗户, bathroom 浴室, renovation 翻新
[记] 可数：a caulking gun。亦说 caulk gun。浴室、门窗、踢脚线填缝标配

**paint roller**
英/美 /peɪnt ˈrəʊlə(r)/
n. 油漆滚筒；滚刷
[义] A roller covered with absorbent material on a handle, used for applying paint to walls. 柄端覆吸水材料滚筒、用于在墙面涂漆的工具（roll paint on the wall 用滚刷涂墙, extension roller 加长滚刷）。可数。
[例] ① Roll the paint in a W pattern for even coverage. 用 W 形滚动涂漆，覆盖更均匀。 ② Rinse the roller sleeve thoroughly after each coat. 每遍漆后彻底冲洗滚筒套。
[搭] roll paint on the wall 用滚刷涂墙, extension roller 加长滚刷, roller sleeve 滚筒套, roller tray 滚漆盘
[联] paintbrush 漆刷, brush 刷子, paint 漆, wall 墙, coat 涂层, tray 盘, decorate 装饰
[记] 可数：a paint roller。大面积涂墙比刷子快；边角常配合 brush

**lawn mower**
英/美 /lɔːn ˈməʊə(r)/
n. 割草机
[义] A machine for cutting the grass on a lawn. 修剪草坪草木的机器（electric lawn mower 电动割草机, push the mower 推割草机, mow the lawn 割草坪）。可数。
[例] ① He mowed the lawn every Saturday with a push mower. 他每周六用推式割草机修草坪。 ② Empty the grass bag when the mower clogs. 割草机堵了就把草袋清空。
[搭] electric lawn mower 电动割草机, push the mower 推割草机, mow the lawn 割草坪, riding lawn mower 坐骑式割草机
[联] lawn 草坪, grass 草, rake 耙, garden 花园, trim 修剪, blade 刀片, hedge trimmer 修边机

**hedge trimmer**
英/美 /hed ˈtrɪmə(r)/
n. 修枝机；绿篱修剪机
[义] A tool or machine with blades for cutting and shaping hedges and shrubs. 带刃、用于修剪整形树篱与灌木的工具或机器（electric hedge trimmer 电动修枝机, trim the hedge 修剪树篱）。可数。
[例] ① Wear goggles and gloves when using an electric hedge trimmer. 用电动修枝机时要戴护目镜和手套。 ② The trimmer blades need sharpening after a long season. 用了一季后修枝机刀片该磨了。
[搭] electric hedge trimmer 电动修枝机, trim the hedge 修剪树篱, cordless hedge trimmer 无绳修枝机, hedge trimmer blades 修枝机刀片
[联] pruning shears 修枝剪, hedge 树篱, garden 花园, trim 修剪, blade 刀片, shrub 灌木, goggles 护目镜
[记] 可数：a hedge trimmer。大面积树篱用 trimmer；精细修剪用 shears

**garden fork**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːdn fɔːk/
n. 园艺叉；草叉
[义] A tool with a long handle and several prongs, used for digging and lifting soil or compost. 长柄多齿、用于挖土或叉起土壤堆肥的工具（dig with a garden fork 用园艺叉挖土, compost fork 堆肥叉）。可数。
[例] ① Loosen the soil with a garden fork before planting. 种植前用园艺叉松土。 ② She turned the compost pile with a sturdy fork. 她用结实的草叉翻动堆肥堆。
[搭] dig with a garden fork 用园艺叉挖土, compost fork 堆肥叉, digging fork 挖掘叉, turn the soil 翻土
[联] shovel 铲, spade 锹, rake 耙, garden 花园, soil 土, compost 堆肥, dig 挖
[记] 可数：a garden fork。比 spade 更适合松土、不压实。pitchfork 齿更长、更尖

**pitchfork**
英/美 /ˈpɪtʃfɔːk/
n. 干草叉；草耙
[义] A farm tool with a long handle and two or more long curved prongs, used for lifting and pitching hay or straw. 长柄、两或多个长弯齿、用于挑举干草稻草的农具（lift hay with a pitchfork 用干草叉挑草, pitchfork prongs 草叉齿）。可数。
[例] ① He pitched bales onto the wagon with a pitchfork. 他用干草叉把草捆叉上马车。 ② Keep the pitchfork tines sharp for easier penetration. 草叉尖保持锋利，刺入更轻松。
