[联] fork 叉, hay 干草, farm 农场, shovel 铲, barn 谷仓, lift 举起, garden fork 园艺叉
[记] 可数：a pitchfork。pitch 动词「投掷」；pitchfork 挑举用。民俗意象常关联农场

**pickaxe**
英/美 /ˈpɪkæks/
n. 鹤嘴锄；十字镐
[义] A large heavy tool with a long handle and a head with a point at one end and a blade at the other, used for breaking hard ground or rock. 长柄、一端尖一端刃的重型工具，用于凿硬土或岩石（swing a pickaxe 挥镐, break rock with a pickaxe 用镐凿岩）。可数。亦作 pickax.
[例] ① Miners once broke ore with pickaxes and shovels. 矿工曾用镐和铲破矿石。 ② The trail crew used a pickaxe to widen the rocky path. 护林队用十字镐拓宽岩石小径。
[搭] swing a pickaxe 挥镐, break rock with a pickaxe 用镐凿岩, pickaxe head 镐头, mining pickaxe 采矿镐
[联] axe 斧, shovel 铲, dig 挖, rock 岩石, mining 采矿, heavy 重的, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a pickaxe。pick + axe。比普通 shovel 更适合凿硬地

**work gloves**
英/美 /wɜːk ɡlʌvz/
n. 工作手套；劳保手套
[义] Strong gloves worn to protect the hands during manual work. 手工劳动时保护双手的结实手套（pair of work gloves 一副工作手套, wear work gloves 戴工作手套, leather work gloves 皮工作手套）。复数形式。
[例] ① Wear work gloves when handling splintery lumber. 搬带刺木料时戴工作手套。 ② The gloves were soaked with oil after the engine repair. 发动机修完后手套浸满了油。
[搭] pair of work gloves 一副工作手套, wear work gloves 戴工作手套, leather work gloves 皮工作手套, rubber-coated gloves 橡胶涂层手套
[联] glove 手套, protect 保护, safety 安全, hands 手, workshop 车间, construction 建筑, goggles 护目镜
[记] 复数 a pair of work gloves。比 dress gloves 厚、耐磨。与 oven mitt 用途不同

**dust mask**
英/美 /dʌst mæsk/
n. 防尘口罩
[义] A mask worn over the nose and mouth to filter dust, particles, or light fumes. 罩住口鼻、过滤粉尘颗粒或轻度烟尘的口罩（wear a dust mask 戴防尘口罩, N95 dust mask N95 防尘口罩）。可数。
[例] ① Sand the wood wearing a dust mask and goggles. 打磨木头时戴防尘口罩和护目镜。 ② Replace the dust mask when breathing becomes difficult. 呼吸费力时更换防尘口罩。
[搭] wear a dust mask 戴防尘口罩, N95 dust mask N95 防尘口罩, disposable dust mask 一次性防尘口罩, sanding mask 打磨口罩
[联] mask 口罩, goggles 护目镜, dust 灰尘, protect 保护, sand 打磨, safety 安全, respirator 呼吸器
[记] 可数：a dust mask。轻度防尘用 mask；有毒气体须用 respirator。打磨、清扫必备

**earmuffs**
英/美 /ˈɪəmʌfs/
n. 耳罩；防寒耳罩
[义] A pair of soft fabric or padded covers joined by a band over the head, worn to protect ears from cold or loud noise. 头顶系带连接的一对软布或衬垫罩，防寒或防噪音（wear earmuffs 戴耳罩, noise-canceling earmuffs 降噪耳罩）。复数形式。
[例] ① Children wore earmuffs on the snowy walk to school. 下雪天上学路上孩子们戴着耳罩。 ② Use hearing earmuffs when operating loud power tools. 操作嘈杂电动工具时戴隔音耳罩。
[搭] wear earmuffs 戴耳罩, noise-canceling earmuffs 降噪耳罩, winter earmuffs 防寒耳罩, hearing protection earmuffs 听力防护耳罩
[联] ear 耳朵, noise 噪音, protect 保护, cold 寒冷, goggles 护目镜, safety 安全, headphones 耳机
[记] 复数 earmuffs。防寒与工业隔音同形；轻便降噪用 earplugs，高噪声作业耳罩更稳

**awl**
英/美 /ɔːl/
n. 锥子；鞋锥
[义] A pointed tool for making holes in leather, wood, or other materials by pushing or twisting. 尖头工具，通过推或拧在皮革、木头等材料上打孔（punch holes with an awl 用锥子打孔, leather awl 皮革锥）。可数。
[例] ① He marked stitch holes with an awl before sewing the leather. 缝皮革前他用锥子标出针孔位。 ② An awl leaves a smaller hole than a drill bit. 锥子留下的孔比钻头小。
[搭] punch holes with an awl 用锥子打孔, leather awl 皮革锥, scratch awl 划针, sewing awl 缝纫锥
[联] pierce 刺穿, leather 皮革, hole 孔, needle 针, drill 钻, tool 工具, punch 打孔
[记] 可数：an awl。鞋匠、皮具工常用。与 drill 相比更手工、孔更小

**wedge**
英/美 /wedʒ/
n. 楔子；楔形物
v. 楔入；挤进
[义] A piece of wood, metal, or rubber tapered to a thin edge, driven between objects to split, tighten, or hold them; to fix tightly with a wedge. 削成薄边的木金属或橡胶块，楔入物体间以劈开、收紧或固定；用楔子楔紧（door wedge 门楔, wedge sth. in place 用楔子固定, wooden wedge 木楔）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Slide a wedge under the door to keep it open. 在门下塞楔子把门撑开。 ② He wedged the log before splitting it with an axe. 他用斧劈原木前先楔入楔子。
[搭] door wedge 门楔, wedge sth. in place 用楔子固定, wooden wedge 木楔, wedge-shaped 楔形的
[联] split 劈开, axe 斧, fix 固定, tighten 收紧, shim 垫片, tool 工具, hold 固定
[记] 可数：a wedge。wedge 动词 wedge sth. in 表「挤进、塞紧」。几何楔形亦同词

**socket wrench**
英/美 /ˈsɒkɪt rentʃ/
n. 套筒扳手
[义] A wrench with a handle and interchangeable sockets that fit over nuts and bolts. 带手柄、可换套筒以套在螺母螺栓上的扳手（ratchet socket wrench 棘轮套筒扳手, socket set 套筒组, tighten bolts with a socket wrench 用套筒扳手拧螺栓）。可数。
[例] ① A ratchet socket wrench speeds up work in tight spaces. 棘轮套筒扳手在狭小空间作业更快。 ② Match the socket size to the bolt head exactly. 套筒尺寸要与螺栓头完全匹配。
[搭] ratchet socket wrench 棘轮套筒扳手, socket set 套筒组, tighten bolts with a socket wrench 用套筒扳手拧螺栓, extension bar 加长杆
[联] wrench 扳手, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, allen key 内六角扳手, mechanic 技工, toolbox 工具箱, tighten 拧紧
[记] 可数：a socket wrench。socket set 常含多种尺寸套筒与棘轮手柄

**utility knife**
英/美 /juːˈtɪləti naɪf/
n. 美工刀；实用刀
[义] A knife with a retractable or replaceable blade, used for cutting cardboard, drywall, and other materials. 刀片可缩回或可更换、用于裁纸板石膏板等的刀（retractable utility knife 伸缩美工刀, replace the blade 更换刀片, cut cardboard 裁纸板）。可数。
[例] ① Score the drywall with a utility knife before snapping it. 掰石膏板前先用美工刀划道。 ② Retract the blade when you put the knife in your pocket. 装进口袋前把刀片缩回。
[搭] retractable utility knife 伸缩美工刀, replace the blade 更换刀片, cut cardboard 裁纸板, box cutter 开箱刀（近义）
[联] knife 刀, blade 刀刃, cut 切, sharp 锋利, cardboard 纸板, tool 工具, scissors 剪刀
[记] 可数：a utility knife。亦说 box cutter / Stanley knife（品牌泛指）。刀片锋利，须缩回保管

**rubber band**
英/美 /ˈrʌbə bænd/
n. 橡皮筋；橡胶圈
[义] A loop of elastic rubber used for holding things together. 弹性橡胶环，用于捆扎物品（stretch a rubber band 拉橡皮筋, bundle papers with rubber bands 用橡皮筋捆文件）。可数。
[例] ① She wrapped a rubber band around the rolled poster. 她用橡皮筋捆住卷起的海报。 ② Rubber bands lose elasticity if left in the sun. 橡皮筋暴晒会失去弹性。
[搭] stretch a rubber band 拉橡皮筋, bundle papers with rubber bands 用橡皮筋捆文件, elastic band 橡皮筋, rubber band ball 橡皮筋球
[联] elastic 弹性的, bind 捆, paper 纸, clip 夹子, fasten 固定, office 办公室, stretch 拉伸
[记] 可数：a rubber band。英式亦说 elastic band。勿过度拉伸老化

**washer**
英/美 /ˈwɒʃə(r)/
n. 垫圈；洗衣机
[义] A thin flat ring of metal, rubber, or plastic placed under a nut or bolt to spread pressure or prevent leakage; a machine for washing clothes. 置于螺母螺栓下以分散压力或防漏的薄平环；洗衣机（lock washer 防松垫圈, flat washer 平垫圈, bolt and washer 螺栓与垫圈）。可数。
[例] ① Always use a washer so the nut doesn't crush the wood. 务必加垫圈，免得螺母压坏木头。 ② A rubber washer seals the faucet joint against drips. 橡胶垫圈密封水龙头接头防滴漏。
[搭] lock washer 防松垫圈, flat washer 平垫圈, bolt and washer 螺栓与垫圈, rubber washer 橡胶垫圈, spring washer 弹簧垫圈
[联] bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, screw 螺丝, fasten 固定, seal 密封, metal 金属, leak 渗漏
[记] 可数：a washer。本章取垫圈义；washing machine 洗衣机另义。washer + bolt + nut 常成套

**cable tie**
英/美 /ˈkeɪbl taɪ/
n. 扎带；线缆扎带
[义] A narrow strip of plastic with a ratchet, used to bundle cables or fasten items together. 带棘扣的窄塑料条，用于捆扎线缆或固定物品（zip tie 同义, secure cables with ties 用扎带固定线缆, cut the tie tail 剪掉扎带尾）。可数。
[例] ① Bundle the cords behind the desk with cable ties. 用扎带把桌后线缆捆好。 ② Snip the excess tail after pulling the tie tight. 扎紧后剪掉多余尾端。
[搭] zip tie 扎带, secure cables with ties 用扎带固定线缆, cut the tie tail 剪掉扎带尾, reusable cable tie 可重复扎带
[联] tie 系, cable 线缆, wire 电线, fasten 固定, bundle 捆扎, plastic 塑料, clip 夹子
[记] 可数：a cable tie。亦说 zip tie / tie wrap。一次性居多；有可松开款

**extension cord**
英/美 /ɪkˈstenʃn kɔːd/
n. 延长线；插线板线
[义] An electric cable that extends the reach of a power socket to a distant appliance or tool. 将电源插座延伸到远处电器或工具的电缆（heavy-duty extension cord 重型延长线, plug into an extension cord 插到延长线上）。可数。
[例] ① Run an extension cord from the garage outlet to the yard. 从车库插座拉延长线到院子。 ② Check the cord rating before plugging in a space heater. 插暖炉前先看线缆额定功率。
[搭] heavy-duty extension cord 重型延长线, plug into an extension cord 插到延长线上, outdoor extension cord 户外延长线, cord length 线长
[联] plug 插头, socket 插座, power 电力, cable 电缆, electric 电动的, tool 工具, outlet 插座
[记] 可数：an extension cord。勿超载；户外用须防雨款。英式 extension lead 同义

**dustpan**
英/美 /ˈdʌstpæn/
n. 簸箕
[义] A flat pan with a raised edge, used with a brush to sweep dust and dirt into for disposal. 带立边的扁平盘，与刷子配合把灰尘扫入倒掉（dustpan and brush 簸箕和刷子, sweep into the dustpan 扫进簸箕）。可数。
[例] ① She swept the crumbs into a dustpan and emptied them in the bin. 她把碎屑扫进簸箕再倒进垃圾桶。 ② A long-handled dustpan saves your back when cleaning floors. 长柄簸箕扫地时省腰。
[搭] dustpan and brush 簸箕和刷子, sweep into the dustpan 扫进簸箕, long-handled dustpan 长柄簸箕, handheld dustpan 手持簸箕
[联] broom 扫帚, brush 刷子, sweep 扫, dust 灰尘, clean 清洁, floor 地板, bin 垃圾桶
[记] 可数：a dustpan。常与 brush 成套。dust + pan

**magnifying glass**
英/美 /ˈmæɡnɪfaɪɪŋ ɡlæs/
n. 放大镜
[义] A lens that makes objects look larger when held over them. 置于物体上方使其看起来更大的透镜（look through a magnifying glass 透过放大镜看, handheld magnifying glass 手持放大镜）。可数。
[例] ① He examined the stamp through a magnifying glass. 他用放大镜察看邮票。 ② A magnifying glass helps read tiny serial numbers on tools. 放大镜有助于读工具上的小序列号。
[搭] look through a magnifying glass 透过放大镜看, handheld magnifying glass 手持放大镜, magnifying lens 放大镜片, jeweler's loupe 珠宝放大镜
[联] lens 透镜, examine 检查, enlarge 放大, glass 玻璃, inspect 检查, read 读, precision 精密
[记] 可数：a magnifying glass。magnify 动词。珠宝、集邮、细修常用

**sledgehammer**
英/美 /ˈsledʒhæmə(r)/
n. 大锤；长柄大锤
[义] A large heavy hammer with a long handle, used for breaking concrete or driving stakes. 长柄重型锤，用于破混凝土或打桩（swing a sledgehammer 挥大锤, break concrete 破混凝土）。可数。
[例] ① They used a sledgehammer to crack the old patio slab. 他们用大锤砸裂旧露台石板。 ② Wear gloves—vibration from a sledgehammer blisters hands. 戴手套，大锤震动容易磨出水泡。
[搭] swing a sledgehammer 挥大锤, break concrete 破混凝土, sledgehammer handle 大锤柄, heavy sledgehammer 重型大锤
[联] hammer 锤子, mallet 木槌, strike 敲击, concrete 混凝土, heavy 重的, demolition 拆除, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a sledgehammer。比 hammer 大得多。比喻 sledgehammer to crack a nut 小题大做

**hatchet**
英/美 /ˈhætʃɪt/
n. 短柄小斧；手斧
[义] A small axe with a short handle, used with one hand. 短柄、可单手使用的小斧（hatchet job 恶意抨击〔喻〕, bury the hatchet 和解〔喻〕）。可数。
[例] ① He split kindling with a hatchet by the campfire. 他在营火旁用手斧劈引火柴。 ② Keep a hatchet in the emergency kit for clearing brush. 应急包里放手斧，方便清理灌木。
[搭] hatchet job 恶意抨击, bury the hatchet 和解, camping hatchet 露营手斧, hatchet handle 手斧柄
[联] axe 斧, chop 砍, wood 木头, camp 露营, blade 刃, tool 工具, kindling 引火柴
[记] 可数：a hatchet。比 axe 小、单手可握。bury the hatchet 习语「化敌为友」

**clipboard**
英/美 /ˈklɪpbɔːd/
n. 写字板；夹板
[义] A small board with a clip at the top for holding papers while writing on them. 顶端带夹、写字时夹住纸张的小板（clip papers to a clipboard 把纸夹在写字板上, fill out a form on a clipboard 在夹板上填表）。可数。
[例] ① The inspector wrote notes on a clipboard during the site walk. 巡检员在现场走动时在写字板上记笔记。 ② Clip the checklist to a clipboard at the workshop door. 把检查表夹在车间门口的写字板上。
[搭] clip papers to a clipboard 把纸夹在写字板上, fill out a form on a clipboard 在夹板上填表, aluminum clipboard 铝制写字板
[联] clip 夹子, paper 纸, write 写, office 办公室, checklist 检查表, binder 活页夹, stationery 文具
[记] 可数：a clipboard。clip + board。工地、仓库巡检常用

**spoon**
英/美 /spuːn/
n. 勺子；匙
v. 用勺舀
[义] A tool with a small shallow bowl and a handle, used for eating, stirring, or serving food. 小浅碗加长柄，用于进食、搅拌或盛食物（tablespoon 大汤匙, teaspoon 茶匙, wooden spoon 木勺）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Stir the soup with a wooden spoon so it doesn't stick. 用木勺搅汤，免得粘锅。 ② She spooned sugar into her tea one teaspoon at a time. 她一勺一勺把糖加进茶里。
[搭] tablespoon 大汤匙, teaspoon 茶匙, wooden spoon 木勺, soup spoon 汤勺, spoon-feed 填鸭式灌输
[联] fork 叉, knife 刀, ladle 汤勺, stir 搅拌, eat 吃, utensil 用具, bowl 碗
[记] 可数：a spoon。spoon-feed 喻「过度照顾、填鸭」。born with a silver spoon 出身富贵

**fork**
英/美 /fɔːk/
n. 叉；餐叉；岔口
v. 叉；分叉
[义] A tool with a handle and pointed prongs, used for eating or lifting food; a place where something divides. 长柄带尖齿、用于进食或叉取食物；分岔处（knife and fork 刀叉, garden fork 园艺叉, tuning fork 音叉）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Place the knife and fork parallel when you finish eating. 吃完后把刀叉并排放好。 ② He forked hay into the stall with a pitchfork. 他用干草叉把草叉进畜栏。
[搭] knife and fork 刀叉, garden fork 园艺叉, tuning fork 音叉, salad fork 沙拉叉, fork in the road 岔路口
[联] spoon 勺, knife 刀, pitchfork 干草叉, prong 叉齿, eat 吃, utensil 用具, lift 举起
[记] 可数：a fork。本章侧重餐叉与园艺叉；fork in the road 喻人生岔路

**kitchen shears**
英/美 /ˈkɪtʃɪn ʃɪəz/
n. 厨房剪刀
[义] Strong scissors designed for kitchen tasks such as cutting poultry, herbs, or packaging. 为切禽类、香草或包装等厨房任务设计的强力剪刀（a pair of kitchen shears 一把厨房剪刀, snip herbs with shears 用剪刀剪香草）。复数形式。
[例] ① Kitchen shears cut through chicken bones more easily than ordinary scissors. 厨房剪刀比普通剪刀更容易剪断鸡骨。 ② Rinse and dry the shears after handling raw meat. 处理生肉后把剪刀冲洗擦干。
[搭] a pair of kitchen shears 一把厨房剪刀, snip herbs with shears 用剪刀剪香草, poultry shears 禽类剪, heavy-duty kitchen shears 重型厨房剪
[联] scissors 剪刀, knife 刀, cut 剪, poultry 禽类, herb 香草, kitchen 厨房, utensil 用具
[记] 复数 a pair of kitchen shears。比普通 scissors 更厚、更利。有的可拆 blade 清洗

**skewer**
英/美 /ˈskjuːə(r)/
n. 烤签；串肉扦
v. 用签串；刺穿
[义] A long pin of wood or metal pushed through pieces of food to hold them together for cooking. 穿过食物块以便一起烧烤的长木签或金属签；刺穿（metal skewer 金属烤签, kebab skewer 烤肉签, skewer the vegetables 把蔬菜串起来）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Thread the chicken and peppers onto skewers before grilling. 烧烤前把鸡肉和彩椒串到签子上。 ② Soak wooden skewers in water so they don't burn. 木签先泡水，免得烤焦。
[搭] metal skewer 金属烤签, kebab skewer 烤肉签, skewer the vegetables 把蔬菜串起来, bamboo skewer 竹签
[联] grill 烧烤, kebab 烤肉串, roast 烤, pin 别针, stick 棍, barbecue 烧烤, tongs 夹子
[记] 可数：a skewer。动词 skewer 亦喻「严厉批评、戳穿」。shish kebab 常用 skewer

**funnel**
英/美 /ˈfʌnl/
n. 漏斗
v. 通过漏斗注入；汇集
[义] A tube with a wide mouth at one end, used for pouring liquids or powders into a narrow opening. 一端宽口、用于把液体或粉末倒入窄口的管（pour oil through a funnel 通过漏斗倒油, filter funnel 过滤漏斗）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Use a funnel when you refill the car's washer fluid. 加玻璃水时用漏斗，免得洒出来。 ② She poured the flour through a funnel into the storage jar. 她用漏斗把面粉倒进储存罐。
[搭] pour oil through a funnel 通过漏斗倒油, filter funnel 过滤漏斗, plastic funnel 塑料漏斗, kitchen funnel 厨房漏斗
[联] pour 倒, liquid 液体, bottle 瓶, container 容器, filter 过滤, narrow 窄的, spill 洒出
[记] 可数：a funnel。动词 funnel 喻「使汇集、导入」（funnel money into 把资金导入）

**jigsaw**
英/美 /ˈdʒɪɡsɔː/
n. 曲线锯；拼图
[义] A power tool with a narrow blade that moves up and down for cutting curves in wood or metal; a puzzle of interlocking pieces. 窄刃上下运动、在木或金属上裁曲线的电动工具；拼图（electric jigsaw 电动曲线锯, cut a curve with a jigsaw 用曲线锯裁曲线）。可数。
[例] ① An electric jigsaw makes curved cuts faster than a coping saw. 电动曲线锯比弓锯裁曲线更快。 ② Clamp the board firmly before you start the jigsaw. 开锯前把板子夹牢。
[搭] electric jigsaw 电动曲线锯, cut a curve with a jigsaw 用曲线锯裁曲线, jigsaw blade 曲线锯条, orbital jigsaw 轨道曲线锯
[联] saw 锯, cut 切, blade 刀刃, curve 曲线, power tool 电动工具, wood 木头, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a jigsaw。本章取工具义；jigsaw puzzle 拼图为另义。换 blade 可切不同材料

**grinder**
英/美 /ˈɡraɪndə(r)/
n. 磨床；研磨机；角磨机
[义] A machine or power tool that rotates a wheel or disc to grind, sharpen, or cut hard materials. 带动砂轮或磨盘旋转以磨削、刃磨或切割硬材料的机器或电动工具（angle grinder 角磨机, bench grinder 台磨, coffee grinder 咖啡研磨机）。可数。
[例] ① Wear a face shield when using an angle grinder on steel. 用角磨机切钢材时要戴面罩。 ② He sharpened the chisel on a bench grinder. 他在台磨上把凿子磨快。
[搭] angle grinder 角磨机, bench grinder 台磨, coffee grinder 咖啡研磨机, grinding wheel 砂轮, meat grinder 绞肉机
[联] grind 磨, sander 砂光机, blade 刀刃, sharpen 磨快, metal 金属, power tool 电动工具, spark 火花
[记] 可数：a grinder。angle grinder 危险，须护具。coffee/meat grinder 为厨房义

**router**
英/美 /ˈruːtə(r)/
n. 木工铣刀；路由器
[义] A power tool that spins a bit to cut grooves, edges, or shapes in wood; a device that directs data on a network. 带动铣刀头在木材上开槽、修边或造型的电动工具；网络路由设备（wood router 木工铣机, router bit 铣刀头, route an edge 铣边）。可数。
[例] ① A router gives clean rounded edges on shelf boards. 铣机给搁板边缘做出圆润倒角。 ② Always clamp the workpiece before routing freehand. 徒手铣削前务必夹紧工件。
[搭] wood router 木工铣机, router bit 铣刀头, route an edge 铣边, plunge router 压入式铣机, trim router 修边铣机
[联] wood 木头, cut 切, groove 槽, edge 边缘, power tool 电动工具, workshop 车间, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a router。本章取木工工具义；network router 为另义，拼读相同

**impact driver**
英/美 /ˈɪmpækt ˈdraɪvə(r)/
n. 冲击起子；冲击钻
[义] A power tool that delivers strong rotational blows to drive screws and bolts with less wrist strain. 以强力旋转冲击拧入螺丝螺栓、减轻手腕负担的电动工具（cordless impact driver 无绳冲击起子, drive lag bolts 拧入大螺栓）。可数。
[例] ① An impact driver sank the deck screws in seconds. 冲击起子几秒就把甲板螺丝拧进去。 ② Use impact-rated bits so they don't shatter under torque. 用耐冲击批头，免得在高扭矩下碎裂。
[搭] cordless impact driver 无绳冲击起子, drive lag bolts 拧入大螺栓, impact driver bit 冲击批头, hex shank bit 六角柄批头
[联] drill 钻, screw 螺丝, screwdriver 螺丝刀, power tool 电动工具, bolt 螺栓, torque 扭矩, battery 电池
[记] 可数：an impact driver。比 drill 更适合大螺丝、长螺栓。与 impact wrench 相近

**circular saw**
英/美 /ˈsɜːkjələ sɔː/
n. 圆锯；电锯
[义] A power saw with a round toothed blade that spins to cut wood or other materials. 圆形齿刃旋转切割木材或其他材料的电锯（portable circular saw 手提圆锯, circular saw blade 圆锯片, rip cut 纵切）。可数。
[例] ① Guide the circular saw along the chalk line for a straight cut. 沿粉线引导圆锯，切口才直。 ② Unplug the saw before changing the blade. 换锯片前先拔掉电锯插头。
[搭] portable circular saw 手提圆锯, circular saw blade 圆锯片, rip cut 纵切, crosscut 横切, guard 护罩
[联] saw 锯, blade 刀刃, wood 木头, cut 切, power tool 电动工具, chainsaw 链锯, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a circular saw。比 chainsaw 更常用于裁板。须戴护目镜、注意 kickback

**tire iron**
英/美 /ˈtaɪə aɪən/
n. 轮胎撬棍；换胎工具
[义] A metal lever used to remove a tire from a wheel or to pry off a hubcap. 用于把轮胎从轮毂撬下或撬下毂盖的金属杠杆（change a tire with a tire iron 用撬棍换胎, L-shaped tire iron L 形轮胎撬棍）。可数。英式 tyre iron.
[例] ① After loosening lug nuts with a lug wrench, he used the tire iron to pry the tire off the rim. 用轮胎扳手松螺母后，他用撬棍把轮胎从轮毂撬下。 ② Keep a tire iron in the trunk with the spare. 后备箱里和备胎一起放撬棍。
[搭] pry off the tire 撬下轮胎, L-shaped tire iron L 形轮胎撬棍, lug wrench 轮胎扳手, pry off the hubcap 撬下毂盖
[联] tire 轮胎, lug wrench 轮胎扳手, jack 千斤顶, car 汽车, lever 杠杆, repair 修理, wheel 轮子
[记] 可数：a tire iron。拧螺母用 lug wrench；撬胎、撬毂盖用 tire iron。英式 tyre iron

**torque wrench**
英/美 /tɔːk rentʃ/
n. 扭力扳手
[义] A wrench that measures and limits the force applied when tightening a nut or bolt. 拧紧螺母螺栓时测量并限制施力大小的扳手（set the torque wrench 设定扭力, tighten to spec 按规格拧紧）。可数。
[例] ① Set the torque wrench to the value in the manual before tightening wheel nuts. 紧轮毂螺母前把扭力扳手调到手册数值。 ② Overtightening can strip threads even with a torque wrench. 即使用扭力扳手，过紧也会拧花螺纹。
[搭] set the torque wrench 设定扭力, tighten to spec 按规格拧紧, click-type torque wrench 咔嗒式扭力扳手, digital torque wrench 数显扭力扳手
[联] wrench 扳手, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, tighten 拧紧, mechanic 技工, spec 规格, socket wrench 套筒扳手
[记] 可数：a torque wrench。咔嗒声或数显表示达到设定扭矩。汽车、自行车装配常用

**chalk line**
英/美 /tʃɔːk laɪn/
n. 粉线；墨线
[义] A cord coated with chalk, stretched tight and snapped against a surface to mark a straight line. 涂有粉笔的绳，拉紧后在表面弹出一道直线（snap a chalk line 弹粉线, chalk line reel 粉线轮）。可数。
[例] ① Snap a chalk line across the plywood before cutting. 锯胶合板前先弹条粉线。 ② The carpenter marked stud locations with a chalk line on the floor. 木匠在地板上弹粉线标出墙骨位置。
[搭] snap a chalk line 弹粉线, chalk line reel 粉线轮, blue chalk line 蓝色粉线, mark a straight line 标直线
[联] chalk 粉笔, line 线, measure 测量, straight 直的, carpenter 木匠, cut 切, mark 标记
[记] 可数：a chalk line。snap the line 快速弹线。线轮收纳见 chalk reel

**plumb bob**
英/美 /plʌm bɒb/
n. 铅垂；线坠
[义] A pointed weight on a string used to establish a true vertical line; the weight itself or the whole plumb line assembly. 系在绳上的坠重，用于拉出真实垂直线；可指坠子本身，亦可指整套铅垂线（hang a plumb bob 挂铅垂, check vertical with a plumb bob 用铅垂查垂直, plumb line 铅垂线）。可数。
[例] ① The mason checked the wall with a plumb bob before laying the next course. 砌下一层前泥瓦工用铅垂检查墙面。 ② Snap a plumb line from the bob before setting the door jamb. 装门框前先弹铅垂线定垂直。
[搭] hang a plumb bob 挂铅垂, check vertical with a plumb bob 用铅垂查垂直, plumb line 铅垂线, brass plumb bob 黄铜线坠
[联] vertical 垂直, level 水平, mason 泥瓦工, wall 墙, chalk line 粉线, measure 测量, construction 建筑
[记] 可数：a plumb bob。整条垂绳亦称 plumb line。激光水平仪没电时仍可用

**generator**
英/美 /ˈdʒenəreɪtə(r)/
n. 发电机；发生器
[义] A machine that converts fuel or motion into electricity; a device that produces something. 将燃料或运动转化为电的机器；产生某物的装置（portable generator 便携发电机, backup generator 备用发电机, start the generator 启动发电机）。可数。
[例] ① They ran a portable generator during the blackout to keep the fridge cold. 停电期间他们用便携发电机给冰箱供电。 ② Never operate a generator indoors—carbon monoxide kills. 切勿在室内使用发电机，一氧化碳致命。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *generare*（产生），*genus*（种类、出生）→ 产生电或动力的装置。
[族] generate 产生, generation 一代, generic 通用的, engine 发动机
[搭] portable generator 便携发电机, backup generator 备用发电机, start the generator 启动发电机, gas generator 燃气发电机
[联] power 电力, electricity 电, engine 发动机, fuel 燃料, outage 停电, extension cord 延长线, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a generator。户外、应急常用。须通风、远离门窗

**multimeter**
英/美 /ˈmʌltɪmiːtə(r)/
n. 万用表
[义] An electronic instrument that measures voltage, current, and resistance. 测量电压、电流和电阻的电子仪器（digital multimeter 数字万用表, test voltage with a multimeter 用万用表测电压）。可数。
[例] ① Check the battery voltage with a multimeter before replacing the part. 换零件前用万用表测电池电压。 ② Set the multimeter to the correct range so you don't blow a fuse. 把万用表调到正确量程，免得烧保险丝。
[搭] digital multimeter 数字万用表, test voltage with a multimeter 用万用表测电压, measure resistance 测电阻, continuity test 通断测试
[联] voltage 电压, current 电流, measure 测量, circuit 电路, electrician 电工, battery 电池, test 测试
[记] 可数：a multimeter。multi- + meter。家用维修、汽车电路排查常用

**power strip**
英/美 /ˈpaʊə strɪp/
n. 插线板；排插
[义] A block of electrical sockets on a cord, allowing several devices to share one wall outlet. 带线的多孔插座块，让多台设备共用一个墙插（surge-protected power strip 防浪涌插线板, plug into a power strip 插到插线板上）。可数。
[例] ① Don't overload the power strip with space heaters and kettles. 别让暖炉和电水壶一起把插线板超载。 ② Label the power strip behind the workbench so you know what to switch off. 给工作台后的插线板贴标签，方便关断。
[搭] surge-protected power strip 防浪涌插线板, plug into a power strip 插到插线板上, extension strip 排插, USB power strip 带 USB 排插
[联] plug 插头, socket 插座, outlet 插座, extension cord 延长线, electricity 电, overload 超载, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a power strip。带 surge protection 可保护精密设备。英式 extension lead/block

**battery charger**
英/美 /ˈbætəri tʃɑːdʒə(r)/
n. 电池充电器
[义] A device that restores charge to rechargeable batteries. 为可充电电池恢复电量的设备（phone battery charger 手机充电器, car battery charger 汽车电瓶充电器, plug in the charger 插上充电器）。可数。
[例] ① Leave the drill battery on the charger overnight. 把电钻电池放充电器上过夜。 ② A trickle charger keeps a stored car battery from dying. 涓流充电器能防止存放中的汽车电瓶亏电。
[搭] phone battery charger 手机充电器, car battery charger 汽车电瓶充电器, plug in the charger 插上充电器, wireless charger 无线充电器
[联] battery 电池, charge 充电, power 电力, cordless 无绳的, electric 电动的, plug 插头, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a battery charger。无绳工具须用配套充电器。勿混用不兼容充电器

**lint roller**
英/美 /lɪnt ˈrəʊlə(r)/
n. 粘毛滚筒
[义] A roll of sticky paper on a handle, used to remove lint, hair, and dust from clothing or fabric. 柄上粘性纸卷，用于去除衣物或织物上的 lint、毛发和灰尘（roll a lint roller over the coat 用粘毛滚筒滚大衣, refill lint roller 更换粘毛纸卷）。可数。
[例] ① She ran a lint roller over her black jacket before the interview. 面试前她用粘毛滚筒滚了一遍黑夹克。 ② Keep a lint roller by the door for pet hair on clothes. 门边放粘毛滚筒，方便粘衣服上的宠物毛。
[搭] roll a lint roller over the coat 用粘毛滚筒滚大衣, refill lint roller 更换粘毛纸卷, sticky lint roller 粘性粘毛滚筒, travel-size lint roller 旅行装粘毛滚筒
[联] lint 线头, hair 毛发, clean 清洁, fabric 织物, clothes 衣服, pet 宠物, brush 刷子
[记] 可数：a lint roller。纸卷用尽可 refill。出门前滚一遍很实用

**string trimmer**
英/美 /strɪŋ ˈtrɪmə(r)/
n. 打草机；割草绳机
[义] A garden tool that cuts grass and weeds with a rotating nylon string or blade. 以旋转尼龙绳或刀片割草除杂草的园艺工具（electric string trimmer 电动打草机, trim along the fence 沿篱笆修边, weed whacker 口语同义）。可数。
[例] ① He trimmed the grass along the walkway with a string trimmer. 他用打草机修过走道边的草。 ② Replace the trimmer line when it wears short. 割绳磨短了就更换。
[搭] electric string trimmer 电动打草机, trim along the fence 沿篱笆修边, weed whacker 打草机（口语）, trimmer line 割绳
[联] lawn mower 割草机, grass 草, weed 杂草, garden 花园, trim 修剪, hedge 树篱, blade 刀片

**pin cushion**

n. 针垫
[义] A small pad for storing pins by sticking them into it. 插针收纳用的小垫（tomato pin cushion 番茄形针垫, stick pins in a cushion 把针插在针垫上）。可数。亦作 pincushion.
[例] ① She pulled a needle from the pin cushion beside the sewing machine. 她从缝纫机旁的针垫上取针。 ② A wrist pin cushion keeps pins handy while tailoring. 腕式针垫做裁缝时随手可取针。
[搭] tomato pin cushion 番茄形针垫, stick pins in a cushion 把针插在针垫上, wrist pin cushion 腕式针垫, sewing pin cushion 缝纫针垫
[联] pin 别针, needle 针, sew 缝, thimble 顶针, sewing machine 缝纫机, thread 线, stitch 针脚
[记] 可数：a pin cushion。亦拼 pincushion。番茄形是经典造型

**knee pads**
英/美 /niː pædz/
n. 护膝
[义] Protective pads strapped over the knees to cushion impact during work or sport. 绑在膝上、缓冲撞击的防护垫（wear knee pads 戴护膝, gardening knee pads 园艺护膝, gel knee pads 凝胶护膝）。复数形式。
[例] ① Wear knee pads when you lay flooring all day. 铺地板干一整天要戴护膝。 ② The gel knee pads made weeding less painful. 凝胶护膝让除草没那么疼膝盖。
[搭] wear knee pads 戴护膝, gardening knee pads 园艺护膝, gel knee pads 凝胶护膝, strap-on knee pads 绑带护膝
[联] protect 保护, knee 膝盖, garden 花园, floor 地板, safety 安全, pad 垫子, work 工作
[记] 复数 knee pads。铺地板、园艺、轮滑常用。与 elbow pads 肘护配用

**apron**
英/美 /ˈeɪprən/
n. 围裙；停机坪
[义] A garment worn over the front of the body to protect clothes while cooking or working; an airport area for aircraft. 烹饪或劳动时罩在身前保护衣服的罩衣；机场停机坪（kitchen apron 厨房围裙, wear an apron 系围裙, leather work apron 皮工作围裙）。可数。
[例] ① Tie your apron before you start frying—the oil splatters. 油炸前系好围裙，油会溅。 ② The blacksmith wore a heavy leather apron at the forge. 铁匠在锻炉前戴着厚皮围裙。

**tape dispenser**
英/美 /teɪp dɪˈspensə(r)/
n. 胶带座；胶带切割器
[义] A device that holds a roll of adhesive tape and has a cutting edge for tearing off lengths. 夹持胶带卷并带刃口以便撕取长度的装置（office tape dispenser 办公胶带座, load a new roll 装入新胶带卷）。可数。
[例] ① She sealed the package with tape from the desk dispenser. 她用桌上胶带座的胶带封包裹。 ② The dispenser blade dulled after years of daily use. 胶带座刀片天天用，几年后钝了。

[记] 可数：a tape dispenser。packing 用大卷胶带常用手持 dispenser 或台式

**letter opener**
英/美 /ˈletə(r) ˈəʊpənə(r)/
n. 开信刀
[义] A blunt or slightly sharp knife used to slit open envelopes. 用于划开信封的钝刃或略锋利小刀（silver letter opener 银开信刀, open mail with a letter opener 用开信刀拆信）。可数。
[例] ① He slid the letter opener under the flap and lifted it cleanly. 他把开信刀滑进封口，干净地挑开。 ② A letter opener is safer than using scissors for envelopes. 拆信封用开信刀比剪刀更安全。

[记] 可数：a letter opener。亦说 paper knife。电子信时代仍见于正式办公

**padlock**
英/美 /ˈpædlɒk/
n. 挂锁
v. 用挂锁锁上
[义] A portable lock with a U-shaped bar, used to secure gates, lockers, and chains. 带 U 形锁梁的便携锁，用于锁大门、储物柜和链条（combination padlock 密码挂锁, padlock the gate 给大门上挂锁, cut a padlock 剪挂锁）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She padlocked the shed after putting the tools away. 工具收好后她给棚屋上了挂锁。 ② A rusted padlock resisted until he sprayed lubricant. 锈死的挂锁喷了润滑剂才打开。

**bar clamp**
英/美 /bɑː klæmp/
n. 杆夹；F 夹
[义] A clamp with long metal bars and a screw mechanism for holding wide workpieces during gluing or cutting. 长金属杆加螺杆机构、胶合或切割时夹持宽工件的工具（pipe clamp 管夹, tighten the bar clamp 拧紧杆夹, spread clamp 撑开夹）。可数。
[例] ① Glue the panel overnight between two bar clamps. 两块板涂胶后用两根杆夹夹住过夜。 ② Bar clamps reach farther across the table than C-clamps. 杆夹横跨桌面比 C 形夹更远。

**hacksaw**
英/美 /ˈhæksɔː/
n. 钢锯；弓锯
[义] A saw with a narrow fine-toothed blade held in a frame, used for cutting metal or plastic. 框内夹窄细齿刃、用于切割金属或塑料的锯（cut metal with a hacksaw 用钢锯切金属, hacksaw blade 钢锯条, replace the blade 更换锯条）。可数。
[例] ① He cut the bolt flush with a hacksaw after the nut seized. 螺母锈死后他用钢锯把螺栓锯平。 ② Install the hacksaw blade so teeth point forward. 装钢锯条时齿口应朝前。

[联] saw 锯, blade 锯条, metal 金属, cut 切, pipe 管, bolt 螺栓, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a hacksaw。比 wood saw 齿更细，专切硬材。blade 消耗品，常更换

**tin snips**
英/美 /tɪn snɪps/
n. 铁皮剪；金属剪
[义] Heavy scissors or shears for cutting thin sheet metal and other stiff materials. 剪薄金属板及其他硬材料的 heavy 剪刀或剪（aviation snips 航空剪, cut sheet metal 剪金属板, straight-cut snips 直剪）。复数形式。
[例] ① Use tin snips to trim the flashing around the vent. 用铁皮剪修剪通风口周围的挡水板。 ② Aviation snips leave a cleaner edge than ordinary scissors. 航空剪切口比普通剪刀整齐。

[记] 复数 tin snips。aviation snips 分 left/right/straight。剪金属比 scissors 省力

**paint tray**
英/美 /peɪnt treɪ/
n. 油漆盘；滚漆盘
[义] A shallow container for holding paint when using a roller or brush. 用滚筒或刷子蘸漆时盛放油漆的浅盘（pour paint into the tray 把漆倒进盘里, roller tray 滚漆盘, liner 一次性内衬）。可数。
[例] ① Pour only enough paint into the tray for one wall section. 盘里只倒够刷一面墙的漆量。 ② Slip a disposable liner into the tray for easy cleanup. 在盘里套一次性内衬，清理方便。

**paintbrush**
英/美 /ˈpeɪntbrʌʃ/
n. 漆刷；画笔
[义] A brush with bristles or synthetic fibers, used for applying paint or varnish. 鬃毛或合成纤维刷头、用于涂漆或清漆的刷子（wide paintbrush 宽漆刷, clean the paintbrush 清洗漆刷, bristle brush 鬃毛刷）。可数。
[例] ① Cut in the corners with a small paintbrush before rolling the wall. 滚墙前用小漆刷先把角落刷好。 ② Rinse the paintbrush in solvent before the paint dries. 漆干前用溶剂冲洗漆刷。

**toolkit**
英/美 /ˈtuːlkɪt/
n. 工具箱；工具包
[义] A set of tools in a case or bag for a particular job or trade. 为特定工作或行业配备、装在箱或袋中的一套工具（basic toolkit 基本工具包, software toolkit 软件工具包〔喻〕, emergency toolkit 应急工具包）。可数。
[例] ① The bike toolkit has everything needed to fix a flat tire. 自行车工具包里有补胎所需的一切。 ② Keep a small toolkit in the car for roadside repairs. 车里放个小工具包，路上坏车好修。

**cement mixer**
英/美 /sɪˈment ˈmɪksə(r)/
n. 混凝土搅拌机
[义] A machine that mixes cement, sand, gravel, and water into concrete. 将水泥、沙、砾石和水拌成混凝土的机器（portable cement mixer 便携搅拌机, mix a batch of concrete 拌一批混凝土）。可数。

**wire stripper**
英/美 /ˈwaɪə strɪpə(r)/
n. 剥线钳
[义] A tool for removing insulation from electric wire without cutting the conductor. 剥去电线绝缘层而不伤导体的工具（strip wire with a stripper 用剥线钳剥线, adjustable wire stripper 可调剥线钳）。可数。
[例] ① Match the stripper notch to the wire gauge before you squeeze. 挤压前先让剥线钳槽口对准线径。 ② A good wire stripper saves time when rewiring a lamp. 换台灯线时好的剥线钳很省时。

**pipe cutter**

n. 切管器；管子割刀

[例] ① He cut the copper pipe square with a pipe cutter for a clean joint. 他用切管器把铜管垂直切齐，接头更干净。 ② Rotate the cutter around the pipe several times before tightening. 拧紧前先绕管子转几圈切管器。

**laser level**
英/美 /ˈleɪzə levl/
n. 激光水平仪
[义] A tool that projects a straight laser line or cross onto a surface for alignment. 在表面投射直线或十字激光线、用于找平的仪器（self-leveling laser level 自平激光仪, shoot a laser line 打出激光线, cross-line laser 十字线激光仪）。可数。
[例] ① Shoot a level line across the wall before hanging the cabinets. 挂柜前先往墙上打一条水平线。 ② A self-leveling laser level saves time over a bubble level on long runs. 长距离找平时，自平激光仪比气泡水平仪省时。

[记] 可数：a laser level。电池耗尽时 plumb bob、spirit level 仍可用作备用

**spirit level**
英/美 /ˈspɪrɪt levl/
n. 水平仪；气泡水平尺
[义] A tool with a liquid-filled tube and an air bubble, used to check whether a surface is horizontal or vertical. 液封管内带气泡、用于检测表面是否水平或垂直的工具（torpedo spirit level 鱼雷式水平仪, check level with a spirit level 用水平仪找平, bubble level 气泡水平仪）。可数。
[例] ① Place the spirit level on the shelf and adjust until the bubble sits center. 把水平仪放在架子上，调到气泡居中。 ② A short torpedo level fits in a toolbox easily. 短鱼雷式水平仪容易放进工具箱。

[记] 可数：a spirit level。spirit 指管中「spirit 液」；与 level 词条中 spirit level 搭配呼应

**heat gun**
英/美 /hiːt ɡʌn/
n. 热风枪
[义] A power tool that blows hot air for stripping paint, shrinking tubing, or softening materials. 吹出热风、用于除漆、热缩管或软化材料的电动工具（strip paint with a heat gun 用热风枪除漆, heat-shrink tubing 热缩管, variable-temperature heat gun 可调温热风枪）。可数。
[例] ① Keep the heat gun moving so you don't scorch the wood. 热风枪要不断移动，免得烤焦木头。 ② She shrunk the wire labels with a low-temperature heat gun. 她用低温热风枪把线缆标签热缩好。

**blowtorch**

[例] ① He caramelized the crème brûlée top with a kitchen blowtorch. 他用厨房喷枪把布丁表面烧焦糖。 ② Wear goggles when using a blowtorch on copper pipes. 用喷灯焊铜管时要戴护目镜。

**C-clamp**

**dolly**

**utility cart**

**set square**
英/美 /set skweə(r)/
n. 三角板；直角三角尺
[义] A flat triangular plate with a right angle and marked angles, used for drawing and checking angles in drafting and carpentry. 带直角与角度刻度的扁三角板，用于绘图与木工标角（30-60-90 set square 三十四十五三角板, mark a 45-degree angle 标四十五度角, drafting set square 绘图三角板）。可数。
[例] ① Align the set square with the board edge before marking the miter. 标斜切角前先把三角板靠齐板边。 ② School set squares often come in 45° and 60° versions. 学生三角板常见四十五度与六十度两种。
[搭] 30-60-90 set square 三十四十五三角板, mark a 45-degree angle 标四十五度角, drafting set square 绘图三角板, adjustable set square 可调三角板
[联] triangle 三角形, angle 角, ruler 尺子, protractor 量角器, carpenter 木匠, draw 画, try square 检验直角尺
[记] 可数：a set square。英式常指绘图三角板；验 90° 多用 try square。勿与 speed square 混

**straightedge**
英/美 /ˈstreɪtedʒ/
n. 直尺；靠尺
[义] A rigid strip used to test or mark straight lines on a surface. 用于检测或标画表面直线的刚性长条（metal straightedge 金属靠尺, check straightness with a straightedge 用靠尺查直度, 4-foot straightedge 四英尺靠尺）。可数。
[例] ① Run a straightedge along the shelf to see if it bows. 用靠尺沿搁板划过去，看是否弯了。 ② Mark the cut line using a straightedge and pencil. 用靠尺和铅笔标切割线。
[搭] metal straightedge 金属靠尺, check straightness with a straightedge 用靠尺查直度, 4-foot straightedge 四英尺靠尺, aluminum straightedge 铝靠尺
[联] straight 直的, ruler 尺子, line 线, measure 测量, carpenter 木匠, level 水平, mark 标记
[记] 可数：a straightedge。比 flexible ruler 更硬、更准。 drywall 安装常用

**stud finder**
英/美 /stʌd ˈfaɪndə(r)/
n. 墙骨探测仪
[义] A device that locates wooden or metal studs behind a wall surface. 定位墙面后隐藏木骨或金属墙骨的装置（electronic stud finder 电子墙骨仪, scan the wall 扫描墙面, find a stud 找到墙骨）。可数。
[例] ① Scan the wall with a stud finder before drilling for the shelf brackets. 打搁板支架孔前先用墙骨仪扫墙。 ② The finder beeped when it passed over a vertical stud. 经过竖向墙骨时探测仪响了。
[搭] electronic stud finder 电子墙骨仪, scan the wall 扫描墙面, find a stud 找到墙骨, magnetic stud finder 磁性墙骨仪
[联] wall 墙, drill 钻, screw 螺丝, shelf 架子, mount 安装, construction 建筑, measure 测量
[记] 可数：a stud finder。挂重物须拧进 stud，不能只钉 drywall。电子款可测深度

**center punch**
英/美 /ˈsentə pʌntʃ/
n. 中心冲；样冲
[义] A pointed tool struck with a hammer to mark the center of a hole before drilling. 尖头工具，用锤敲击后在钻孔前标出中心点（mark the center with a punch 用样冲打点, automatic center punch 自动中心冲）。可数。
[例] ① Center-punch the mark so the drill bit doesn't wander. 先样冲打点，钻头才不会跑偏。 ② An automatic center punch works without a hammer. 自动中心冲不用锤也能打点。
[搭] mark the center with a punch 用样冲打点, automatic center punch 自动中心冲, center-punch the steel 在钢上打点
[联] punch 冲, drill 钻, awl 锥, hammer 锤, mark 标记, metal 金属, hole 孔
[记] 可数：a center punch。与 hole punch（打孔器）不同义。钻孔、冲孔前必备

**rasp**
英/美 /rɑːsp/
n. 木锉；粗锉
[义] A coarse file-like tool with raised teeth, used for shaping wood, stone, or hooves. 齿棱凸起、类似粗锉的工具，用于修形木材、石头或蹄（wood rasp 木锉, rasp the edge smooth 用粗锉修边, half-round rasp 半圆锉）。可数。
[例] ① He rasped the chair leg until it fit the mortise. 他用木锉把椅腿锉到能嵌进榫眼。 ② A rasp removes material faster than sandpaper on rough curves. 粗曲线上去料，粗锉比砂纸快。
[搭] wood rasp 木锉, rasp the edge smooth 用粗锉修边, half-round rasp 半圆锉, cabinet rasp 柜体锉
[联] file 锉, sandpaper 砂纸, shape 修形, wood 木头, smooth 磨平, carpenter 木匠, curve 曲线
[记] 可数：a rasp。比 file 齿更粗、去料更快。动词 rasp

**coping saw**
英/美 /ˈkəʊpɪŋ sɔː/
n. 弓锯；线锯
[义] A hand saw with a narrow blade in a U-shaped frame, used for cutting curves and fine joints. 窄锯条配 U 形框的手锯，用于裁曲线和精细接合（cut a coping joint 用弓锯做迭接, replace the coping saw blade 更换弓锯条）。可数。
[例] ① A coping saw follows tight curves better than a jigsaw on small trim. 小线条裁曲线，弓锯有时比曲线锯更稳。 ② He cut the crown moulding joint with a coping saw. 他用弓锯切顶角线迭接口。
[搭] cut a coping joint 用弓锯做迭接, replace the coping saw blade 更换弓锯条, fine-tooth coping saw 细齿弓锯
[联] saw 锯, jigsaw 曲线锯, blade 锯条, cut 切, curve 曲线, moulding 线条, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a coping saw。coping joint 为木工迭接术语。锯条可转向固定角度

**face shield**
英/美 /feɪs ʃiːld/
n. 面罩；防护面屏
[义] A clear visor worn over the face to protect against sparks, splashes, or debris. 罩在面部、防火花飞溅或碎屑的透明面屏（clear face shield 透明面罩, wear a face shield 戴面罩, grinding face shield 打磨面罩）。可数。
[例] ① Wear a face shield when grinding metal—the sparks fly upward. 磨金属时戴面罩，火花会往上飞。 ② The face shield fogged until he adjusted the ventilation holes. 他调整通风孔前面罩才不起雾。
[搭] clear face shield 透明面罩, wear a face shield 戴面罩, grinding face shield 打磨面罩, flip-up visor 上翻面屏
[联] goggles 护目镜, protect 保护, spark 火花, grind 磨, safety 安全, helmet 头盔, shield 盾
[记] 可数：a face shield。比 goggles 覆盖面更大；可与 hard hat 组合

**respirator**
英/美 /ˈrespəreɪtə(r)/
n. 呼吸器；防毒口罩
[义] A mask that filters harmful dust, fumes, or vapours from the air you breathe. 过滤吸入空气中有害粉尘、烟雾或蒸气的面罩（N95 respirator N95 呼吸器, wear a respirator 戴呼吸器, half-face respirator 半面式呼吸器）。可数。
[例] ① Wear a respirator when spraying paint in a closed garage. 在封闭车库喷漆时要戴呼吸器。 ② Dust masks are lighter, but a respirator seals better for fumes. 防尘口罩更轻，但防烟雾还是呼吸器密封更好。
[搭] N95 respirator N95 呼吸器, wear a respirator 戴呼吸器, half-face respirator 半面式呼吸器, replace the filter 更换滤片
[联] mask 口罩, dust mask 防尘口罩, fumes 烟雾, paint 漆, safety 安全, filter 过滤, protect 保护
[记] 可数：a respirator。dust mask 较轻；喷漆、溶剂须用带滤罐的 respirator

**tool belt**
英/美 /tuːl belt/
n. 工具腰带；工具袋
[义] A belt with loops and pouches for carrying tools while working. 带环和 pouch、便于作业时携带工具的腰带（leather tool belt 皮工具腰带, load the tool belt 往工具腰带装工具, carpenter's tool belt 木匠工具腰带）。可数。
[例] ① He clipped his hammer and tape measure to the tool belt. 他把锤子和卷尺别在工具腰带上。 ② A balanced tool belt saves trips back to the toolbox. 工具腰带配得均衡，少跑工具箱。
[搭] leather tool belt 皮工具腰带, load the tool belt 往工具腰带装工具, carpenter's tool belt 木匠工具腰带, nail pouch 钉袋
[联] tool 工具, belt 腰带, hammer 锤, pouch 袋, carpenter 木匠, carry 携带, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a tool belt。亦说 tool pouch / nail bag。电工、木匠、装修工常用

**wall anchor**
英/美 /wɔːl ˈæŋkə(r)/
n. 墙锚；膨胀螺栓
[义] A fastening device inserted into a wall to hold screws or hooks securely in drywall or masonry. 插入墙体、在石膏板或砌体中牢固固定螺丝或挂钩的紧固件（plastic wall anchor 塑料墙锚, drill for the anchor 为墙锚钻孔, toggle anchor 蝶形墙锚）。可数。
[例] ① Use the right wall anchor for the shelf weight—drywall alone won't hold. 按搁板重量选对墙锚，只钉石膏板挂不住。 ② She tapped the anchor flush before driving the screw. 她把墙锚敲平再拧螺丝。
[搭] plastic wall anchor 塑料墙锚, drill for the anchor 为墙锚钻孔, toggle anchor 蝶形墙锚, masonry anchor 砌体锚栓
[联] screw 螺丝, drill 钻, wall 墙, mount 安装, fasten 固定, stud 墙骨, shelf 架子
[记] 可数：a wall anchor。heavy load 须找 stud 或砌体锚。toggle anchor 承重大

**grease gun**
英/美 /ɡriːs ɡʌn/
n. 黄油枪；润滑枪
[义] A tool that applies grease under pressure through a nozzle onto bearings or fittings. 经喷嘴加压把润滑脂打到轴承或接头的工具（load the grease gun 给润滑枪装脂, grease the fitting 给接头打 grease, lever-action grease gun 杠杆式润滑枪）。可数。
[例] ① Pump the grease gun until fresh grease appears at the bearing seal. 压润滑枪直到轴承油封处冒出新脂。 ② Wipe the nozzle before storing the grease gun. 收枪前把喷嘴擦干净。
[搭] load the grease gun 给润滑枪装脂, grease the fitting 给接头打脂, lever-action grease gun 杠杆式润滑枪, grease cartridge 润滑脂筒
[联] grease 润滑脂, lubricate 润滑, bearing 轴承, mechanic 技工, car 汽车, tool 工具, oil 油
[记] 可数：a grease gun。cartridge 式较卫生。过度润滑也会损 bearing

**leaf blower**
英/美 /liːf ˈbləʊə(r)/
n. 吹叶机；落叶吹风机
[义] A power tool that blows air to move leaves and debris from lawns and paths. 吹气移动草坪和路面上落叶碎屑的电动工具（electric leaf blower 电动吹叶机, blow leaves into a pile 把落叶吹成堆, backpack blower 背负式吹叶机）。可数。
[例] ① He cleared the driveway with a leaf blower after the storm. 风暴后他用吹叶机清车道落叶。 ② Wear ear protection—the blower is louder than you expect. 戴耳塞，吹叶机比你想的响。
[搭] electric leaf blower 电动吹叶机, blow leaves into a pile 把落叶吹成堆, backpack blower 背负式吹叶机, cordless leaf blower 无绳吹叶机
[联] leaf 叶, rake 耙, lawn 草坪, garden 花园, blow 吹, debris 碎屑, earmuffs 耳罩
[记] 可数：a leaf blower。亦说 leaf vacuum（吸叶机）。早秋吹叶，邻居可能嫌吵

**lawn edger**
英/美 /lɔːn ˈedʒə(r)/
n. 修边机；草坪修边器
[义] A tool that cuts a neat edge between lawn and pavement or beds. 在草坪与路面或花床之间切出整齐边缘的工具（manual lawn edger 手动修边器, edge along the sidewalk 沿人行道修边, powered edger 电动修边机）。可数。
[例] ① Edge the border after mowing so the lawn looks crisp. 割草后修一遍边，草坪才利落。 ② A half-moon edger works well on soft soil borders. 半月形修边器在软土边沿好用。
[搭] manual lawn edger 手动修边器, edge along the sidewalk 沿人行道修边, powered edger 电动修边机, half-moon edger 半月修边器
[联] lawn mower 割草机, lawn 草坪, edge 边缘, trim 修剪, garden 花园, sidewalk 人行道, string trimmer 打草机
[记] 可数：a lawn edger。与 string trimmer 互补：edger 切硬边，trimmer 修草

**mandoline**
英/美 /ˈmændəlɪn/
n. 切片器；刨丝器
[义] A kitchen tool with an adjustable blade for slicing vegetables evenly and thinly. 可调刀片、用于把蔬菜切得均匀薄片的厨房工具（slice on a mandoline 用切片器切菜, mandoline guard 切片器护指, adjust the blade 调节刀片）。可数。
[例] ① Use the guard—mandoline blades are razor sharp. 务必用护指，切片器刀片极锋利。 ② She sliced potatoes paper-thin on the mandoline for chips. 她用切片器把土豆刨到纸片薄炸薯片。
[搭] slice on a mandoline 用切片器切菜, mandoline guard 切片器护指, adjust the blade 调节刀片, V-blade mandoline V 形刀片切片器
[联] slice 切片, knife 刀, grater 刨丝器, peeler 削皮器, kitchen 厨房, vegetable 蔬菜, sharp 锋利
[记] 可数：a mandoline。拼写亦 mandolin（与乐器同形）。须戴 guard，事故常见

**zester**
英/美 /ˈzestə(r)/
n. 刨皮器；柠檬刨
[义] A kitchen tool with small sharp holes for removing fine strips of citrus peel or grating spices. 带细锐孔、用于刨取柑橘皮细丝或磨香料的厨房工具（lemon zester 柠檬刨, zest an orange 刨橙皮, microplane zester 细刨 zester）。可数。
[例] ① Zest the lemon before juicing it for the glaze. 做糖釉前先刨柠檬皮再榨汁。 ② A zester adds aroma without bitter white pith. 刨皮器能增香而不带苦白瓤。
[搭] lemon zester 柠檬刨, zest an orange 刨橙皮, microplane zester 细刨 zester, channel knife 沟槽刀（近义）
[联] peel 削皮, citrus 柑橘, kitchen 厨房, grater 刨丝器, aroma 香气, lemon 柠檬, spice 香料
[记] 可数：a zester。zest 名词/动词「外果皮屑」。与 peeler 不同：zester 刨细丝

**ironing board**
英/美 /ˈaɪənɪŋ bɔːd/
n. 烫衣板
[义] A padded board, usually foldable, on which clothes are ironed. 通常可折叠的垫板，供熨烫衣物（folding ironing board 折叠烫衣板, set up the ironing board 支起烫衣板, adjustable height board 可调高度烫衣板）。可数。
[例] ① She set up the ironing board in the laundry room. 她在洗衣房支起烫衣板。 ② Pad the board cover when it wears thin. 烫衣板套磨薄了要加垫。
[搭] folding ironing board 折叠烫衣板, set up the ironing board 支起烫衣板, adjustable height board 可调高度烫衣板, board cover 烫衣板套
[联] iron 熨斗, press 压平, clothes 衣服, laundry 洗衣, wrinkle 褶皱, steam 蒸汽, garment 衣物
[记] 可数：an ironing board。与 iron 配套。height 可调款对腰更友好

**duct tape**
英/美 /dʌkt teɪp/
n. 强力胶带； duct 胶带
[义] Strong cloth-backed adhesive tape used for sealing, patching, and temporary repairs. 强韧布基胶带，用于密封、补丁和临时修理（patch with duct tape 用强力胶带补, silver duct tape 银色 duct 胶带, duct tape fix 胶带应急修〔口语〕）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① They duct-taped the cracked hose until a replacement arrived. 新管到货前他们用强力胶带缠住裂口。 ② Duct tape held the bumper on for one last drive. 强力胶带把保险杠绑住，又开了一程。
[搭] patch with duct tape 用强力胶带补, silver duct tape 银色 duct 胶带, duct tape fix 胶带应急修, waterproof duct tape 防水强力胶带
[联] tape 胶带, adhesive 黏合剂, repair 修理, seal 密封, patch 补丁, sticky 黏的, temporary 临时的
[记] 不可数/可数。口语万能修修补丁。原名 duck tape；现多写 duct。非专业 plumbing 用

**Velcro**
英/美 /ˈvelkrəʊ/
n. 魔术贴；尼龙搭扣
[义] A fastening system of two fabric strips that stick together when pressed, one with hooks and one with loops. 两片织物按压即粘合的紧固系统，一片钩、一片环（Velcro strap 魔术贴绑带, Velcro patch 魔术贴片, peel apart the Velcro 撕开魔术贴）。不可数/可数。商标名泛指。
[例] ① The toddler's shoes close with Velcro instead of laces. 幼儿的鞋用魔术贴代替鞋带。 ② Velcro cable ties tame desk wires neatly. 魔术贴理线带把桌面线理整齐。
[搭] Velcro strap 魔术贴绑带, Velcro patch 魔术贴片, peel apart the Velcro 撕开魔术贴, hook-and-loop 钩环搭扣
[联] fasten 固定, strap 绑带, hook 钩, loop 环, fabric 织物, cable tie 扎带, patch 贴片
[记] 商标泛指 hook-and-loop。动词 Velcro 亦常见。洗后可能积 lint、减粘

**wire cutter**
英/美 /ˈwaɪə ˈkʌtə(r)/
n. 钢丝钳；剪线钳
[义] Pliers designed to cut wire and metal cable cleanly. 专为干净剪断金属丝和缆线设计的钳（cut wire with cutters 用剪线钳剪线, flush-cut wire cutters 平口剪线钳, insulated cutters 绝缘剪线钳）。复数形式常指工具本身。
[例] ① Use wire cutters sized for the gauge you are cutting. 剪线钳尺寸要配线径。 ② Insulated cutters are mandatory on live circuits. 带电线路必须用绝缘剪线钳。
[搭] cut wire with cutters 用剪线钳剪线, flush-cut wire cutters 平口剪线钳, insulated cutters 绝缘剪线钳, diagonal cutters 斜口钳
[联] pliers 钳, wire 金属丝, cut 剪, wire stripper 剥线钳, electrician 电工, cable 缆线, tool 工具
[记] 可数/复数 wire cutters。与 wire stripper 不同：cutter 剪断，stripper 剥皮

**bolt cutter**
英/美 /bəʊlt ˈkʌtə(r)/
n. 断线钳；螺栓剪
[义] Large pliers with long handles for cutting through bolts, chain, or padlocks. 长柄大钳，用于剪断螺栓、链条或挂锁（cut a padlock shackle 剪挂锁环, heavy-duty bolt cutters 重型断线钳, compound-action cutters 复合杠杆断线钳）。可数。
[例] ① Bolt cutters made short work of the rusted chain. 断线钳几下就剪断锈链。 ② Keep bolt cutters in the shed for emergency fence repairs. 棚里放断线钳，围栏应急好修。
[搭] cut a padlock shackle 剪挂锁环, heavy-duty bolt cutters 重型断线钳, compound-action cutters 复合杠杆断线钳, 24-inch bolt cutters 24 英寸断线钳
[联] bolt 螺栓, cut 剪, chain 链, padlock 挂锁, pliers 钳, heavy 重的, tool 工具
[记] 可数：bolt cutters。杠杆长、剪力大。仅能剪较软金属，硬钢螺栓可能剪不动

**miter saw**
英/美 /ˈmaɪtə sɔː/
n. 斜切锯；切角机
[义] A power saw on a hinged arm that cuts accurate angles for moulding and framing. 铰臂式电锯，可精确切角，用于线条和框架（compound miter saw 复合斜切锯, miter a 45-degree cut 切 45 度角, miter saw blade 斜切锯片）。可数。
[例] ① Set the miter saw to forty-five degrees for the corner trim. 切角线条把斜切锯调到四十五度。 ② Clamp a stop block for repeated identical cuts. 夹挡块可重复切相同长度。
[搭] compound miter saw 复合斜切锯, miter a 45-degree cut 切 45 度角, miter saw blade 斜切锯片, sliding miter saw 滑动斜切锯
[联] saw 锯, angle 角, cut 切, moulding 线条, carpenter 木匠, power tool 电动工具, frame 框架
[记] 可数：a miter saw。英式 mitre saw。compound 款可 bevel + miter

**reciprocating saw**
英/美 /rɪˈsɪprəkeɪtɪŋ sɔː/
n. 往复锯；马刀锯
[义] A power saw with a blade that moves back and forth, used for demolition and rough cuts. 锯条往复运动、用于拆除和粗裁的电锯（recip saw 往复锯, demolition blade 拆除锯条, cut through nails 锯穿钉子）。可数。
[例] ① A reciprocating saw tore out the old window frame in minutes. 往复锯几分钟就拆下旧窗框。 ② Switch blades for metal versus wood demolition work. 拆金属和木头要换不同锯条。
[搭] recip saw 往复锯, demolition blade 拆除锯条, cut through nails 锯穿钉子, variable-speed recip saw 调速往复锯
[联] saw 锯, blade 锯条, cut 切, demolition 拆除, power tool 电动工具, jigsaw 曲线锯, construction 建筑
[记] 可数：a reciprocating saw。口语 recip saw。拆除、狭小空间利器

**crimping tool**
英/美 /ˈkrɪmpɪŋ tuːl/
n. 压接钳；压线钳
[义] A tool that compresses a connector onto a wire or seals a terminal. 把连接器压到导线上或压紧端子的工具（crimp a connector 压接端子, RJ45 crimping tool 网线压接钳, ratchet crimper 棘轮压线钳）。可数。
[例] ① Crimp the connector firmly and tug-test the wire. 把端子压紧，再拉线测试。 ② Match the die size to the connector you are crimping. 压模尺寸要配端子型号。
[搭] crimp a connector 压接端子, RJ45 crimping tool 网线压接钳, ratchet crimper 棘轮压线钳, wire ferrule crimper 线鼻压接钳
[联] wire 电线, pliers 钳, connector 连接器, electrician 电工, cable 线缆, network 网络, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a crimping tool。网线、端子、同轴接头常用。压不紧会接触不良

**T-square**
英/美 /ˈtiːskweə(r)/
n. T 形尺
[义] A T-shaped ruler used for drawing horizontal lines and checking right angles on drafting boards. T 形直尺，用于在绘图板上画水平线和检验直角（draw parallel lines 画平行线, align the T-square 对齐 T 形尺, drafting T-square 绘图 T 形尺）。可数。
[例] ① Align the T-square with the board edge before marking the line. 标线前先把 T 形尺靠齐板边。 ② Architects once relied on a T-square for every plan sheet. 建筑师画每张图都曾靠 T 形尺。
[搭] draw parallel lines 画平行线, align the T-square 对齐 T 形尺, drafting T-square 绘图 T 形尺, transparent edge 透明边 T 形尺
[联] ruler 尺子, straightedge 靠尺, set square 三角板, draw 画, angle 角, draft 绘图, measure 测量
[记] 可数：a T-square。数字绘图后少用，学校与手工制图仍见

**chalk reel**
英/美 /tʃɔːk riːl/
n. 粉线轮；墨线轮
[义] A case holding chalk-coated string that feeds out for snapping layout lines. 收纳涂粉绳、放出后用于弹线的线轮（load chalk into the reel 给线轮装粉, snap a line from the reel 用线轮弹线, chalk reel case 粉线轮盒）。可数。
[例] ① He rewound the chalk reel after snapping floor joist lines. 弹完地板搁栅线后他把粉线轮收回。 ② Blue chalk in the reel shows up clearly on plywood. 线轮里用蓝粉在胶合板上更显眼。
[搭] load chalk into the reel 给线轮装粉, snap a line from the reel 用线轮弹线, chalk reel case 粉线轮盒, replace the string 更换粉线
[联] chalk line 粉线, chalk 粉笔, carpenter 木匠, mark 标记, straight 直的, construction 建筑, measure 测量
[记] 可数：a chalk reel。与 chalk line 配套。粉线颜色可选，避免与后续工序混淆

**welding helmet**
英/美 /ˈweldɪŋ ˈhelmɪt/
n. 焊接面罩；焊工头盔
[义] A helmet with a dark visor that protects the face and eyes from welding sparks and UV light. 带深色面屏、防护面部与眼睛免受焊火花和紫外线的头盔（auto-darkening welding helmet 自动变光焊盔, wear a welding helmet 戴焊接面罩）。可数。
[例] ① Never strike an arc without a welding helmet and gloves. 没戴焊盔和手套绝不起弧。 ② The auto-darkening lens flipped dark the instant he started welding. 他一焊接，自动变光镜片立刻变暗。
[搭] auto-darkening welding helmet 自动变光焊盔, wear a welding helmet 戴焊接面罩, flip-up visor 上翻面屏, welding hood 焊工面罩
[联] goggles 护目镜, face shield 面罩, weld 焊接, spark 火花, safety 安全, glove 手套, blowtorch 喷灯
[记] 可数：a welding helmet。比护目镜更全面；弧光可灼伤角膜，必须佩戴

**welding torch**
英/美 /ˈweldɪŋ tɔːtʃ/
n. 焊枪；焊接炬
[义] A tool that produces a hot flame or electric arc to melt and join metal parts. 产生高温火焰或电弧以熔化并连接金属部件的工具（MIG welding torch MIG 焊枪, adjust the torch flame 调节焊枪火焰, oxy-acetylene torch 氧乙炔焊炬）。可数。
[例] ① He fed wire through the MIG torch at a steady pace. 他匀速送丝通过 MIG 焊枪。 ② Keep the welding torch angled correctly to avoid weak beads. 焊枪角度要对，焊缝才不虚。
[搭] MIG welding torch MIG 焊枪, adjust the torch flame 调节焊枪火焰, oxy-acetylene torch 氧乙炔焊炬, TIG torch TIG 焊炬
[联] weld 焊接, blowtorch 喷灯, soldering iron 电烙铁, metal 金属, spark 火花, flame 火焰, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a welding torch。与 blowtorch（小喷灯）不同，焊炬功率更高。须通风、防火

**sharpening stone**
英/美 /ˈʃɑːpnɪŋ stəʊn/
n. 磨刀石；油石
[义] A block of stone or synthetic abrasive used to sharpen blades and tools. 用于磨利刀刃和工具的石块或合成磨料块（wet sharpening stone 水磨石, hone a blade on a stone 在石上磨刃, oil stone 油石）。可数。
[例] ① Soak the sharpening stone before you hone the kitchen knives. 磨厨刀前先泡磨刀石。 ② A coarse stone shapes the edge; a fine stone finishes it. 粗石开刃，细石收锋。
[搭] wet sharpening stone 水磨石, hone a blade on a stone 在石上磨刃, oil stone 油石, diamond sharpening stone 金刚石磨刀石
[联] hone 磨, blade 刀刃, knife 刀, sharp 锋利, whetstone 油石, grind 磨, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a sharpening stone。亦说 whetstone / oil stone。粗细双面石很常见

**honing steel**
英/美 /ˈhəʊnɪŋ stil/
n. 磨刀棒；钢棒
[义] A long rod of hard steel used to realign and maintain a knife edge between sharpenings. 长硬钢棒，在磨刀之间矫正并维护刀刃（run the knife along the steel 沿钢棒磨刀, honing rod 磨刀棒, ceramic honing rod 陶瓷磨刀棒）。可数。
[例] ① Hone the chef's knife on the steel before service starts. 开餐前在钢棒上把主厨刀霍一下。 ② Honing straightens the edge; sharpening removes metal to reshape it. 霍刀扶正刃口；磨刀才去金属重塑。
[搭] run the knife along the steel 沿钢棒磨刀, honing rod 磨刀棒, ceramic honing rod 陶瓷磨刀棒, butcher's steel 屠夫磨刀棒
[联] knife 刀, sharp 锋利, sharpening stone 磨刀石, blade 刀刃, kitchen 厨房, edge 刃, maintain 维护
[记] 可数：a honing steel。霍刀不等于磨刀；日常维护用 steel，钝了才上 stone

**micrometer**
英/美 /maɪˈkrɒmɪtə(r)/
n. 千分尺；测微计
[义] A precision instrument for measuring very small thicknesses or diameters with a screw mechanism. 用螺旋机构测量极小厚度或直径的精密仪器（outside micrometer 外径千分尺, read the micrometer 读千分尺, digital micrometer 数显千分尺）。可数。
[例] ① Measure the shaft diameter with a micrometer to the nearest thousandth. 用千分尺把轴径量到千分之一英寸。 ② Wipe the micrometer faces before each reading. 每次读数前擦净千分尺测面。
[搭] outside micrometer 外径千分尺, read the micrometer 读千分尺, digital micrometer 数显千分尺, micrometer caliper 千分尺卡尺
[联] caliper 卡尺, measure 测量, precision 精密, diameter 直径, thickness 厚度, mechanic 技工, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a micrometer。比 caliper 更精密。micro- 指微米级读数

**pipe wrench**
英/美 /paɪp rentʃ/
n. 管钳；管子扳手
[义] An adjustable wrench with serrated jaws that grip round pipes and fittings. 锯齿钳口可调、夹持圆管与管件的扳手（adjustable pipe wrench 活动管钳, tighten a pipe fitting 拧紧管件, Stilson wrench 史迪森管钳）。可数。
[例] ① Use a pipe wrench on the fitting, not on the chrome fixture. 拧管件用管钳，别钳镀铬件。 ② The jaws left bite marks on the soft copper pipe. 钳口在软铜管上留下了咬痕。
[搭] adjustable pipe wrench 活动管钳, tighten a pipe fitting 拧紧管件, Stilson wrench 史迪森管钳, chain pipe wrench 链式管钳
[联] wrench 扳手, pliers 钳, pipe 管, plumbing 管道, tighten 拧紧, fitting 管件, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a pipe wrench。与 smooth jaw wrench 不同，专咬圆管。plumbing 必备

**flaring tool**
英/美 /ˈfleərɪŋ tuːl/
n. 扩口器；弯管器
[义] A tool that widens the end of a tube to form a flare for tight joints. 把管端扩宽成扩口形以便紧密接合的工具（flare copper tubing 扩口铜管, flaring tool kit 扩口器套装, double-flare tool 双扩口工具）。可数。
[例] ① Flare the brake line end before attaching the fitting. 装接头前先扩口刹车管端。 ② A clean flare prevents leaks under pressure. 扩口干净，承压才不漏。
[搭] flare copper tubing 扩口铜管, flaring tool kit 扩口器套装, double-flare tool 双扩口工具, tubing flare 管端扩口
[联] pipe 管, plumbing 管道, brake 刹车, flare 扩口, copper 铜, tool 工具, fitting 接头
[记] 可数：a flaring tool。汽车刹车管、空调铜管常用。与 swaging tool 不同

**trivet**
英/美 /ˈtrɪvɪt/
n. 锅垫；隔热架
[义] A small stand placed under a hot dish to protect a table or counter. 置于热锅下、保护桌面或台面的小支架（cast-iron trivet 铸铁锅垫, rest the pot on a trivet 把锅放在锅垫上, silicone trivet 硅胶锅垫）。可数。
[例] ① Set the roasting pan on a trivet so the table doesn't scorch. 烤盘放锅垫上，免得烫坏桌子。 ② Silicone trivets grip slippery glass lids well. 硅胶锅垫能稳住滑手的玻璃盖。
[搭] cast-iron trivet 铸铁锅垫, rest the pot on a trivet 把锅放在锅垫上, silicone trivet 硅胶锅垫, hot pot trivet 热锅垫
[联] pot 锅, hot 烫的, kitchen 厨房, table 桌子, protect 保护, oven mitt 烤箱手套, coaster 杯垫
[记] 可数：a trivet。比 coaster 大，承锅承盘。铸铁款可兼装饰

**knife block**
英/美 /naɪf blɒk/
n. 刀架；刀座
[义] A block or stand with slots to hold kitchen knives safely on a counter. 台面插放厨刀、带槽的块或架（wooden knife block 木制刀架, slot the knives in the block 把刀插进刀架, magnetic knife block 磁性刀架）。可数。
[例] ① Slide each knife into its slot in the knife block blades-down. 每把刀刃朝下插进刀架槽位。 ② A magnetic strip saves counter space compared to a bulky block. 磁性刀架比大块刀座省台面。
[搭] wooden knife block 木制刀架, slot the knives in the block 把刀插进刀架, magnetic knife block 磁性刀架, knife block set 刀架套装
[联] knife 刀, kitchen 厨房, blade 刀刃, sharp 锋利, counter 台面, utensil 用具, storage 收纳
[记] 可数：a knife block。刀须干燥再插，免得木槽发霉。磁性刀架亦常见

**first aid kit**
英/美 /fɪrst eɪd kɪt/
n. 急救箱；急救包
[义] A collection of medical supplies and basic tools for emergency treatment. 应急救治用医疗用品与基本工具的组合（stock a first aid kit 配备急救箱, workplace first aid kit 工作场所急救箱, refill the kit 补充急救包）。可数。
[例] ① Keep a first aid kit in the garage and another in the car. 车库和车里各放一套急救箱。 ② Check expiry dates when you refill the first aid kit each year. 每年补充急救箱时检查有效期。
[搭] stock a first aid kit 配备急救箱, workplace first aid kit 工作场所急救箱, refill the kit 补充急救包, bandage 绷带, tweezers 镊子
[联] bandage 绷带, tweezers 镊子, emergency 应急, safety 安全, workplace 职场, medicine 药品, scissors 剪刀
[记] 可数：a first aid kit。常含剪刀、镊子、胶带等小工具。工地、车间须按规定配备

**jumper cables**
英/美 /ˈdʒʌmpə keɪbəlz/
n. 跨接电缆；电瓶线
[义] Heavy insulated cables used to connect one vehicle battery to another to start a dead battery. 粗绝缘缆线，用于把车电瓶与另一电瓶跨接以启动亏电电池（connect jumper cables 连接电瓶线, red to positive 红线接正极, heavy-duty jumper cables 重型电瓶线）。复数形式。
[例] ① Connect the jumper cables in the correct order to avoid sparks. 按正确顺序接电瓶线，免得打火。 ② Keep jumper cables in the trunk with the emergency kit. 电瓶线和应急包一起放后备箱。
[搭] connect jumper cables 连接电瓶线, red to positive 红线接正极, heavy-duty jumper cables 重型电瓶线, clamp the cables 夹紧电缆
[联] battery 电池, car 汽车, cable 电缆, engine 发动机, emergency 应急, clamp 夹子, electricity 电
[记] 复数 jumper cables。亦说 jump leads（英式）。先正后负，拆时顺序相反

**oil pan**
英/美 /ɔɪl pæn/
n. 接油盘；废油盆
[义] A shallow pan placed under a vehicle or machine to catch draining oil during service. 保养时置于车或机器下方接废油的浅盘（drain oil into a pan 把油倒进接油盘, shallow oil pan 浅接油盘, disposable oil pan 一次性接油盆）。可数。
[例] ① Position the oil pan before you remove the drain plug. 拧放油螺塞前先放好接油盘。 ② Warm oil flows faster into the pan on a cold morning. 冷天早上热油流进盘里更快。
[搭] drain oil into a pan 把油倒进接油盘, shallow oil pan 浅接油盘, disposable oil pan 一次性接油盆, oil drain pan 放油盘
[联] oil 油, drain 排放, car 汽车, mechanic 技工, wrench 扳手, funnel 漏斗, garage 车库
[记] 可数：an oil pan。换机油必备。废油须送回收点，勿倒下水道

**spring clamp**
英/美 /sprɪŋ klæmp/
n. 弹簧夹；快夹
[义] A clamp with spring-loaded jaws that open and close quickly by hand. 弹簧钳口、可用手快速开合的夹子（spring clamp the workpiece 用弹簧夹夹工件, padded spring clamp 带垫弹簧夹, quick-grip spring clamp 快夹）。可数。
[例] ① Spring clamps hold glue-ups without reaching for screws. 胶合用弹簧夹，不必找螺丝。 ② Padded jaws on spring clamps protect finished surfaces. 弹簧夹带垫钳口能保护完工表面。
[搭] spring clamp the workpiece 用弹簧夹夹工件, padded spring clamp 带垫弹簧夹, quick-grip spring clamp 快夹, one-handed clamp 单手夹
[联] clamp 夹钳, C-clamp C 形夹, bar clamp 杆夹, glue 胶, hold 固定, quick 快的, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a spring clamp。比 C-clamp 快，但夹力较小。摄影、木工、胶合常用

**hand drill**
英/美 /hænd drɪl/
n. 手摇钻；手钻
[义] A manual tool with a crank or wheel that rotates a bit to drill holes without electricity. 带曲柄或手轮、不插电即可旋转钻头打孔的手动工具（vintage hand drill 老式手摇钻, crank the hand drill 摇动手钻, brace and bit 弓钻）。可数。
[例] ① A hand drill works when the power is out and you need one hole. 停电要打一个孔时，手摇钻还能用。 ② Brace-and-bit drills bore large holes in timber slowly but steadily. 弓钻在木材上慢而稳地钻大孔。
[搭] vintage hand drill 老式手摇钻, crank the hand drill 摇动手钻, brace and bit 弓钻, eggbeater drill 打蛋器式手钻
[联] drill 钻, bit 钻头, hole 孔, manual 手动的, power drill 电钻, wood 木头, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a hand drill。电钻普及后少见，但无电环境仍有用。brace and bit 为同类

**auger**
英/美 /ˈɒɡə(r)/
n. 螺旋钻；木钻
[义] A tool with a helical blade for boring holes in wood or soil. 螺旋刃、在木头或土里钻孔的工具（post auger 杆孔钻, drill with an auger 用螺旋钻钻孔, ice auger 冰钻）。可数。
[例] ① He bored fence-post holes with a manual auger. 他用手动螺旋钻打篱笆桩孔。 ② Auger bits pull themselves into wood as they cut. 螺旋钻钻头切削时会自行钻进木头。
[搭] post auger 杆孔钻, drill with an auger 用螺旋钻钻孔, ice auger 冰钻, auger bit 螺旋钻头
[联] drill 钻, hole 孔, wood 木头, soil 土, bit 钻头, post 桩, garden 花园
[记] 可数：an auger。大孔木工、冰钓、植桩常用。与 twist drill 不同

**post hole digger**
英/美 /pəʊst həʊl ˈdɪɡə(r)/
n. 挖坑器；杆孔挖掘机
[义] A tool with two hinged blades on a handle, used to dig narrow holes for fence posts. 长柄双铰刀片、用于挖篱笆桩窄坑的工具（dig post holes 挖桩孔, manual post hole digger 手动挖坑器, two-person digger 双人挖坑器）。可数。
[例] ① They dug each post hole two feet deep with a manual digger. 他们用手动挖坑器把每个桩坑挖两英尺深。 ② Wet clay makes a post hole digger harder to close and lift. 湿粘土让挖坑器更难合拢提起。
[搭] dig post holes 挖桩孔, manual post hole digger 手动挖坑器, two-person digger 双人挖坑器, clamshell digger 蛤壳式挖坑器
[联] auger 螺旋钻, shovel 铲, fence 篱笆, dig 挖, post 桩, garden 花园, hole 孔
[记] 可数：a post hole digger。亦说 post hole digger clamshell。比 shovel 挖圆坑更整齐

**pressure washer**

**basin wrench**

[义] A long-handled wrench with a spring-loaded jaw, used to tighten or remove nuts on faucets in tight spaces under sinks. 长柄、弹簧钳口，用于在水槽下方狭窄空间拧紧或拆卸水龙头螺母的扳手（adjust a faucet with a basin wrench 用水槽扳手调龙头, basin wrench jaw 水槽扳手钳口）。可数。
[例] ① A basin wrench reaches the supply nuts behind the pedestal sink. 水槽扳手够得到柱盆后面的进水螺母。 ② Lock the jaw on the nut before you turn the handle. 转动手柄前先把钳口卡死在螺母上。
[搭] adjust a faucet with a basin wrench 用水槽扳手调龙头, basin wrench jaw 水槽扳手钳口, telescoping basin wrench 伸缩水槽扳手, spring-loaded jaw 弹簧钳口
[联] wrench 扳手, faucet 水龙头, plumbing 管道, sink 水槽, tighten 拧紧, pipe 管, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a basin wrench。plumbing 专用；普通 wrench 往往够不到

**tube bender**
英/美 /tjuːb ˈbendə(r)/
n. 弯管器
[义] A tool that bends metal or plastic tubing to a set angle without kinking or collapsing the pipe. 把金属或塑料管弯成设定角度、又不压瘪管道的工具（bend copper tubing 弯铜管, tube bender die 弯管模, spring tube bender 弹簧弯管器）。可数。
[例] ① Use a tube bender so the brake line keeps a smooth radius. 用弯管器，刹车管才能保持圆滑弧度。 ② Match the bender size to the tubing diameter exactly. 弯管器尺寸要与管径完全匹配。
[搭] bend copper tubing 弯铜管, tube bender die 弯管模, spring tube bender 弹簧弯管器, hydraulic tube bender 液压弯管器
[联] pipe 管, bend 弯, copper 铜, plumbing 管道, flaring tool 扩口器, radius 弧度, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a tube bender。与 flaring tool 常配套用于 brake/fuel 线

**loppers**
英/美 /ˈlɒpəz/
n. 长柄剪；高枝剪
[义] Large pruning shears with long handles for cutting thick branches out of reach. 长柄大号修枝剪，用于剪够不着的高处粗枝（a pair of loppers 一把长柄剪, lop branches with loppers 用高枝剪修枝, bypass loppers 旁路式长柄剪）。复数形式。
[例] ① Loppers cut branches up to two inches thick when the blades are sharp. 刀片锋利时，长柄剪能剪两英寸粗枝。 ② Extend loppers fully so you don't strain your back reaching. 把长柄伸开再剪，免得探身伤腰。
[搭] a pair of loppers 一把长柄剪, lop branches with loppers 用高枝剪修枝, bypass loppers 旁路式长柄剪, anvil loppers 砧式长柄剪
[联] pruning shears 修枝剪, prune 修剪, branch 树枝, garden 花园, cut 剪, tree 树, hedge 树篱
[记] 复数 a pair of loppers。bypass 型切口更干净；anvil 型更省力

**pruning saw**
英/美 /ˈpruːnɪŋ sɔː/
n. 修枝锯
[义] A curved or straight saw designed for cutting live or dead branches on trees and shrubs. 专为修剪树木灌木活枝或枯枝设计的弯或直锯（folding pruning saw 折叠修枝锯, saw through a branch 锯断树枝, curved pruning saw 弯修枝锯）。可数。
[例] ① A pruning saw handles limbs too thick for loppers alone. 修枝锯能处理单靠长柄剪剪不动的枝条。 ② Clean sap from the blade after each pruning session. 每次修枝后把锯条上的树液擦干净。
[搭] folding pruning saw 折叠修枝锯, saw through a branch 锯断树枝, curved pruning saw 弯修枝锯, pole pruning saw 长杆修枝锯
[联] saw 锯, loppers 长柄剪, prune 修剪, branch 树枝, garden 花园, tree 树, blade 锯条
[记] 可数：a pruning saw。比 bow saw 更专用于活树修枝。折叠款便于携带

**pruning knife**
英/美 /ˈpruːnɪŋ naɪf/
n. 嫁接刀；修枝刀
[义] A sharp knife with a curved or straight blade for trimming stems, grafting, or delicate pruning. 弯或直刃锋利小刀，用于修茎、嫁接或精细修枝（folding pruning knife 折叠修枝刀, graft with a pruning knife 用修枝刀嫁接, sharpen the pruning knife 磨修枝刀）。可数。
[例] ① He trimmed the rose canes with a clean pruning knife at a slant. 他用干净的修枝刀斜剪玫瑰枝。 ② A sharp pruning knife makes cleaner cuts than dull shears on soft stems. 软茎用锋利修枝刀比钝剪刀切口更整齐。
[搭] folding pruning knife 折叠修枝刀, graft with a pruning knife 用修枝刀嫁接, sharpen the pruning knife 磨修枝刀, curved blade 弯刃
[联] knife 刀, prune 修剪, graft 嫁接, garden 花园, blade 刀刃, stem 茎, sharp 锋利
[记] 可数：a pruning knife。嫁接、精细修枝用。与 pruning shears 互补

**marking gauge**
英/美 /ˈmɑːkɪŋ ɡeɪdʒ/
n. 划规；线规
[义] A woodworking tool with a pin or knife that marks parallel lines at a set distance from an edge. 木工工具，用针或刀在离边缘固定距离处划平行线（set the marking gauge 调整线规, scribe a line with a gauge 用线规划线, mortise gauge 榫眼线规）。可数。
[例] ① Scribe the tenon shoulder with a marking gauge before sawing. 锯榫头前先用线规划肩线。 ② A sharp gauge pin cuts cleaner than a pencil on raw wood. 锋利划针在毛木上比铅笔线更清。
[搭] set the marking gauge 调整线规, scribe a line with a gauge 用线规划线, mortise gauge 榫眼线规, cutting gauge 切划线规
[联] measure 测量, carpenter 木匠, wood 木头, scribe 划线, mark 标记, square 直角, chisel 凿
[记] 可数：a marking gauge。传统木工划平行线必备。mortise gauge 可划双道线

**combination square**
英/美 /ˌkɒmbɪˈneɪʃn skweə(r)/
n. 组合角尺；万能角尺
[义] A ruler with a sliding head that can measure 90° and 45° angles and check depth. 带滑动头、可测 90° 与 45° 角并测深度的直尺（check square with a combination square 用组合角尺验直角, combination square head 组合角尺头, depth gauge 深度测量）。可数。
[例] ① A combination square checks corners and doubles as a depth gauge. 组合角尺验角，还能当深度尺。 ② Lock the head before you mark the cut line along the blade. 沿尺身标线前先把头锁紧。
[搭] check square with a combination square 用组合角尺验直角, combination square head 组合角尺头, depth gauge 深度测量, 45-degree miter 四十五度角
[联] set square 三角板, ruler 尺子, angle 角, carpenter 木匠, measure 测量, level 水平, mark 标记
[记] 可数：a combination square。比 set square 功能多。车间、木工常用

**dovetail saw**
英/美 /ˈdʌvteɪl sɔː/
n. 燕尾锯；细木工锯
[义] A small backsaw with fine teeth for cutting precise joints such as dovetails. 细齿小背锯，用于切割燕尾等精密接合（cut dovetails with a dovetail saw 用燕尾锯开榫, fine-tooth dovetail saw 细齿燕尾锯, stiff back saw 硬背锯）。可数。
[例] ① A dovetail saw leaves a thin kerf for tight-fitting joints. 燕尾锯缝窄，榫接更紧密。 ② Saw to the scribed line and chisel out the waste for dovetails. 沿划线锯到，再用凿子清废料。
[搭] cut dovetails with a dovetail saw 用燕尾锯开榫, fine-tooth dovetail saw 细齿燕尾锯, stiff back saw 硬背锯, joinery saw 细木工锯
[联] saw 锯, chisel 凿, wood 木头, joint 接合, carpenter 木匠, precise 精密, blade 锯条
[记] 可数：a dovetail saw。背板使锯条刚性足，切线更直

**sawhorse**
英/美 /ˈsɔːhɔːs/
n. 锯木架；马凳
[义] A frame that supports a board or plank while it is being cut or worked on. 切割或加工板材时支撑板子的支架（pair of sawhorses 一对锯木架, set up sawhorses 支起锯木架, folding sawhorse 折叠锯木架）。可数。
[例] ① Lay the plywood across two sawhorses before you cut. 锯胶合板前先横搭在两个锯木架上。 ② Folding sawhorses tuck away flat in the garage. 折叠锯木架在车库可平放收纳。
[搭] pair of sawhorses 一对锯木架, set up sawhorses 支起锯木架, folding sawhorse 折叠锯木架, adjustable sawhorse 可调锯木架
[联] saw 锯, wood 木头, carpenter 木匠, support 支撑, workbench 工作台, cut 切, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a sawhorse。通常成对使用。亦作 trestle

**sanding block**
英/美 /ˈsændɪŋ blɒk/
n. 砂纸块；打磨块
[义] A block that holds sandpaper flat for even hand-sanding of surfaces. 夹平砂纸、便于均匀手磨表面的块（wrap sandpaper around a block 把砂纸包在打磨块上, rubber sanding block 橡胶打磨块, contoured sanding block 异形打磨块）。可数。
[例] ① A sanding block keeps edges square better than folding sandpaper in your hand. 打磨块比手捏砂纸更能保持棱边方正。 ② Change paper often so grit doesn't clog and scratch the surface. 经常换砂纸，免得砂粒堵死刮花表面。
[搭] wrap sandpaper around a block 把砂纸包在打磨块上, rubber sanding block 橡胶打磨块, contoured sanding block 异形打磨块, hook-and-loop block 魔术贴打磨块
[联] sandpaper 砂纸, sand 打磨, smooth 磨平, wood 木头, block 块, finish 涂装, carpenter 木匠
[记] 可数：a sanding block。比徒手砂纸更均匀。异形块可磨曲线

**masking tape**
英/美 /ˈmɑːskɪŋ teɪp/
n. 美纹纸胶带；遮蔽胶带
[义] Paper tape used to mask off areas when painting so edges stay clean. 喷漆时遮蔽不需上色区域、保持边缘干净的纸胶带（apply masking tape along the trim 沿线条贴美纹纸, peel off masking tape 撕遮蔽胶带, low-tack masking tape 低粘美纹纸）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Press masking tape down firmly so paint doesn't bleed underneath. 美纹纸要压实，漆才不会渗下去。 ② Remove masking tape before the paint fully cures to avoid tearing. 漆未全干时撕胶带，免得把漆膜扯破。
[搭] apply masking tape along the trim 沿线条贴美纹纸, peel off masking tape 撕遮蔽胶带, low-tack masking tape 低粘美纹纸, painter's tape 油漆胶带
[联] tape 胶带, paint 漆, mask 遮蔽, edge 边缘, trim 线条, adhesive 黏合, decorate 装饰
[记] 不可数/可数 roll。与 duct tape 不同：易撕、低残胶。painter's tape 常同义

**steam cleaner**
英/美 /stiːm ˈkliːnə(r)/
n. 蒸汽清洁机
[义] A machine that cleans surfaces using hot pressurized steam. 用高温高压蒸汽清洁表面的机器（handheld steam cleaner 手持蒸汽清洁机, steam-clean grout lines 蒸汽清洁砖缝, fill the water tank 加水）。可数。
[例] ① A steam cleaner loosened years of grease on the oven door. 蒸汽清洁机松动了烤箱门积年的油垢。 ② Test fabric in a hidden spot before steam-cleaning upholstery. 蒸汽清洁椅面织物前先在隐蔽处试一块布。
[搭] handheld steam cleaner 手持蒸汽清洁机, steam-clean grout lines 蒸汽清洁砖缝, fill the water tank 加水, steam mop 蒸汽拖把
[联] clean 清洁, steam 蒸汽, hot 热的, grout 砖缝, sanitize 消毒, mop 拖把, appliance 电器
[记] 可数：a steam cleaner。无化学剂消毒。烫手、烫面，须按说明使用

**wheel chock**
英/美 /wiːl tʃɒk/
n. 车轮挡块；楔块
[义] A wedge placed against a vehicle wheel to prevent it from rolling. 楔在车轮旁、防止车辆滚动的楔块（place wheel chocks 放置车轮挡块, chock the trailer wheels 楔住拖车车轮, rubber wheel chock 橡胶挡轮块）。可数。
[例] ① Chock both wheels before jacking up the trailer. 顶升拖车前先把两侧车轮楔住。 ② Wheel chocks are required on many job sites for parked trucks. 许多工地要求停放的卡车使用挡轮块。
[搭] place wheel chocks 放置车轮挡块, chock the trailer wheels 楔住拖车车轮, rubber wheel chock 橡胶挡轮块, heavy-duty chock 重型挡块
[联] wheel 轮子, wedge 楔子, car 汽车, trailer 拖车, safety 安全, jack 千斤顶, brake 刹车
[记] 可数：a wheel chock。顶车、装卸、坡道停车必备。与 handbrake 双保险

**lug wrench**
英/美 /lʌɡ rentʃ/
n. 轮胎扳手；轮毂扳手
[义] A wrench for removing lug nuts on vehicle wheels, often L-shaped or cross-shaped. 拆卸车轮轮毂螺母的扳手，常呈 L 形或十字形（cross lug wrench 十字轮胎扳手, loosen lug nuts 松轮毂螺母, telescoping lug wrench 伸缩轮胎扳手）。可数。
[例] ① Loosen lug nuts slightly before jacking the car up. 顶车之前先把轮毂螺母稍松一圈。 ② A cross lug wrench gives more leverage than a short L wrench. 十字轮胎扳手比短 L 形扳手更省力。
[搭] cross lug wrench 十字轮胎扳手, loosen lug nuts 松轮毂螺母, telescoping lug wrench 伸缩轮胎扳手, four-way lug wrench 四通轮胎扳手
[联] wrench 扳手, tire 轮胎, wheel 轮子, jack 千斤顶, nut 螺母, car 汽车, tire iron 轮胎撬棍
[记] 可数：a lug wrench。拧轮毂螺母用；撬胎用 tire iron。星形套筒款亦常见

**socket set**
英/美 /ˈsɒkɪt set/
n. 套筒组；套筒扳手套装
[义] A collection of sockets, ratchet handles, and extensions for turning nuts and bolts. 套筒、棘轮手柄与加长杆的组合，用于拧转螺母螺栓（metric socket set 公制套筒组, organize the socket set 整理套筒组, deep socket 深套筒）。可数。
[例] ① A complete socket set saves trips to the hardware store mid-repair. 齐全套筒组能避免修到一半跑五金店。 ② Match socket size to the fastener so you don't round the head. 套筒尺寸要对，别把螺栓头拧圆。
[搭] metric socket set 公制套筒组, organize the socket set 整理套筒组, deep socket 深套筒, socket wrench 套筒扳手
[联] socket wrench 套筒扳手, ratchet 棘轮, wrench 扳手, bolt 螺栓, nut 螺母, mechanic 技工, toolbox 工具箱
[记] 可数：a socket set。公制/英制须分清。deep socket 适合长螺栓

**locking pliers**
英/美 /ˈlɒkɪŋ ˈplaɪəz/
n. 大力钳；锁钳
[义] Pliers with a locking mechanism that clamp onto work and hold without continuous hand pressure. 带锁紧机构、夹住工件后无需持续手压的钳（Vise-Grip 大力钳〔品牌泛指〕, lock the pliers on the part 把锁钳夹牢在零件上, release the locking pliers 松开锁钳）。复数形式。
[例] ① Locking pliers freed the rounded bolt head when nothing else gripped. 别的工具夹不住时，大力钳卸下了拧圆的螺栓头。 ② Adjust the screw on the handle until the jaws clamp tight. 调手柄螺丝，直到钳口夹紧。
[搭] Vise-Grip 大力钳, lock the pliers on the part 把锁钳夹牢在零件上, release the locking pliers 松开锁钳, curved-jaw locking pliers 弯嘴锁钳
[联] pliers 钳, grip 夹持, wrench 扳手, clamp 夹, bolt 螺栓, tool 工具, mechanic 技工
[记] 复数 locking pliers。Vise-Grip 为品牌泛指。用完后按释放杆打开

**channel locks**
英/美 /ˈtʃænl lɒks/
n. 水泵钳；活动钳
[义] Adjustable pliers with angled jaws that grip pipes and nuts of various sizes. 可调、斜钳口、可夹各种尺寸管与螺母的钳（adjust channel locks 调节水泵钳, grip a pipe with channel locks 用水泵钳夹管, tongue-and-groove pliers 同义）。复数形式。
[例] ① Channel locks adjusted wide enough to grip the PVC coupling. 水泵钳调宽后夹住了 PVC 接头。 ② Keep the jaws perpendicular so they don't slip on rounded fittings. 钳口保持垂直，在圆管件上才不滑。
[搭] adjust channel locks 调节水泵钳, grip a pipe with channel locks 用水泵钳夹管, tongue-and-groove pliers 水泵钳, straight-jaw channel locks 直嘴水泵钳
[联] pliers 钳, pipe wrench 管钳, pipe 管, grip 夹持, plumbing 管道, adjustable 可调的, tool 工具
[记] 复数 channel locks。亦说 tongue-and-groove pliers。与 pipe wrench 用途重叠

**nut driver**
英/美 /nʌt ˈdraɪvə(r)/
n. 螺母旋具；套筒螺丝刀
[义] A tool like a screwdriver but with a socket tip for turning hex nuts and bolts. 形似螺丝刀、套筒头用于拧六角螺母螺栓的工具（nut driver set 螺母旋具组, tighten panel nuts 拧紧面板螺母, magnetic nut driver 磁性螺母旋具）。可数。
[例] ① A nut driver speeds up assembly on electronics chassis screws. 装电子机箱螺丝时，螺母旋具更快。 ② Match the driver size to the nut so you don't strip the corners. 旋具尺寸要对，别把螺母角拧秃。

[联] screwdriver 螺丝刀, socket 套筒, nut 螺母, bolt 螺栓, hex 六角, tool 工具, assemble 组装
[记] 可数：a nut driver。电工、HVAC、机柜组装常用

**drill bit**
英/美 /drɪl bɪt/
n. 钻头
[义] A removable cutting tip fitted into a drill for boring holes in material. 装入钻机、在材料上钻孔的可更换切削头（twist drill bit 麻花钻头, change the drill bit 更换钻头, masonry bit 石工钻头）。可数。
[例] ① Use a masonry bit when drilling into brick, not a wood bit. 钻砖要用石工钻头，别用木钻头。 ② Let the bit cool between holes in metal so it stays sharp. 金属钻孔孔间让钻头冷却，才保持锋利。

[联] drill 钻, hole 孔, bit 刀头, wood 木头, metal 金属, bore 钻孔, power tool 电动工具
[记] 可数：a drill bit。材质与用途须匹配。钝钻头易烧、易偏

**work light**
英/美 /wɜːk laɪt/
n. 工作灯；检修灯
[义] A portable lamp that provides bright directed light for working in dim areas. 提供明亮定向照明、便于在暗处作业的便携灯（LED work light LED 工作灯, hang a work light under the hood 把检修灯挂在引擎盖下, rechargeable work light 充电工作灯）。可数。
[例] ① He clipped a work light to the fender while checking the belt. 查皮带时他把工作灯夹在翼子板上。 ② A rechargeable work light saves extension cords in the attic. 充电工作灯在阁楼里省得拉延长线。

[联] miter saw 斜切锯, angle 角, cut 切, moulding 线条, carpenter 木匠, trim 线条, saw 锯
[记] 可数：a miter box。手锯切角用；大量重复切角用 miter saw 更快

**block plane**
英/美 /blɒk pleɪn/
n. 块刨；小刨
[义] A small woodworking plane held in one hand for trimming edges and end grain. 单手持有、修边与端面木纹的小木工刨（trim with a block plane 用块刨修边, adjust the block plane iron 调整块刨刃, low-angle block plane 低角度块刨）。可数。
[例] ① A block plane chamfers sharp edges on a shelf in seconds. 块刨几秒就能给搁板棱边倒角。 ② Sharpen the iron often—end grain dulls blades quickly. 刃要常磨，端面木纹很快钝刃。
[搭] trim with a block plane 用块刨修边, adjust the block plane iron 调整块刨刃, low-angle block plane 低角度块刨, pocket block plane 口袋块刨
[联] plane 刨, wood 木头, edge 边缘, carpenter 木匠, shave 刨花, sharp 锋利, hand tool 手工具
[记] 可数：a block plane。比 bench plane 小，单手可握。修端面木纹常用

**spokeshave**
英/美 /ˈspəʊkʃeɪv/
n. 辐刨；曲柄刨
[义] A small plane with handles on the sides, used to shape curved surfaces such as chair legs or wheel spokes. 两侧有柄的小刨，用于塑形曲面，如椅腿或辐条（shape a curve with a spokeshave 用辐刨修曲线, adjust the spokeshave blade 调整辐刨刃, flat-bottom spokeshave 平底辐刨）。可数。
[例] ① He smoothed the chair leg with a spokeshave after the bandsaw cut. 带锯切完后他用辐刨把椅腿修顺。 ② Take thin shavings—deep cuts chatter on curved grain. 薄刨花即可，深切在曲纹上会跳刀。
[搭] shape a curve with a spokeshave 用辐刨修曲线, adjust the spokeshave blade 调整辐刨刃, flat-bottom spokeshave 平底辐刨, round-bottom spokeshave 圆底辐刨
[联] plane 刨, curve 曲线, wood 木头, chair 椅子, shave 刨花, carpenter 木匠, hand tool 手工具
[记] 可数：a spokeshave。spoke + shave。椅腿、弧形 trim 传统工具

**belt sander**
英/美 /belt ˈsændə(r)/
n. 带式砂光机
[义] A power sander that uses a continuous loop of sandpaper for fast material removal on flat surfaces. 用环形砂纸快速去除平面材料的电动砂光机（strip paint with a belt sander 用带砂机除漆, belt sander grit 砂带粒度, keep the sander moving 保持砂光机移动）。可数。
[例] ① A belt sander levels a rough tabletop fast if you keep it moving. 带砂机移动着用，粗桌面很快能找平。 ② Start with coarse grit and work up so you don't gouge the wood. 先粗后细，免得刨伤木头。
[搭] strip paint with a belt sander 用带砂机除漆, belt sander grit 砂带粒度, keep the sander moving 保持砂光机移动, variable-speed belt sander 调速带砂机
[联] sander 砂光机, sandpaper 砂纸, wood 木头, smooth 磨平, power tool 电动工具, grit 粒度, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a belt sander。比 orbital sander 去料更猛。勿停在一处

**planer**
英/美 /ˈpleɪnə(r)/
n. 刨床；电刨
[义] A power tool that shaves a thin layer off a board to make it flat and even in thickness. 从板材上削薄一层、使其平整且厚度均匀的电动工具（thickness planer 厚度刨, plane a board 刨平板材, snipe at the ends 端部下陷〔术语〕）。可数。
[例] ① Run bowed boards through the planer until they lie flat on the bench. 弯板反复过刨床直到能在台面上平放。 ② Support long boards at both ends to reduce snipe. 长板两端支撑，减少端部多刨。
[搭] thickness planer 厚度刨, plane a board 刨平板材, benchtop planer 台刨, reduce snipe 减少端部下陷
[联] plane 刨, wood 木头, thickness 厚度, smooth 磨平, carpenter 木匠, power tool 电动工具, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a planer。与 hand plane 不同，为电动厚度刨。出料方向注意 grain

**tailor's tape measure**
英/美 /ˈteɪləz teɪp ˈmeʒə(r)/
n. 软尺；卷尺（裁缝用）
[义] A flexible cloth or fiberglass tape for measuring body dimensions and curved surfaces. 测量身体尺寸与曲面的柔性布尺或玻纤尺（wrap the tape around the waist 用软尺绕腰, retractable tailor's tape 伸缩软尺, dual-sided tape 双面软尺）。可数。
[例] ① The tailor's tape measure followed the curve of the hem exactly. 软尺沿裙摆曲线量得很准。 ② Retractable cloth tapes don't kink like metal tape measures on small circles. 伸缩布尺绕小圆时不像金属卷尺那样折弯。
[搭] wrap the tape around the waist 用软尺绕腰, retractable tailor's tape 伸缩软尺, dual-sided tape 双面软尺, sewing tape measure 缝纫卷尺
[联] tape measure 卷尺, measure 测量, sew 缝, fabric 织物, length 长度, body 身体, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数：a tailor's tape measure。与刚性 tape measure 互补：软尺贴体、卷尺测长距

**laser distance meter**
英/美 /ˈleɪzə ˈdɪstəns ˈmiːtə(r)/
n. 激光测距仪
[义] A handheld device that measures distance to a target using a laser beam. 用激光束测量到目标距离的手持设备（point and measure 对准即测, laser distance meter accuracy 激光测距精度, indoor laser measure 室内激光测距仪）。可数。
[例] ① He measured the room diagonal with a laser distance meter in one click. 他用激光测距仪一点就量出房间对角线。 ② Bright sunlight can weaken outdoor readings on some models. 强光下部分型号户外读数会偏弱。
[搭] point and measure 对准即测, laser distance meter accuracy 激光测距精度, indoor laser measure 室内激光测距仪, Bluetooth laser measure 蓝牙激光测距仪
[联] measure 测量, laser 激光, distance 距离, ruler 尺子, construction 建筑, accurate 准确的, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a laser distance meter。亦说 laser measure。装修量房、验尺寸常用

**feeler gauge**
英/美 /ˈfiːlə ɡeɪdʒ/
n. 塞尺；厚薄规
[义] A set of thin metal blades of known thickness used to measure small gaps and clearances. 一组已知厚度的薄金属片，用于测量小间隙与公隙（check gap with a feeler gauge 用塞尺测间隙, blade thickness 片厚, metric feeler gauge 公制塞尺）。可数。
[例] ① Slide feeler gauges between the parts until one drags slightly. 在零件间试塞尺片，直到某片略紧。 ② Mechanics use feeler gauges for spark plug gaps and valve clearance. 技工用塞尺调火花塞间隙与气门公隙。
[搭] check gap with a feeler gauge 用塞尺测间隙, blade thickness 片厚, metric feeler gauge 公制塞尺, stacked blades 叠片测量
[联] measure 测量, gap 间隙, thickness 厚度, mechanic 技工, precision 精密, caliper 卡尺, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a feeler gauge / a set of feeler gauges。多片叠合测间隙。汽车、机械装配常用

**depth gauge**
英/美 /depθ ɡeɪdʒ/
n. 深度尺；测深规
[义] A tool for measuring the depth of holes, slots, or recesses. 测量孔、槽或凹坑深度的工具（measure hole depth 测孔深, combination square depth gauge 组合角尺测深, digital depth gauge 数显深度尺）。可数。
[例] ① Check hole depth with a depth gauge before tapping threads. 攻丝前用深度尺检查孔深。 ② The combination square head doubles as a depth gauge on the ruler blade. 组合角尺头在尺身上可当测深规。
[搭] measure hole depth 测孔深, combination square depth gauge 组合角尺测深, digital depth gauge 数显深度尺, tire tread depth gauge 胎纹深度尺
[联] measure 测量, depth 深度, hole 孔, combination square 组合角尺, ruler 尺子, precision 精密, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a depth gauge。combination square 常内置测深功能。胎纹深度尺为另用

**sanding sponge**
英/美 /ˈsændɪŋ spʌndʒ/
n. 砂海绵；海绵砂块
[义] A flexible abrasive sponge used for sanding curves, corners, and contoured surfaces. 用于打磨曲线、棱角与异形面的柔性磨砂海绵（sand curves with a sponge 用海绵砂块磨曲线, fine-grit sanding sponge 细粒度砂海绵, wet sanding sponge 水磨砂海绵）。可数。
[例] ① A sanding sponge follows moulding profiles better than flat sandpaper. 海绵砂块比平砂纸更贴线条轮廓。 ② Rinse the sponge when grit clogs during wet sanding. 水磨时砂粒堵了就冲洗海绵。
[搭] sand curves with a sponge 用海绵砂块磨曲线, fine-grit sanding sponge 细粒度砂海绵, wet sanding sponge 水磨砂海绵, contoured sanding sponge 异形砂海绵
[联] sandpaper 砂纸, sanding block 砂纸块, sponge 海绵, smooth 磨平, curve 曲线, abrasive 磨料, finish 涂装
[记] 可数：a sanding sponge。比 sanding block 更贴曲面。可干湿两用

**tool chest**
英/美 /tuːl tʃest/
n. 工具箱；工具柜
[义] A large chest or cabinet with drawers for storing and organizing tools. 带抽屉、存放整理工具的大箱或柜（roll-out tool chest 抽拉式工具柜, organize tools in a chest 在工具柜里整理工具, mechanic's tool chest 技工工具箱）。可数。
[例] ① His roll-around tool chest followed him bay to bay in the shop. 他的移动工具柜在车间工位间跟着走。 ② Label drawers so wrenches and sockets go back to the same place. 抽屉贴标签，扳手套筒各归各位。
[搭] roll-out tool chest 抽拉式工具柜, organize tools in a chest 在工具柜里整理工具, mechanic's tool chest 技工工具箱, top chest and cabinet 顶箱加底柜
[联] toolbox 工具箱, tool 工具, drawer 抽屉, garage 车库, workshop 车间, organize 整理, mechanic 技工
[记] 可数：a tool chest。比 toolbox 大，多抽屉。专业车间标配

**pegboard**
英/美 /ˈpeɡbɔːd/
n. 挂板；洞洞板
[义] A perforated board with holes for pegs that hold tools on a wall. 带孔、用挂钩挂墙收纳工具的板（hang tools on pegboard 把工具挂在挂板上, pegboard hooks 挂板钩, outline tools in marker 用马克笔描工具轮廓）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Outline each tool on the pegboard so missing items show at a glance. 在挂板上描出每件工具轮廓，缺了哪件一眼看出。 ② Pegboard hooks come in loops and straight prongs for different tools. 挂板钩有环有直叉，配不同工具。

[记] 不可数/可数 panel。亦拼 peg board。车间可视化整理常用

**safety glasses**
英/美 /ˈseɪfti ɡlɑːsɪz/
n. 安全眼镜；防护眼镜
[义] Impact-resistant glasses worn to protect the eyes from flying debris and splashes. 防冲击眼镜，防护飞溅碎屑与液体入眼（wear safety glasses 戴安全眼镜, side-shield safety glasses 侧翼防护眼镜, ANSI-rated safety glasses 符合 ANSI 标准的安全眼镜）。复数形式。
[例] ① Safety glasses are mandatory on the shop floor when machines are running. 机器运转时车间必须戴安全眼镜。 ② Side shields block chips that bounce from the side, not just the front. 侧翼挡的不只是正面，还有侧面弹来的屑。
[搭] wear safety glasses 戴安全眼镜, side-shield safety glasses 侧翼防护眼镜, ANSI-rated safety glasses 符合 ANSI 标准的安全眼镜, prescription safety glasses 处方安全眼镜
[联] goggles 护目镜, protect 保护, eye 眼睛, safety 安全, workshop 车间, debris 碎屑, face shield 面罩
[记] 复数 safety glasses。比 goggles 更日常；化学飞溅仍可能需要 goggles

**earplugs**
英/美 /ˈɪəplʌɡz/
n. 耳塞
[义] Small inserts worn in the ear canals to reduce noise exposure. 塞入耳道、降低噪声暴露的小塞子（insert earplugs 塞耳塞, disposable foam earplugs 一次性泡沫耳塞, reusable earplugs 可重复使用耳塞）。复数形式。
[例] ① Wear earplugs when running the planer or belt sander for long sessions. 长时间开刨床或带砂机时要戴耳塞。 ② Roll foam earplugs thin before inserting so they expand to seal. 泡沫耳塞先搓细再塞，展开才密封。
[搭] insert earplugs 塞耳塞, disposable foam earplugs 一次性泡沫耳塞, reusable earplugs 可重复使用耳塞, corded earplugs 带线耳塞
[联] earmuffs 耳罩, noise 噪音, hearing 听力, protect 保护, safety 安全, loud 大声的, workshop 车间
[记] 复数 earplugs。与 earmuffs 互补：耳塞轻便，耳罩隔音更强

**dust collector**
英/美 /dʌst kəˈlektə(r)/
n. 集尘器；除尘机
[义] A system that captures wood or metal dust at the source during machining. 加工时在源头收集木屑或金属粉尘的系统（hook up a dust collector 连接集尘器, dust collector hose 集尘管, cyclone dust collector 旋风集尘器）。可数。
[例] ① A dust collector at the table saw keeps the shop air breathable. 台锯接集尘器，车间空气才好呼吸。 ② Empty the collector bag before it restricts airflow. 集尘袋满了就倒，免得风量减少。
[搭] hook up a dust collector 连接集尘器, dust collector hose 集尘管, cyclone dust collector 旋风集尘器, shop dust collector 车间集尘器

[记] 可数：a dust collector。比 shop vac 更适合持续机加工。仍须戴呼吸防护

**air compressor**
英/美 /eə kəmˈpresə(r)/
n. 空气压缩机；空压机
[义] A machine that compresses air into a tank for powering pneumatic tools. 把空气压入储气罐、为气动工具供能的机器（portable air compressor 便携空压机, PSI rating 磅压额定, drain the tank 放掉罐内积水）。可数。
[例] ① The nail gun and impact wrench both ran off the shop air compressor. 钉枪和冲击扳手都接车间空压机。 ② Drain the compressor tank regularly to prevent rust inside. 定期放罐内水，免得内部生锈。
[搭] portable air compressor 便携空压机, PSI rating 磅压额定, drain the tank 放掉罐内积水, pancake compressor 薄饼式空压机

[记] 可数：an air compressor。注意 PSI 与工具匹配。噪音大，戴耳塞

**paint sprayer**
英/美 /peɪnt ˈspreɪə(r)/
n. 喷漆枪；喷涂机
[义] A tool that applies paint in a fine spray for even coverage on large surfaces. 以细雾喷涂、在大面积上均匀上漆的工具（HVLP paint sprayer HVLP 喷漆枪, thin paint for spraying 调稀漆以便喷涂, clean the sprayer 清洗喷枪）。可数。
[例] ① An HVLP sprayer laid an even coat on the cabinet doors outdoors. HVLP 喷枪在户外给柜门上了均匀一层漆。 ② Strain paint and thin it correctly or the tip clogs mid-job. 漆要过滤、稀释到位，不然喷到一半嘴就堵。

[记] 可数：a paint sprayer。须戴 respirator、遮蔽周边。无气喷涂机压力更高

**drywall knife**
英/美 /ˈdraɪwɔːl naɪf/
n. 刮刀；抹刀（石膏板）
[义] A wide flexible blade on a handle for applying and smoothing joint compound on drywall. 宽柔韧刃长柄，用于在石膏板上批刮与抹平嵌缝膏（mud the seam 批缝, 6-inch drywall knife 六英寸刮刀, feather the edge 把边抹薄）。可数。
[例] ① Feather the joint compound with a wide drywall knife so the seam disappears. 用宽刮刀把嵌缝膏边缘抹薄，接缝才看不见。 ② Keep the knife clean between passes to avoid streaks. 每道之间把刀刮干净，免得留痕。

**mud pan**
英/美 /mʌd pæn/
n. 灰盆；嵌缝膏盘
[义] A shallow pan for holding joint compound while taping and finishing drywall. 嵌缝与修整石膏板时盛放嵌缝膏的浅盘（load mud into the pan 把嵌缝膏装进灰盆, clean the mud pan 清洗灰盆, metal mud pan 金属灰盆）。可数。
