**oil filter wrench**
英/美 /ɔɪl ˈfɪltə rentʃ/
n. 机油滤清器扳手
[义] A wrench designed to grip and remove cylindrical oil filters on engines. 专用于夹持并拆卸发动机圆柱形机油滤清器的扳手（strap wrench oil filter 带式机油滤扳手, cap wrench 帽式滤清器扳手, remove the oil filter 拆机油滤）。可数。
[例] ① The strap wrench gripped the slick filter when a socket wouldn't fit. 套筒套不上时，带式扳手咬住了滑溜溜的滤清器。 ② Lubricate the new filter gasket before threading it on by hand. 新滤清器垫圈先抹油，再用手拧上。
[搭] strap wrench oil filter 带式机油滤扳手, cap wrench 帽式滤清器扳手, remove the oil filter 拆机油滤, chain filter wrench 链式滤清器扳手
[联] wrench 扳手, oil 机油, filter 滤清器, car 汽车, engine 发动机, mechanic 技工, oil pan 接油盘
[记] 可数：an oil filter wrench。cap 式须匹配滤清器尺寸。换油常见工具

**car ramp**
英/美 /kɑːr ræmp/
n. 汽车坡道；维修坡道
[义] A pair of inclined ramps that lift the front or rear of a vehicle for access underneath. 一对斜坡，把车辆前部或后部顶高以便钻到车下（drive up the ramps 开上坡道, chock the wheels 楔住车轮, pair of car ramps 一对汽车坡道）。可数。
[例] ① He drove slowly up the car ramps and set the parking brake. 他慢慢开上坡道，拉好手刹。 ② Chock the rear wheels before working under a ramped car. 坡道顶车后在后轮楔挡块再钻车底。
[搭] drive up the ramps 开上坡道, chock the wheels 楔住车轮, pair of car ramps 一对汽车坡道, weight-rated ramps 额定承重坡道
[联] jack 千斤顶, wheel chock 挡轮块, car 汽车, mechanic 技工, undercarriage 底盘, oil change 换油, garage 车库
[记] 可数：a pair of car ramps。须核对承重。与 jack 相比更稳，仍须楔轮

**hedge shears**
英/美 /hed ʃɪəz/
n. 绿篱剪；修篱剪
[义] Large shears with long blades for trimming hedges and shrubs. 长刃大剪，用于修剪树篱与灌木（trim the hedge with shears 用修篱剪修树篱, sharpen hedge shears 磨绿篱剪, telescoping hedge shears 伸缩修篱剪）。复数形式。
[例] ① Sharp hedge shears cut cleanly without tearing leaves. 锋利的修篱剪切口整齐，不会撕叶。 ② Telescoping handles reach tall hedges without a ladder. 伸缩柄够高树篱，不必爬梯。

[联] hedge 树篱, prune 修剪, garden 花园, loppers 长柄剪, scissors 剪刀, shrub 灌木, trim 修剪
[记] 复数 hedge shears。与 pruning shears 不同：修篱剪刃更长、更直

**pole saw**
英/美 /pəʊl sɔː/
n. 长杆锯；高枝锯
[义] A saw on an extension pole for cutting high branches from the ground. 接长杆、在地面够高枝切割的锯（extend the pole saw 伸长高枝锯, prune high branches 修高枝, manual pole saw 手动长杆锯）。可数。
[例] ① A pole saw removed dead limbs over the roofline safely from the ground. 长杆锯在地面安全地锯掉屋顶线上的枯枝。 ② Check overhead for power lines before extending a pole saw. 伸长高枝锯前先看头顶有无电线。
[搭] extend the pole saw 伸长高枝锯, prune high branches 修高枝, manual pole saw 手动长杆锯, powered pole saw 电动高枝锯
[联] saw 锯, prune 修剪, branch 树枝, ladder 梯子, garden 花园, tree 树, loppers 长柄剪
[记] 可数：a pole saw。比爬梯安全，仍须注意落枝方向

**hand cultivator**
英/美 /hænd ˈkʌltɪveɪtə(r)/
n. 手耙；手用松土器
[义] A hand tool with curved tines for loosening soil and removing weeds in garden beds. 弯齿手工具，用于松土和除花坛杂草（loosen soil with a cultivator 用手耙松土, three-tine cultivator 三齿手耙, stir the top layer 翻动表层土）。可数。
[例] ① A hand cultivator breaks crusted soil between rows without disturbing roots deeply. 手耙在行间破板结，又不深扰根。 ② Work the cultivator shallowly around shallow-rooted plants. 浅根植物周围手耙要浅翻。
[搭] loosen soil with a cultivator 用手耙松土, three-tine cultivator 三齿手耙, stir the top layer 翻动表层土, ergonomic handle 人体工学柄
[联] hoe 锄, rake 耙, garden 花园, soil 土, weed 杂草, trowel 小铲, cultivate 耕作
[记] 可数：a hand cultivator。小床、容器园艺常用。比 hoe 更轻、更密齿

**hose nozzle**
英/美 /həʊz ˈnɒzl/
n. 水管喷头；花洒嘴
[义] An attachment at the end of a garden hose that controls spray pattern and flow. 接在花园水管末端、控制喷洒形态与流量的附件（adjust the hose nozzle 调节喷头, jet and shower settings 射流与喷淋模式, shut-off nozzle 止水喷头）。可数。
[例] ① Twist the hose nozzle to a gentle shower for seedlings. 把喷头拧到柔和喷淋档浇幼苗。 ② A shut-off nozzle at the end saves trips back to the spigot. 末端止水喷头省得跑回去关龙头。
[搭] adjust the hose nozzle 调节喷头, jet and shower settings 射流与喷淋模式, shut-off nozzle 止水喷头, fireman-style nozzle 消防式喷头
[联] hose 水管, spray 喷洒, garden 花园, water 水, adjust 调节, watering 浇水, lawn 草坪
[记] 可数：a hose nozzle。不同喷洒模式适合浇花与冲洗车道

**mixing paddle**
英/美 /ˈmɪksɪŋ ˈpædl/
n. 搅拌桨；搅拌棒
[义] A flat or helical paddle attached to a drill for mixing paint, mortar, or compound. 接在电钻上、搅拌油漆、砂浆或嵌缝膏的扁平或螺旋桨（mix paint with a paddle 用搅拌桨调漆, mud mixing paddle 嵌缝膏搅拌桨, drill-mounted paddle 电钻搅拌桨）。可数。
[例] ① Churn the bucket of thinset with a mixing paddle on low speed. 低速电钻配搅拌桨搅桶里薄贴砂浆。 ② Scrape the paddle against the bucket side to break dry lumps. 把桨沿桶壁刮，打散干块。

**grill brush**
英/美 /ɡrɪl brʌʃ/
n. 烧烤刷；烤架刷
[义] A stiff brush for cleaning grease and char from barbecue grill grates. 硬刷，用于清理烧烤架栅上的油脂与焦垢（scrub the grill grates 刷烤架, wire grill brush 钢丝烧烤刷, clean while warm 趁热清理）。可数。
[例] ① Scrub the grill grates with a wire brush while they are still warm. 烤架还热时用钢丝刷刷洗栅条。 ② Replace frayed grill brush wires so bristles don't stick to food. 刷丝磨损就换，免得刷毛粘到食物上。
[搭] scrub the grill grates 刷烤架, wire grill brush 钢丝烧烤刷, clean while warm 趁热清理, brass grill brush 黄铜烧烤刷
[联] brush 刷子, grill 烧烤架, clean 清洁, barbecue 烧烤, grease 油脂, wire 金属丝, kitchen 厨房
[记] 可数：a grill brush。趁热刷更省力。曾有 wire 刷毛脱落烫伤风险，须检查磨损

**bottle brush**
英/美 /ˈbɒtl brʌʃ/
n. 瓶刷；试管刷
[义] A long brush with bristles around a wire stem for cleaning inside bottles and narrow vessels. 长柄、绕钢丝杆的刷毛，用于清洁瓶内与窄口容器（scrub inside the bottle 刷瓶内, narrow-neck bottle brush 窄颈瓶刷, baby bottle brush 奶瓶刷）。可数。
[例] ① A bottle brush reached the corners of the thermos that a sponge couldn't. 瓶刷刷到了海绵够不到的保温瓶角。 ② Rinse the brush and hang it to dry so it doesn't mildew. 刷完冲净挂干，免得发霉。
[搭] scrub inside the bottle 刷瓶内, narrow-neck bottle brush 窄颈瓶刷, baby bottle brush 奶瓶刷, reusable straw brush 吸管刷

**chalk holder**
英/美 /tʃɔːk ˈhəʊldə(r)/
n. 粉笔夹；粉笔套

[例] ① A chalk holder keeps hands cleaner when snapping long layout lines. 弹长布局线时，粉笔夹让手更干净。 ② Break chalk to length before loading the holder for tight corners. 角位紧的话先把粉笔掰短再装入夹具。

**floor jack**
英/美 /flɔː dʒæk/
n. 卧式千斤顶；地板千斤顶
[义] A low-profile hydraulic jack on wheels used to lift vehicles in garages. 带轮、机身低矮的液压千斤顶，车库顶车用（pump the floor jack 压卧式千斤顶, jack up the car 顶起汽车, 3-ton floor jack 三吨卧式千斤顶）。可数。
[例] ① Roll the floor jack under the jack point and pump until the tire clears. 把卧式千斤顶推到顶车点，压到轮胎离地。 ② Always use jack stands after lifting—never rely on the jack alone. 顶起后务必用支架，别单靠千斤顶。
[搭] pump the floor jack 压卧式千斤顶, jack up the car 顶起汽车, 3-ton floor jack 三吨卧式千斤顶, low-profile floor jack 低型卧式千斤顶

**jack stand**
英/美 /dʒæk stænd/
n. 千斤顶支架；顶车支架
[义] A rigid support placed under a vehicle after it is lifted, so work can be done safely. 车辆顶起后垫在车下的刚性支架，便于安全作业（set jack stands under the frame 在车架下放置顶车支架, adjust jack stand height 调节支架高度, pair of jack stands 一对顶车支架）。可数。
[例] ① Never crawl under a car supported only by a jack—always set jack stands. 别在只靠千斤顶顶着的车下钻，务必用支架。 ② He lowered the car onto jack stands before removing the wheel. 卸轮前他把车落到支架上。
[搭] set jack stands under the frame 在车架下放置顶车支架, adjust jack stand height 调节支架高度, pair of jack stands 一对顶车支架, 3-ton jack stands 三吨顶车支架

[例] ① He threaded the cut pipe with a manual threader before screwing on the elbow. 切好管后用手动套丝器攻丝，再拧弯头。 ② Apply cutting oil while threading so the die doesn't gall. 套丝时加切削油，免得板牙咬死。

**abrasive pad**

n. 磨砂垫；百洁布

[例] ① An abrasive pad removed the rust ring around the faucet without scratching chrome. 磨砂垫去掉水龙头锈圈，又没刮花镀铬层。 ② Switch to a finer pad before the final polish on stainless steel. 不锈钢最后一道抛光前换细粒度垫。

[义] A wheel on a handle that records distance rolled along the ground. 装在长柄上的滚轮，沿地面滚动累计距离（roll the measuring wheel 推测距轮, digital measuring wheel 数显测距轮, survey measuring wheel 测量用测距轮）。可数。
[例] ① He paced the fence line with a measuring wheel before ordering posts. 订桩前先推测距轮量围栏长度。 ② Keep the wheel straight on slopes so the reading stays accurate. 坡地上轮子要推直，读数才准。

**impact wrench**
英/美 /ˈɪmpækt rentʃ/
n. 冲击扳手；风炮
[义] A powered wrench that delivers high torque in rapid bursts to loosen or tighten fasteners. 以短促冲击输出大扭矩的电动或气动扳手（impact wrench on lug nuts 用风炮拧轮毂螺母, pneumatic impact wrench 气动冲击扳手, cordless impact wrench 充电冲击扳手）。可数。
[例] ① A cordless impact wrench spun the rusted bolt off in seconds. 充电风炮几秒就把锈螺栓拧下来。 ② Use the correct socket size—impact wrenches can round soft fasteners. 套筒尺寸要对，风炮容易拧圆软质紧固件。
[搭] impact wrench on lug nuts 用风炮拧轮毂螺母, pneumatic impact wrench 气动冲击扳手, cordless impact wrench 充电冲击扳手, torque stick 扭矩杆

[记] 可数：an impact wrench。亦说 impact gun / air gun。汽修拆轮毂、框架螺栓常用

**hand truck**

n. 手推车；搬运车
[义] An L-shaped wheeled cart used to tilt and roll heavy boxes or appliances. L 形带轮小车，可倾斜后滚运重箱或家电（load boxes on a hand truck 把箱子装上手推车, strap the load 绑牢货物, stair-climbing hand truck 爬楼手推车）。可数。
[例] ① Two people stacked the fridge onto a hand truck and rolled it through the door. 两人把冰箱抬上手推车，推过门。 ② Strap tall loads so they don't tip backward on the stairs. 高货要绑牢，下楼梯别向后翻。

**ratchet strap**

n. 棘轮绑带；拉紧带
[义] A strap with a ratcheting buckle used to secure loads on trucks or trailers. 带棘轮扣的绑带，用于固定货车或拖车上的货物（tighten a ratchet strap 收紧棘轮绑带, release the ratchet 松开棘轮, tie down furniture 绑牢家具）。可数。
[例] ① Ratchet straps kept the dresser from sliding in the back of the pickup. 棘轮绑带把梳妆台在皮卡后斗里勒住。 ② Release tension slowly so the strap doesn't snap back. 松张力要慢，免得绑带弹回。
[搭] tighten a ratchet strap 收紧棘轮绑带, release the ratchet 松开棘轮, tie down furniture 绑牢家具, 15-foot ratchet strap 十五英尺棘轮绑带
[联] strap 绑带, tie down 系牢, truck 货车, load 货物, secure 固定, move 搬运, trailer 拖车
[记] 可数：a ratchet strap。亦说 tie-down strap。比绳结更稳、更快。勿超过额定拉力

**conduit bender**
英/美 /ˈkɒndjuːɪt ˈbendə(r)/
n. 线管弯管器
[义] A tool that bends metal electrical conduit to precise angles without kinking. 把金属电线管弯成精确角度且不易折瘪的工具（bend conduit to 90 degrees 把线管弯成九十度, mark the bend point 标弯点, offset bend 偏移弯）。可数。
[例] ① The electrician marked each stub-up before bending conduit with a hand bender. 电工用手动弯管器弯管前，先标好每处竖段位置。 ② Over-bending weakens the conduit wall—use the degree marks on the tool. 弯过头会削弱管壁，按工具上的角度刻度来。

[联] conduit 线管, wire 电线, electrician 电工, bend 弯, pipe 管, electrical 电气的, fish tape 穿线器

**toilet auger**
英/美 /ˈtɔɪlət ˈɔːɡə(r)/
n. 马桶疏通器；坐便器通渠器
[义] A short auger designed to clear clogs in toilet traps without scratching the bowl. 专清坐便器弯管堵塞的短螺旋钻，且不易刮伤陶瓷（run a toilet auger 用马桶疏通器, feed the auger into the trap 把钻伸进存水弯, crank the handle 转动手柄）。可数。
[例] ① A plunger failed, so he cleared the clog with a toilet auger instead. 搋子没用，他改用马桶疏通器。 ② Insert the protective sleeve so the cable doesn't mar the porcelain. 套上保护套，钢缆才不会刮花瓷面。
[搭] run a toilet auger 用马桶疏通器, feed the auger into the trap 把钻伸进存水弯, crank the handle 转动手柄, closet auger 马桶疏通器
[联] plunger 搋子, toilet 马桶, clog 堵塞, drain 下水道, plumbing 管道, auger 螺旋钻, bathroom 浴室
[记] 可数：a toilet auger。亦说 closet auger。比通用 drain snake 短、带护套。plunger 无效时再用

**bench grinder**
英/美 /bentʃ ˈɡraɪndə(r)/
n. 台式砂轮机
[义] A fixed grinder with two abrasive wheels for sharpening tools and removing metal. 固定式双砂轮磨床，用于刃具开刃与去金属（sharpen a chisel on the bench grinder 在台砂轮上磨凿子, dress the grinding wheel 修整砂轮, wire wheel on a bench grinder 台砂轮钢丝轮）。可数。
[例] ① He touched up the lawnmower blade on the bench grinder before spring. 开春前他在台砂轮上把割草机刀片磨快。 ② Keep the tool moving—holding still can overheat and blue the steel. 刀具要移动着磨，停在一处会把钢烧蓝。
[搭] sharpen a chisel on the bench grinder 在台砂轮上磨凿子, dress the grinding wheel 修整砂轮, wire wheel on a bench grinder 台砂轮钢丝轮, eye shield 防护挡板
[联] grinder 磨床, grind 磨, wheel 砂轮, sharpen 磨快, workshop 车间, metal 金属, safety glasses 安全眼镜
[记] 可数：a bench grinder。车间标配。须戴护目镜；火花勿靠近易燃物

**angle grinder**
英/美 /ˈæŋɡl ˈɡraɪndə(r)/
n. 角磨机；砂轮机（手持）
[义] A handheld grinder with a rotating disc for cutting, grinding, or polishing metal and masonry. 手持旋转砂片机，用于切割、打磨或抛光金属与砖石（cut metal with an angle grinder 用角磨机切金属, grinding disc 砂片, guard facing the user 护罩朝向操作者）。可数。
[例] ① An angle grinder made short work of the rusted bolt heads. 角磨机很快切掉锈螺栓头。 ② Never remove the guard or use a disc rated above the grinder's RPM. 别卸护罩，也别用超过额定转速的砂片。
[搭] cut metal with an angle grinder 用角磨机切金属, grinding disc 砂片, guard facing the user 护罩朝向操作者, flap disc 百叶砂片
[联] grinder 磨床, cut 切, metal 金属, disc 砂片, power tool 电动工具, spark 火花, safety 安全
[记] 可数：an angle grinder。亦说 side grinder。火花飞溅，须护目镜与防火措施

**speed square**
英/美 /spiːd skweə(r)/
n. 三角尺；快尺
[义] A triangular marking tool combining a try square, miter square, and saw guide for carpentry. 木工用三角划规，兼作直角尺、斜角尺与锯导（mark a cut with a speed square 用快尺划切线, roof pitch marks 屋顶坡度刻度, saw guide lip 锯导唇边）。可数。
[例] ① He hooked the speed square on the board edge and drew a square line in one move. 他把快尺钩在板边，一笔划出垂直线。 ② Speed squares double as a quick protractor for common roof pitches. 快尺上的刻度可快速读常见屋顶坡度角。
[搭] mark a cut with a speed square 用快尺划切线, roof pitch marks 屋顶坡度刻度, saw guide lip 锯导唇边, aluminum speed square 铝快尺
[联] square 角尺, carpenter 木匠, mark 标记, angle 角, cut 切, framing 框架, ruler 尺
[记] 可数：a speed square。亦说 rafter square / triangle square。北美框架木工口袋常备

**framing square**
英/美 /ˈfreɪmɪŋ skweə(r)/
n. 曲尺；框架角尺
[义] A large L-shaped square with tables stamped on it for laying out rafters and stairs. 大号 L 形角尺，尺身刻表，用于放样椽条与楼梯（lay out rafters with a framing square 用曲尺放样椽条, stair gauges on the square 楼梯卡子, blade and tongue 长臂与短臂）。可数。
[例] ① The carpenter stepped off the rafter cuts with a framing square and stair gauges. 木匠用曲尺和楼梯卡子步测椽条切口。 ② The stamped tables on the square save lookup time for common pitches. 尺上刻表省去查常见坡度的功夫。
[搭] lay out rafters with a framing square 用曲尺放样椽条, stair gauges on the square 楼梯卡子, blade and tongue 长臂与短臂, steel framing square 钢曲尺
[联] square 角尺, carpenter 木匠, rafter 椽, stair 楼梯, layout 放样, angle 角, roof 屋顶
[记] 可数：a framing square。亦说 steel square / carpenter's square。比 speed square 大，放样用

**push stick**
英/美 /pʊʃ stɪk/
n. 推棒；送料棒
[义] A stick used to push workpieces through saws while keeping hands away from the blade. 推工件过锯、使手远离锯片的棒子（use a push stick at the table saw 台锯用推棒, shop-made push stick 自制推棒, notched push stick 缺口推棒）。可数。
[例] ① Always use a push stick when the rip fence closes the gap near the blade. 靠导尺推窄条时，一定要用推棒。 ② A push stick costs nothing to make but can save a finger. 推棒自己做不花钱，却能少伤一根手指。
[搭] use a push stick at the table saw 台锯用推棒, shop-made push stick 自制推棒, notched push stick 缺口推棒, push block 推块
[联] table saw 台锯, safety 安全, blade 锯片, wood 木头, carpenter 木匠, hand 手, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a push stick。与 push block 互补。台锯、圆锯窄切标配安全件

**hammer drill**
英/美 /ˈhæmə drɪl/
n. 冲击钻；锤钻
[义] A drill that combines rotation with a hammering action for drilling masonry and concrete. 旋转兼锤击的钻机，用于在砖石与混凝土上钻孔（drill into concrete with a hammer drill 用冲击钻打混凝土, hammer drill mode 冲击模式, masonry bit 石工钻头）。可数。
[例] ① Switch the hammer drill to drill-only mode when you work in wood. 打木头时把冲击钻切到纯钻模式。 ② Let the tool do the hammering—pushing hard wears out bits faster. 让工具自己锤，硬顶反而更快磨钻头。
[搭] drill into concrete with a hammer drill 用冲击钻打混凝土, hammer drill mode 冲击模式, masonry bit 石工钻头, SDS hammer drill SDS 冲击钻
[联] drill 钻, concrete 混凝土, masonry 砖石, bit 钻头, power tool 电动工具, wall 墙, anchor 锚栓
[记] 可数：a hammer drill。比普通电钻多锤击。挂电视、装膨胀螺栓常用

**rotary hammer**
英/美 /ˈrəʊtəri ˈhæmə(r)/
n. 电锤；旋转锤
[义] A heavy-duty hammer drill that uses a piston mechanism for faster drilling in hard concrete. 用活塞机构在硬混凝土中更快钻孔的重型锤钻（rotary hammer with SDS bit 电锤配 SDS 钻头, chisel mode 凿削模式, core drilling 取芯钻孔）。可数。
[例] ① A rotary hammer bored the anchor holes in the basement wall in half the time. 电锤打地下室墙锚孔，时间减半。 ② Use chisel mode to chip tile adhesive off the slab. 凿削模式可铲掉地面旧瓷砖胶。
[搭] rotary hammer with SDS bit 电锤配 SDS 钻头, chisel mode 凿削模式, core drilling 取芯钻孔, demolition chisel 拆除凿
[联] hammer drill 冲击钻, concrete 混凝土, drill 钻, demolition 拆除, power tool 电动工具, bit 钻头, construction 建筑
[记] 可数：a rotary hammer。比 hammer drill 更重、更快。SDS 快换钻头系统常见

**drop cloth**
英/美 /drɒp klɒθ/
n. 防尘布；铺地布
[义] A sheet spread on floors or furniture to catch paint drips and dust during work. 施工时铺在地面或家具上、接漆滴与灰尘的布（spread a drop cloth 铺防尘布, canvas drop cloth 帆布防尘布, plastic drop cloth 塑料防尘布）。可数。
[例] ① Tape the edges of the drop cloth so it doesn't slide underfoot. 把防尘布边贴牢，免得踩滑。 ② A canvas drop cloth absorbs splashes better than thin plastic. 帆布比薄塑料更能吸溅漆。
[搭] spread a drop cloth 铺防尘布, canvas drop cloth 帆布防尘布, plastic drop cloth 塑料防尘布, taped drop cloth 贴边防尘布
[联] paint 漆, protect 保护, floor 地板, furniture 家具, renovation 翻新, cover 遮盖, clean 清洁
[记] 可数：a drop cloth。刷漆、打磨、吊顶前必铺。canvas 可反复用，塑料多为一次性

**thread seal tape**
英/美 /θred siːl teɪp/
n. 生料带；螺纹密封带
[义] A thin PTFE tape wrapped on pipe threads to help prevent leaks. 缠在管螺纹上、助防渗漏的薄聚四氟乙烯带（wrap thread seal tape 缠生料带, two wraps clockwise 顺时针缠两圈, Teflon tape 特氟龙带）。不可数。
[例] ① Wrap thread seal tape clockwise on male threads so it doesn't unwind when you tighten. 在阳螺纹上顺时针缠生料带，拧紧时才不会松脱。 ② Don't use tape on compression fittings—it isn't needed and can cause leaks. 压缩接头别缠带，不需要还可能漏。
[搭] wrap thread seal tape 缠生料带, two wraps clockwise 顺时针缠两圈, Teflon tape 特氟龙带, PTFE thread tape 聚四氟乙烯螺纹带
[联] pipe 管, thread 螺纹, plumbing 管道, seal 密封, leak 渗漏, fitting 管件, plumber 水管工
[记] 不可数。亦说 Teflon tape / plumber's tape。只用于锥管螺纹，压缩接头除外

**grout float**
英/美 /ɡraʊt fləʊt/
n. 勾缝抹子；填缝板
[义] A flat rubber or sponge tool used to spread and smooth grout between tiles. 扁平橡胶或海绵工具，用于在瓷砖间抹填勾缝剂（spread grout with a float 用抹子填缝, rubber grout float 橡胶勾缝抹子, clean excess grout 清多余勾缝剂）。可数。
[例] ① Work diagonally across the tile with a grout float to pack the joints full. 用勾缝抹子对角抹，把缝压实。 ② Rinse the float often so hardened bits don't scratch the tile face. 抹子常冲水，硬块才不会刮花砖面。
[搭] spread grout with a float 用抹子填缝, rubber grout float 橡胶勾缝抹子, clean excess grout 清多余勾缝剂, epoxy grout float 环氧勾缝抹子
[联] grout 勾缝剂, tile 瓷砖, float 抹子, bathroom 浴室, kitchen 厨房, spread 抹, renovation 翻新
[记] 可数：a grout float。与 notched trowel（铺胶）不同，专填缝。对角抹、及时清残浆

**tile cutter**
英/美 /taɪl ˈkʌtə(r)/
n. 瓷砖切割器；划刀切砖器
[义] A manual tool that scores ceramic tile and snaps it along the score line. 在陶瓷砖上划痕并沿划痕折断的手动工具（score and snap tile 划切瓷砖, tile cutter wheel 切砖轮, measure twice before scoring 划前量两遍）。可数。
[例] ① A manual tile cutter gave clean straight cuts on the bathroom wall tiles. 手动切砖器在浴室墙砖上切出笔直边。 ② Replace the cutting wheel when scores get shallow and chips increase. 划痕变浅、崩边增多就换划轮。
[搭] score and snap tile 划切瓷砖, tile cutter wheel 切砖轮, measure twice before scoring 划前量两遍, rail tile cutter 导轨切砖器
[联] tile 瓷砖, cut 切, wet saw 湿切锯, bathroom 浴室, floor 地板, renovation 翻新, score 划
[记] 可数：a tile cutter。直切常用；曲线或厚砖可能需 wet saw。划一次、压断

**wet saw**
英/美 /wet sɔː/
n. 湿切锯；水锯
[义] A power saw that uses water to cool the blade while cutting tile or stone. 切瓷砖或石材时用水冷却锯片的电动锯（cut tile on a wet saw 湿切锯切砖, diamond wet saw blade 金刚石湿切锯片, keep water flowing 保持水流）。可数。
[例] ① A wet saw handled the thick porcelain cuts the manual cutter couldn't. 湿切锯切动了手动切砖器搞不定的厚玻化砖。 ② Wear eye protection—slurry can spray from the blade guard opening. 戴护目镜，浆液会从护罩口溅出。
[搭] cut tile on a wet saw 湿切锯切砖, diamond wet saw blade 金刚石湿切锯片, keep water flowing 保持水流, rent a wet saw 租湿切锯
[联] tile 瓷砖, saw 锯, cut 切, water 水, diamond blade 金刚石锯片, bathroom 浴室, renovation 翻新
[记] 可数：a wet saw。比 manual tile cutter 慢但可切厚砖、斜切。租房装修常租一台

**magnetic pickup tool**
英/美 /mæɡˈnetɪk ˈpɪkʌp tuːl/
n. 磁性拾取器；吸铁棒
[义] A telescoping tool with a magnet at the tip for retrieving metal parts from tight spaces. 可伸缩、尖端带磁铁，从窄缝拾取金属零件的工具（fish out a dropped screw 吸出掉落的螺丝, telescoping magnetic pickup 伸缩磁性拾取器, LED pickup tool 带灯拾取器）。可数。
[例] ① A magnetic pickup tool retrieved the bolt that fell into the engine bay. 磁性拾取器把掉进发动机舱的螺栓吸了出来。 ② Extend the shaft slowly so the magnet doesn't snap against painted surfaces. 慢慢伸长，别让磁铁啪地吸在漆面上。
[搭] fish out a dropped screw 吸出掉落的螺丝, telescoping magnetic pickup 伸缩磁性拾取器, LED pickup tool 带灯拾取器, flexible magnetic pickup 柔性磁性拾取器
[联] magnet 磁铁, screw 螺丝, retrieve 拾取, mechanic 技工, tool 工具, engine 发动机, drop 掉落
[记] 可数：a magnetic pickup tool。车间、汽修常备。非磁性零件用爪式拾取器

**inspection mirror**
英/美 /ɪnˈspekʃn ˈmɪrə(r)/
n. 检查镜；探视镜
[义] A small mirror on a handle or telescoping shaft for viewing hidden areas. 装在手柄或伸缩杆上的小镜，用于查看看不见的部位（check behind the engine 看发动机后面, telescoping inspection mirror 伸缩检查镜, articulating mirror 万向检查镜）。可数。
[例] ① An inspection mirror showed the leak dripping from a hose clamp he couldn't see directly. 检查镜照出他直接看不到的喉箍渗漏。 ② Wipe the mirror face so grime doesn't blur the view. 镜面擦干净，油污才不会糊住视野。
[搭] check behind the engine 看发动机后面, telescoping inspection mirror 伸缩检查镜, articulating mirror 万向检查镜, lighted inspection mirror 带灯检查镜
[联] mirror 镜子, inspect 检查, mechanic 技工, view 查看, hidden 隐藏的, leak 渗漏, tool 工具
[记] 可数：an inspection mirror。与 magnetic pickup 常成对放工具箱。汽修、管道检修常用

**spring compressor**
英/美 /sprɪŋ kəmˈpresə(r)/
n. 弹簧压缩器；避震弹簧压缩钳
[义] A tool that compresses coil springs so they can be removed or installed safely. 压缩螺旋弹簧以便安全拆装的工具（compress the strut spring 压缩支柱弹簧, spring compressor claws 弹簧钳爪, safety pins on the tool 工具安全销）。可数。
[例] ① Never remove a spring compressor until the spring is fully seated and tension is released. 弹簧未完全就位、张力未释放前，别卸压缩器。 ② Rent a proper spring compressor—coil springs store dangerous energy. 租正规弹簧压缩器，螺旋弹簧储能很危险。
[搭] compress the strut spring 压缩支柱弹簧, spring compressor claws 弹簧钳爪, safety pins on the tool 工具安全销, MacPherson strut tool 麦弗逊支柱工具
[联] spring 弹簧, strut 支柱, suspension 悬挂, mechanic 技工, car 汽车, safety 安全, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a spring compressor。拆装麦弗逊支柱必备。操作不当弹簧弹出可致命

**bearing puller**
英/美 /ˈbeərɪŋ pʊlə(r)/
n. 拉轴承器；轴承拉马
[义] A tool that pulls bearings, gears, or pulleys off shafts without damage. 从轴上拉下轴承、齿轮或皮带轮且尽量不伤件的工具（pull the bearing with a puller 用拉马拉轴承, two-jaw bearing puller 两爪轴承拉马, three-jaw puller 三爪拉马）。可数。
[例] ① A bearing puller removed the pressed-on pulley without hammer blows. 拉马取下过盈皮带轮，不用锤敲。 ② Choose jaws that grip evenly so the bearing comes off square. 爪要均匀受力，轴承才端正脱出。
[搭] pull the bearing with a puller 用拉马拉轴承, two-jaw bearing puller 两爪轴承拉马, three-jaw puller 三爪拉马, slide hammer puller 滑锤拉马
[联] bearing 轴承, pull 拉, shaft 轴, mechanic 技工, gear 齿轮, pulley 皮带轮, tool 工具
[记] 可数：a bearing puller。亦说 gear puller（义近）。过盈配合件别硬敲

**timing light**
英/美 /ˈtaɪmɪŋ laɪt/
n. 正时灯；点火正时灯
[义] A strobe light used with engine marks to check ignition timing on older vehicles. 与发动机标记配合、检查旧车点火正时的频闪灯（hook up the timing light 接正时灯, aim at the timing marks 对准正时标记, adjust distributor 调整分电器）。可数。
[例] ① He clipped the timing light to the number-one plug wire and checked the mark at idle. 他把正时灯夹在一号缸高压线上，怠速看标记。 ② Many modern engines have fixed timing—this tool is mainly for classics. 很多现代车正时固定，这工具多用于老爷车。
[搭] hook up the timing light 接正时灯, aim at the timing marks 对准正时标记, adjust distributor 调整分电器, inductive timing light 感应式正时灯
[联] engine 发动机, ignition 点火, timing 正时, mechanic 技工, strobe 频闪, car 汽车, tune-up 调校
[记] 可数：a timing light。电子点火、无分电器车型较少用到。读 mark 须在指定转速

**jump starter**
英/美 /dʒʌmp ˈstɑːtə(r)/
n. 应急启动电源；搭电宝
[义] A portable battery pack used to start a vehicle when its own battery is dead. 主电瓶亏电时用来启动车辆的便携电源（jump-start the car 应急启动汽车, portable jump starter 便携搭电宝, clamp to the terminals 夹到电桩）。可数。
[例] ① A jump starter in the glove box saved him when the battery died in the parking lot. 手套箱里的搭电宝在停车场电瓶趴窝时救了他一命。 ② Connect red to positive and black to a ground point—not always to the negative terminal. 红夹正极，黑夹搭铁点，不一定夹负极桩。
[搭] jump-start the car 应急启动汽车, portable jump starter 便携搭电宝, clamp to the terminals 夹到电桩, lithium jump starter 锂电搭电宝
[联] battery 电瓶, car 汽车, start 启动, charger 充电器, emergency 应急, cable 电缆, roadside 路边
[记] 可数：a jump starter。亦说 jump pack / booster pack。比叫道路救援快。定期充电保持电量

**brake bleeder**
英/美 /breɪk ˈbliːdə(r)/
n. 刹车放气嘴；制动放气螺钉
[义] A screw valve on a brake caliper or wheel cylinder used to release air from the brake lines. 制动卡钳或轮缸上的放气螺钉，用于排出制动管路空气（open the bleeder screw 打开放气螺钉, bleed the brakes 放气排刹, one-man brake bleeder kit 单人放气套件）。可数。
[例] ① Open the bleeder screw a quarter turn while your helper presses the pedal. 助手踩刹车时，把放气螺钉拧开四分之一圈。 ② Tighten the bleeder before the pedal is released to avoid sucking air back in. 松踏板前拧紧放气螺钉，免得吸回空气。
[搭] open the bleeder screw 打开放气螺钉, bleed the brakes 放气排刹, one-man brake bleeder kit 单人放气套件, bleeder wrench 放气螺钉扳手
[联] brake 刹车, bleed 放气, caliper 卡钳, fluid 制动液, mechanic 技工, pedal 踏板, safety 安全
[记] 可数：a brake bleeder / bleeder screw。换制动液、维修后须排气。顺序常由远及近

**extension bar**
英/美 /ɪkˈstenʃn bɑː(r)/
n. 接杆；套筒延长杆
[义] A bar that extends a socket wrench to reach recessed or deep fasteners. 接在套筒扳手上、够到深处或内陷紧固件的延长杆（socket on an extension bar 套筒接延长杆, wobble extension 万向接杆, long extension bar 长接杆）。可数。
[例] ① An extension bar let him reach the spark plug buried under the intake plenum. 接杆够到了进气歧管下的火花塞。 ② Use the shortest extension that works—extra length can flex and round bolts. 能用多短就多短，太长会弯、易拧圆螺栓。
[搭] socket on an extension bar 套筒接延长杆, wobble extension 万向接杆, long extension bar 长接杆, impact-rated extension 冲击级接杆
[联] socket 套筒, wrench 扳手, ratchet 棘轮, mechanic 技工, reach 够到, tool 工具, fastener 紧固件
[记] 可数：an extension bar。1/4、3/8、1/2 英寸方榫各一套。与 universal joint 配用够死角

**router table**
英/美 /ˈruːtə teɪbl/
n. 修边机台；router 台
[义] A table with a mounted router upside down for shaping edges and cutting profiles. 倒置安装修边机的台面，用于修边与铣型（mount the router in the table 把修边机装入修边机台, feed stock against the fence 靠导板送料, roundover bit 圆角铣刀）。可数。
[例] ① A router table made consistent roundovers on dozens of shelf edges. 修边机台给几十块搁板棱边做了统一圆角。 ② Keep fingers on the push block, never near the exposed bit above the table. 手指放在推块上，别靠近台面孔里露出的铣刀。
[搭] mount the router in the table 把修边机装入修边机台, feed stock against the fence 靠导板送料, roundover bit 圆角铣刀, router table fence 修边机台导板
[联] router 修边机, wood 木头, edge 边缘, workshop 车间, bit 铣刀, carpenter 木匠, fence 导板
[记] 可数：a router table。与 handheld router 互补：台定、手灵活。network router 为另义同形异义

**featherboard**
英/美 /ˈfeðəbɔːd/
n. 压板；羽毛板
[义] A slotted board that presses work against a fence or table to prevent kickback. 带槽压板，把工件压向导板或台面，减少回弹（clamp a featherboard to the fence 把压板夹到导板上, table saw featherboard 台锯压板, flexible fingers 柔性指条）。可数。
[例] ① A featherboard kept the narrow strip tight to the fence during the rip cut. 纵切时压板把窄条紧压在导尺上。 ② Adjust finger pressure so the board feeds smoothly without binding. 指条压力要适中，送料顺又不卡。
[搭] clamp a featherboard to the fence 把压板夹到导板上, table saw featherboard 台锯压板, flexible fingers 柔性指条, magnetic featherboard 磁性压板
[联] table saw 台锯, fence 导板, safety 安全, kickback 回弹, wood 木头, push stick 推棒, workshop 车间
[记] 可数：a featherboard。与 push stick 同为台锯安全件。指条像羽毛，故得名

**orbital sander**
英/美 /ˈɔːbɪtl ˈsændə(r)/
n. 轨道砂光机；方砂机
[义] A power sander whose pad moves in small orbits for smooth finishing on flat surfaces. 砂垫做小轨道运动的电动砂光机，用于平面精细打磨（finish sand with an orbital sander 用轨道砂光机精磨, random orbital sander 随机轨道砂光机, hook-and-loop pad 魔术贴砂垫）。可数。
[例] ① An orbital sander removed swirl marks left by the belt sander. 轨道砂光机去掉带砂机留下的旋纹。 ② Keep the pad flat and moving—lingering creates dips. 砂垫要平、要移动，停住会磨出坑。
[搭] finish sand with an orbital sander 用轨道砂光机精磨, random orbital sander 随机轨道砂光机, hook-and-loop pad 魔术贴砂垫, variable-speed orbital sander 调速轨道砂光机
[联] sander 砂光机, sandpaper 砂纸, belt sander 带式砂光机, smooth 磨平, wood 木头, finish 涂装, power tool 电动工具
[记] 可数：an orbital sander。random orbital 额外旋转，更少旋纹。精磨在 belt sander 之后

**breaker bar**
英/美 /ˈbreɪkə bɑː(r)/
n. 加力杆；破拆杆
[义] A long non-ratcheting bar that gives extra leverage to break loose tight fasteners. 无棘轮的长杆，用更大杠杆拧松锈死紧固件（breaker bar on lug nuts 加力杆拧轮毂螺母, long breaker bar 长加力杆, cheater bar 同义口语）。可数。
[例] ① A breaker bar cracked the seized bolt free before he switched to a ratchet. 锈死螺栓先用加力杆拧松，再换棘轮。 ② Stand clear when the bolt breaks loose—the bar can swing hard. 螺栓突然松时人站开，杆会猛甩。
[搭] breaker bar on lug nuts 加力杆拧轮毂螺母, long breaker bar 长加力杆, 1/2-inch drive breaker bar 半英寸方榫加力杆, slide pipe cheater 套管加力
[联] wrench 扳手, socket 套筒, torque 扭矩, leverage 杠杆, mechanic 技工, rust 锈, lug nut 轮毂螺母
[记] 可数：a breaker bar。无棘轮，只用于初始松扣。impact wrench 之前常先用它
